### [1] [Modular Lie Algebraic PDE Control of Multibody Flexible Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06709)
*Sadeq Yaqubi,Jouni Mattila*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对柔性多体机器人系统，提出了基于偏微分方程（PDE）的子系统控制框架，利用旋量李代数模型描述串行机械臂在三维空间中的运动与变形，通过应变-变形PDE消除分布式弹性加速度，结合变形补偿逆运动学与自适应李雅普诺夫控制，实现全局指数收敛的轨迹跟踪与有界弹性变形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决柔性多体机器人系统在三维空间中精确控制的问题，特别是考虑连杆弹性变形及其与运动耦合的挑战，需要一种能够处理无限维弹性动力学且具有模块化、可扩展特性的控制框架。

Method: 基于旋量李代数（se(3)）的模型，将运动、变形和力表示为体固定 twists 和 wrenches；通过应变-变形PDE替代动力学中的分布式弹性加速度；采用变形补偿逆运动学从任务空间端点生成子系统轨迹；设计名义控制器与自适应控制器，利用复合李雅普诺夫函数 V=∑ν_i 和 V^a=∑ν_i^a，利用牛顿第三定律和 se(3)×se*(3) 上的功率对偶性实现连杆间相互作用功率项的 telescoping 消零。

Result: 实现了跟踪误差的全局指数收敛，参数误差的指数收敛，以及通过欧拉-伯努利能量论证保证了有界弹性变形；数值仿真验证了方案的物理一致性。

Conclusion: 旋量李代数结构使得相互作用抵消精确成立，稳定性证明具有模块化和可扩展性，适用于任意长度铰接链的柔性机器人系统。

Abstract: This paper addresses PDE-based control for flexible multibody robotic systems, presenting a subsystem-based framework for serial manipulators with arbitrary links in 3D space. The approach uses a screw-theoretic Lie-algebraic model where motion, deformation, and forces are expressed as body-fixed twists and wrenches in se(3). By substituting a strain-based deformation PDE into the dynamics, distributed elastic acceleration is eliminated, yielding a model governed by twist acceleration and the deformation field. Subsystem twist trajectories are generated from task-space endpoints via deflection-compensating inverse kinematics, providing real-time correction for tip deformation. A nominal controller for each link ensures exponential decay of twist errors via a Lyapunov function nu_i. An adaptive modification replaces physical parameters with online estimates, establishing exponential convergence of both twist and parameter errors. Summing over all links, composite Lyapunov functions V = sum(nu_i) and V^a = sum(nu_i^a) yield time derivatives where inter-link interaction power terms telescope to zero. This cancellation is ensured by Newton's third law and the frame invariance of the power pairing on se(3) x se*(3), establishing global exponential convergence of tracking errors. Bounded elastic deformation is guaranteed by an Euler-Bernoulli energy argument. The screw-theoretic structure renders interaction cancellation exact, making the stability certificate modular and scalable to chains of arbitrary length. Numerical simulations demonstrate the scheme's physical consistency.

</details>


### [2] [An Aerial Manipulator for Perception-Driven Flower Targeting Toward Contactless Pollination in Vertical Farming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06759)
*Chenzhe Jin,Zhuohang Wu,Yifan Cai,Xiangqi Li,Jan Ming Kevin Tan,Narsimlu Kemsaram,Valerio Modugno*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于空中操作平台的无人机系统，集成RGBD感知、MPPI控制和2自由度轻量机械臂，用于垂直农业环境中花朵的精准检测、定位和接近，实验验证了厘米级末端定位精度，为未来非接触式授粉系统奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然传粉者减少对室内垂直农业作物生产构成挑战，亟需开发能够精准定位花朵且物理干扰最小的机器人系统。

Method: 采用基于RGBD的感知模块进行花朵检测与定位，结合模型预测路径积分（MPPI）控制器实现无人机稳定飞行，搭载2自由度轻量机械臂执行末端精准定位，在MuJoCo仿真和真实无人机实验室环境中进行评估。

Result: 仿真中控制器实现一致轨迹收敛和精准目标对齐；真实实验中集成框架实现了稳定花朵定位和末端对齐，达到厘米级定位精度。

Conclusion: 所提出的空中操作平台可作为未来非接触式授粉系统的稳健载体和定位框架，为集成声学花粉操控等先进末端执行器提供了实用基础。

Abstract: The decline of natural pollinators has created a major challenge for crop production in controlled indoor agriculture, particularly in vertical farming environments where natural insect pollination is absent. This motivates the development of robotic systems capable of performing precise flower targeting tasks while minimizing physical interference with delicate floral structures. This paper presents an aerial manipulator platform for perception driven flower detection, localization, and approach in vertical farming environments. The proposed system integrates onboard RGBD based perception, model predictive path integral (MPPI) based unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) control on a PX4 platform, and a lightweight 2DoF manipulator for precise end effector positioning. The platform is evaluated in both MuJoCo simulation and UAV lab experiments using a flower targeting testbed. The experimental results demonstrate stable UAV flight, reliable flower localization, and centimeter level end effector positioning accuracy. In simulation, the proposed controller achieves consistent trajectory convergence and accurate target alignment. In the real world UAV lab environment, the integrated perception control manipulation framework enables stable flower targeted positioning and end effector alignment under constrained aerial operation. These results validate the proposed aerial manipulator as a robust robotic carrier and positioning framework for future contactless pollination systems. While the current study focuses on perception guided targeting and positioning, the developed platform provides a practical foundation for integrating advanced contactless end effectors, including acoustic based pollen manipulation modules, in future work.

</details>


### [3] [AirBender: Adaptive Transportation of Bendable Objects Using Dual UAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07003)
*Jiawei Xu,Longsen Gao,Rafael Fierro,David Saldaña*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应控制器，使两架无人机无需依赖弹性模型即可协同运输可弯曲物体，并通过李雅普诺夫分析证明其渐近稳定性，硬件实验验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中机器人与可弯曲物体交互时，由于驱动能力有限且需持续保持飞行，控制难度大，易导致性能下降甚至坠毁。现有方法依赖精确弹性模型，而实际中物体变形特性未知且多变，亟需一种不依赖显式模型的自适应控制方案。

Method: 提出自适应控制器，在线实时适应物体未知的可变形特性，无需预先知晓弹性模型；采用李雅普诺夫分析证明系统的渐近稳定性；通过多旋翼无人机硬件实验验证。

Result: 在多种场景下的硬件实验中，两架无人机成功协同跟踪轨迹并运输可弯曲物体，控制器表现出良好的稳定性和轨迹跟踪性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的自适应控制器能够有效处理空中机器人协同运输可弯曲物体的控制问题，无需弹性模型先验知识，具有渐近稳定性保证和实际可行性。

Abstract: The interaction of robots with bendable objects in midair presents significant challenges in control, often resulting in performance degradation and potential crashes, especially for aerial robots due to their limited actuation capabilities and constant need to remain airborne. This paper presents an adaptive controller that enables two aerial vehicles to collaboratively follow a trajectory while transporting a bendable object without relying on explicit elasticity models. Our method allows on-the-fly adaptation to the object's unknown deformable properties, ensuring stability and performance in trajectory-tracking tasks. We use Lyapunov analysis to demonstrate that our adaptive controller is asymptotically stable. Our method is evaluated through hardware experiments in various scenarios, demonstrating the capabilities of using multirotor aerial vehicles to handle bendable objects.

</details>


### [4] [Bi3: A Biplatform, Bicultural, Biperson Dataset for Social Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06863)
*Andrew Stratton,Phani Teja Singamaneni,Pranav Goyal,Rachid Alami,Christoforos Mavrogiannis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了Bi3数据集，这是一个在受限实验室空间中机器人导航于人群中的数据集，包含10.5小时的多模态数据（运动轨迹、RGB视频、用户主观评价），涉及五种导航算法、两种机器人平台、74名来自美法两地的参与者，为密集空间中的社交机器人导航研究提供了独特基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有社交机器人导航数据集缺乏在受限空间中人与机器人紧密交互的场景，Bi3通过创新的实验设计（两人一机器人的近距离导航遭遇）填补了这一空白，旨在理解人与机器人如何在受限环境中有效融合其活动。

Method: 数据集通过精心设计的实验采集，包含五种不同的导航算法、两种机器人平台，并在美国和法国的两个地点招募了74名参与者。数据流包括人体和机器人的地面真实运动轨迹、RGB视频以及用户对机器人性能的主观印象。

Result: 通过交互密度和人体速度等指标分析，Bi3数据集在多样性和建模复杂度方面构成了一个独特的基准。

Conclusion: Bi3数据集有助于理解人机在受限环境中的高效协同活动，可作为人类运动预测模型和密集空间导航机器人控制策略的训练资源。

Abstract: We contribute Bi3, a dataset of social robot navigation among groups of people in a constrained lab space. Compared to prior data collection efforts for social robot navigation, our dataset is unique in that it features: an original experiment design giving rise to close navigation encounters between two humans and a robot; five different navigation algorithms; two different robot platforms; a diverse participant pool of 74 people recruited from two sites in the USA and France; multimodal data streams including 10.5 hours of human and robot ground-truth motion tracks, RGB video, and user impressions over robot performance. Our analysis of the collected dataset through metrics like interaction density and human velocity suggests that Bi3 represents a benchmark of unique diversity and modeling complexity. Bi3 contributes towards understanding how humans and robots can productively mesh their activities in constrained environments, and can be a resource for training models of human motion prediction and robot control policies for navigation in densely crowded spaces.

</details>


### [5] [Traffic Scenario Orchestration from Language via Constraint Satisfaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06966)
*Frieda Rong,Chris Zhang,Kelvin Wong,Raquel Urtasun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大模型推理和约束求解的场景编排方法，将自然语言描述转化为约束条件，通过求解器生成满足精确测试意图的自动驾驶闭环测试场景，在成功率上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆需要在仿真环境中进行大量测试，但手动编程测试场景虽精确可控却难以规模化，而统计模型虽可利用数据和算力但在分布外场景中难以精确控制。因此需要一种既能保持精确可控性又能规模化生成场景的方法。

Method: 将场景编排视为约束求解问题，利用基础模型（大模型）的推理能力将自然语言描述转化为一组约束作为场景表示，然后使用现成的求解器求解参与者行为，在闭环中满足精确的测试意图。

Result: 在精心设计的多样化场景描述基准测试中，该方法在场景编排成功率上大幅优于基线方法，特别是在需要自我反应（ego-reactive）规格的场景中，闭环方法表现尤为关键。

Conclusion: 语言输入-仿真输出的场景编排器能够有效结合自然语言的灵活性和约束求解的精确性，为自动驾驶闭环测试中的分布外场景生成提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles (AVs) require extensive testing in simulation, but test case generation for driving scenarios is laborious. The desired scenarios are often out-of-distribution and have precise requirements on interactions with the AV policy under test. Manually programming scenarios allows for precise controllability but is difficult to scale. On the other hand, statistical models can leverage compute and data, but struggle with precise controllability when out-of-distribution. We cast scenario orchestration as a constraint-solving problem and present a language-in, simulation-out scenario orchestrator for closed-loop testing AVs. Our approach leverages foundation model reasoning to translate general, natural language descriptions into a set of constraints as a scenario representation. This then allows us to leverage off the shelf solvers to solve for actor behaviors which meet precise testing intentions in closed-loop. Under a benchmark of carefully crafted and diverse scenario descriptions, our approach greatly outperforms our baselines in orchestration success rate. We further show that our closed-loop approach is especially important for scenarios which require ego-reactive specifications.

</details>


### [6] [Variable Aerodynamic Damping via Co-Contraction: A Dynamic Isomorphism with Variable Stiffness Actuators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07292)
*Antonio Franchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文证明了冗余双旋翼执行器中的气动共收缩可以通过调节被动、配平定义的空气-机械阻尼，同时保持指令净力恒定。定义增量阻尼系数为配平处净推力对空气相对速度的局部灵敏度，并证明在温和气动硬化条件下该系数沿恒力纤维单调递增。通过第一性原理叶素理论推导验证了所需阻尼与硬化特性，得到最小推力模型。将该机制形式化为可变气动阻尼执行器（VADA），并证明其在动力学上与拮抗变刚度执行器（VSA）中的刚度调节同构。该纤维密度原理还可增强冗余多旋翼的主动气动敏捷性。最后，阻抗形式表示阐明了共模与差模驱动在VADA系统被动阻抗和平衡速度控制中的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索冗余双旋翼执行器中通过气动共收缩独立调节阻尼而不改变净力的能力，揭示其与生物肌肉共收缩及变刚度执行器的深层联系。

Method: 定义增量阻尼系数，在气动硬化条件下证明单调性；通过叶素理论推导最小推力模型；建立VADA与VSA的动力学同构关系；引入纤维密度原理分析主动敏捷性；使用阻抗形式表征共模/差模控制。

Result: 气动共收缩可实现阻尼的单调调节而保持净力恒定；VADA与拮抗VSA在动力学上同构；纤维密度原理可增强冗余多旋翼的主动气动敏捷性。

Conclusion: VADA提供了一种新的被动阻抗调控机制，共模/差模驱动可分别控制被动阻抗和平衡速度，为冗余旋翼系统的阻抗控制奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: We prove that aerodynamic co-contraction in a redundant dual-rotor actuator can tune a passive, trim-defined aero-mechanical damping while keeping the commanded net force constant. In particular, we define an incremental damping coefficient as the local sensitivity of net thrust to air-relative velocity at a trim and prove that it increases monotonically along constant-force fibers under a mild aerodynamic hardening condition. We then validate the required damping and hardening properties from a first-principles Blade Element Theory derivation, which yields a minimal thrust model affine in inflow and explicitly reveals the speed--inflow coupling driving the effect. The resulting mechanism is formalized as a Variable Aerodynamic Damping Actuator (VADA) and shown to be dynamically isomorphic to stiffness modulation in antagonistic variable-stiffness actuation (VSA), similar to the co-contraction of tendons by muscle co-activation. The same fiber-density principle also enhances the active aerodynamic promptness measure of redundant multirotors. Finally, an impedance-form representation clarifies the roles of common-mode and differential-mode actuation in the control of passive impedance and the equilibrium velocity of the VADA system.

</details>


### [7] [MORPH-U: Multi-Objective Resilient Motion Planning for V2X-Enabled Autonomous Driving in High-Uncertainty Environments via Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07370)
*Shih-Yu Lai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MORPH-U，一种基于CARBA的闭环运动规划与控制框架，融合V2X通信与多传感器构建局部动态地图(LDM)，通过混合A*重规划应对不确定性，并引入拜占庭容错门控机制抵御虚假V2X攻击，实验验证了其在安全性、舒适性与响应性之间的可控权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: V2X通信存在延迟、丢包甚至伪造消息的不确定性，同时地图信息可能动态变化，车辆需在实时约束下进行鲁棒的运动规划与控制。

Method: 融合LiDAR/雷达/摄像头与V2X(CAM/DENM)构建LDM；触发混合A*重规划；采用多目标优化框架（跟踪误差、安全裕度TTC、响应性、平滑性）并通过Pareto前沿分析选择工作点；引入拜占庭容错门控（quorum规则+车载传感器否决权）过滤虚假V2X触发。

Result: V2X增强的LDM提升下游安全性；Pareto调优实现可控的精度-舒适权衡；门控机制在饱和伪造DENM注入攻击(p_attack=1.0)下成功阻止不安全重规划。

Conclusion: MORPH-U有效融合V2X与局部感知实现鲁棒运动规划，其拜占庭门控机制能在极端攻击下保障安全，多目标优化框架为规划-控制提供了可调节的权衡空间。

Abstract: V2X can warn an autonomous vehicle about hazards beyond line-of-sight, but it also brings uncertainty: messages may be delayed, dropped, or even forged. Meanwhile, map knowledge may change during a trip, forcing the vehicle to replan under tight real-time budgets. This paper studies how to make motion planning and low-level control robust to such uncertain, event-driven updates. We present MORPH-U, a CARLA-based closed-loop stack that fuses LiDAR/radar/camera with V2X (CAM/DENM) into a Local Dynamic Map (LDM) and triggers Hybrid-A* replanning when validated hazards or map changes affect the planned route. We expose the planning/control trade-offs via a multi-objective formulation over tracking error, safety margin (minimum TTC), responsiveness, and smoothness, and select operating points using Pareto-frontier analysis. To avoid unsafe replanning from faulty V2X triggers, MORPH-U adds a lightweight Byzantine-inspired acceptance gate that combines a quorum rule with an on-board sensor veto. Experiments in dynamic CARLA scenarios show that V2X-augmented LDM improves downstream safety, Pareto tuning provides controllable accuracy-comfort trade-offs, and the gate prevents replanning under saturated false-DENM injection ($p_{\text{attack}}=1.0$).

</details>


### [8] [Intention assimilation control for accurate tracking with variable impedance in teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07037)
*Atsushi Takagi,Yanan Li,Hiroaki Gomi,Etienne Burdet*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种意图同化控制（IAC）策略，通过估计领导者的目标位置而非当前位置，解决了遥操作中跟踪精度与安全性之间的权衡问题，使从属机器人能够在低刚度下实现高跟踪精度，并在两台7自由度机械臂上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的弹簧式力控制策略中，从属机器人的跟踪精度与刚度之间存在矛盾：低刚度导致跟踪精度差，高刚度则对环境和人员构成危险。为解决这一跟踪精度与安全性的权衡问题，需要一种新的控制策略。

Method: 提出意图同化控制（IAC）策略，不直接传输领导者的当前位置，而是估计并传输领导者的目标位置给从属机器人。从属机器人的阻抗可实时调整，以反映用户期望的阻抗或自动满足任务需求。实验在两台7自由度机械臂上进行，对比了IAC与遥阻抗控制（TIC）在自由跟踪、气球交互、插销插入和桌面抛光（带力反馈）四种场景下的表现。

Result: IAC相比TIC在跟踪精度、任务完成率和完成时间上均有显著提升。IAC能够在低刚度下实现高跟踪精度，同时允许用户根据意图自由调节阻抗。

Conclusion: IAC策略使机器人能够在变阻抗条件下精确复现用户运动，为用户提供了对从属机器人位置和阻抗前所未有的控制水平，适用于单边和双边遥操作。

Abstract: Robot systems for teleoperation commonly use a spring-like force pulling the follower robot towards the leader's position to track their movements. With this control strategy, the tracking accuracy deteriorates when the follower' stiffness is low, but high stiffness poses a danger to objects or people in the follower robot's environment. To address this trade-off between tracking accuracy and safety, we propose an alternative intention assimilation control (IAC) strategy where the robot's tracking accuracy can be ensured without high stiffness. Different from traditional approaches, which transmit the leader's current position to the follower, this new controller estimates the leader's target position and transmits it to the follower. With this strategy, the follower impedance can be changed on-the-fly to continuously reflect the user's desired impedance or modulated automatically to fulfill the task requirements. Our controller was validated on two 7 degree-of-freedom manipulators, yielding high tracking accuracy with varying impedance. Four experiments were conducted to compare {teleoperation} with IAC to tele-impedance control (TIC) during free tracking, interaction with a balloon, during peg insertion, and table polishing with force feedback. The results show that IAC increases tracking accuracy, improves task completion rate and reduces completion time. IAC enables the robot to accurately replicate the user's movement while giving them freedom to modulate the impedance according to their intention, providing an unprecedented level of control of the follower's position and its impedance during unilateral and bilateral teleoperation.

</details>


### [9] [Dr-BA: Separable Optimization for Direct Radar Bundle Adjustment & Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07041)
*Daniil Lisus,Cedric Le Gentil,Timothy D. Barfoot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Dr-BA，首个直接在2D旋转雷达强度图像上运行的雷达光束法平差框架，通过可分离优化实现密集地图构建与位姿联合估计，并在超过200公里道路数据上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于旋转雷达的状态估计方法通常从距离-方位-强度测量中提取稀疏点云并进行点云配准，未能充分利用雷达的全部返回信息。雷达相比相机和激光雷达具有全天候鲁棒性，但缺乏直接操作于雷达强度图像的BA框架。

Method: 将问题建模为可分离优化，解耦位姿估计与地图构建；直接在2D旋转雷达强度图像上操作，利用多扫描的全部雷达返回信息进行密集地图与传感器位姿的联合估计；自然地扩展到仅雷达定位（DRL）。

Result: 在五条不同路线、超过200公里的道路数据上，Dr-BA达到了雷达BA和跨会话定位的SOTA性能。

Conclusion: Dr-BA验证了直接利用雷达强度图像进行BA的可行性与优越性，为全天候自主系统的状态估计提供了新的高效解决方案，且代码已开源。

Abstract: This paper introduces Dr-BA, a first-of-its-kind radar bundle adjustment (BA) framework that operates directly on 2D spinning radar intensity images. Unlike camera or lidar sensors, radar is largely unaffected by precipitation, making it a critical modality for autonomous systems that require all-weather robustness. Existing state estimation approaches using spinning radar typically extract sparse point clouds from range-azimuth-intensity measurements and apply point cloud alignment techniques to estimate vehicle motion, scene structure, or to localize within an existing map. In contrast, Dr-BA uses the full radar returns from multiple scans to jointly estimate dense maps and sensor poses. By formulating the problem as a separable optimization, we derive an efficient and general solution that decouples pose estimation from mapping. In addition to solving the BA problem, this formulation naturally extends to direct radar-only localization (DRL) within a previously built map. Dr-BA achieves state-of-the-art radar-based BA and cross-session localization performance, demonstrated on more than 200 km of on-road data across five distinct routes. Our implementation is publicly available at https://github.com/utiasASRL/dr_ba.

</details>


### [10] [PISTO: Proximal Inference for Stochastic Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07215)
*Hongzhe Yu,Zinuo Chang,Yongxin Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PISTO算法，通过将KL散度正则化引入STOMP的变分推断框架，实现了对非可微代价函数的稳定轨迹优化，在机器人运动规划任务中显著优于CHOMP、STOMP、CEM和MPPI等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: STOMP虽然能处理非可微代价函数，但其更新机制缺乏稳定性。作者发现STOMP隐式最小化玻尔兹曼轨迹分布的KL散度，揭示其变分推断结构，因此希望通过引入KL正则化来稳定优化过程。

Method: 在STOMP的变分推断框架基础上，引入连续高斯提议分布之间的KL正则化作为近端项，形成信任区域约束；通过重要性加权蒙特卡洛采样估计闭式均值更新，得到无导数优化算法PISTO。

Result: 在机械臂运动规划中，PISTO成功率89%（CHOMP 63%，STOMP 68%），路径更短更平滑，速度是其他随机方法的2倍；在接触丰富的MuJoCo locomotion和manipulation任务中，奖励持续优于CEM和MPPI。

Conclusion: PISTO通过变分推断视角下的近端正则化，在保持STOMP处理非可微代价能力的同时显著提升了优化稳定性与性能，是一种简单、无导数且高效的轨迹优化方法。

Abstract: Stochastic trajectory optimization methods like STOMP enable planning with non-differentiable costs, offering substantial flexibility over gradient-based approaches. We show that STOMP implicitly minimizes the KL divergence from a Boltzmann trajectory distribution, revealing an elegant Variational Inference (VI) structure underlying its updates. Building on this insight, we propose the \textit{Proximal Inference for Stochastic Trajectory Optimization} (PISTO) algorithm that stabilizes the updates by augmenting the objective with a KL regularization between successive Gaussian proposals. This proximal formulation admits a trust-region interpretation and yields closed-form mean updates computable as expectations under a surrogate distribution. We estimate these expectations via importance-weighted Monte Carlo sampling, producing a simple, derivative-free algorithm that inherits STOMP's ability to handle non-differentiable and discontinuous costs without modification. On robot arm motion planning benchmarks, PISTO achieves an 89\% success rate -- outperforming CHOMP (63\%) and STOMP (68\%) -- while producing shorter, smoother paths at twice the speed of competing stochastic methods. We further validate PISTO on contact-rich MuJoCo locomotion and manipulation tasks, where it consistently outperforms both CEM and MPPI baselines in reward.

</details>


### [11] [Palm-sized Omnidirectional Vision-Based UAV Exploration with Sparse Topological Map Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07275)
*Zirui Wang,Xinjia Luo,Haotian Sun,Jun Ma,Jian Guo,Boyu Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轻量化自主探索系统，利用全方位视觉和稀疏拓扑地图引导，在无需密集占用地图或点云的情况下，通过多鱼眼相机实现深度估计，以拓扑节点表示前沿区域，并在稀疏图上进行全局路径规划，在仿真和仅400克重的微型无人机上验证了其高效性和极低计算消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统探索方法依赖密集占用地图或高分辨率点云，内存和计算开销大，且微型无人机受SWaP约束难以搭载LiDAR等传感器获取精确几何测量。

Method: 采用多鱼眼相机设置实现全方位视场并进行深度估计；将前沿区域表示为拓扑节点而非显式边界，避免维护占用网格或全局点云；利用稀疏拓扑地图（关键节点及其描述符）抽象环境，直接在稀疏图上进行全局路径规划。

Result: 在仿真环境和真实世界中11厘米轴距、400克重的掌上视觉无人机上验证，该方法能够以极低计算消耗实现高效探索。

Conclusion: 提出的轻量化探索系统在保持探索效率的同时显著降低了内存和计算需求，适用于资源受限的微型无人机平台。

Abstract: Classic exploration methods often rely on dense occupancy maps or high-resolution point clouds for frontier detection and path planning, resulting in substantial memory consumption and computational overhead. Moreover, micro UAVs under size, weight, and power (SWaP) constraints are not practical to be equipped with sensors like LiDAR to obtain accurate environmental geometric measurements. This paper presents a lightweight autonomous exploration system that leverages omnidirectional vision and sparse topological map guidance. Specifically, we utilize a multi-fisheye camera setup to achieve omnidirectional Field of View (FoV) and perform depth estimation. To address the limited depth estimation accuracy, frontiers are represented as potential unexplored regions characterized by topological nodes instead of explicit boundaries, enabling efficient identification of frontier regions without maintaining occupancy grids or global point clouds. Unlike classic dense representations, our approach abstracts the environment using a sparse topological map composed of key nodes and their descriptors, reducing memory consumption and computational demands. Global path planning is performed directly on the sparse graph. The proposed method is validated in both simulation and on a palm-sized vision-based UAV with an 11 cm wheelbase and a 400 g weight in real-world experiments, demonstrating that our method can achieve efficient exploration with extremely low computational consumption.

</details>


### [12] [BioProVLA-Agent: An Affordable, Protocol-Driven, Vision-Enhanced VLA-Enabled Embodied Multi-Agent System with Closed-Loop-Capable Reasoning for Biological Laboratory Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07306)
*Zhaohui Du,Zhe Wang,Hongmei Fei,Xiwen Cao,Ting Xiao,Qi Wang,Huanbo Jin,Jiaming Gu,Quan Lu,Zhe Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了BioProVLA-Agent，一个基于视觉-语言-动作(VLA)模型、以协议为驱动的低成本多智能体生物实验自动化系统。系统包含协议解析、视觉状态验证和具身执行三大模块，并提出了面向湿实验室视觉扰动的在线增强策略AugSmolVLA。在多层级基准测试中，系统在15个原子任务、6个复合工作流和3个双手任务上表现出色，尤其在高曝光和视觉退化场景下提升了执行稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物实验室自动化可减少重复人工、提升可重复性，但现有机器人系统依赖昂贵硬件、固定工作流、专用仪器或机器人专用接口；湿实验室环境中的协议非结构化、器皿透明/反光、多步操作需状态感知执行，导致可靠具身执行仍具挑战。

Method: 构建三智能体系统：(1) Tailored LLM Protocol Agent将自然语言协议解析为可验证子任务；(2) VLM-RAG Verification Agent基于观测、机器人状态、检索知识与成功/失败示例评估就绪状态与完成状态；(3) VLA Embodied Agent通过轻量策略执行已验证子任务。同时提出AugSmolVLA在线增强策略，针对透明器皿、反射、光照变化和过曝光等湿实验室视觉扰动进行鲁棒性提升。

Result: 在15个原子任务、6个复合工作流和3个双手任务（包括试管装载、分选、废物处理、拧盖、液体倾倒）的层级基准上，AugSmolVLA在正常和高曝光设置下均优于ACT、X-VLA和原始SmolVLA，尤其在精确放置、透明物体操作、复合工作流和视觉退化场景中提升显著。

Conclusion: 研究表明，以协议为中心、具备验证能力的VLA具身AI系统为可负担的生物实验自动化提供了可行路径，降低了硬件成本门槛并提升了湿实验室环境下的执行鲁棒性。

Abstract: Biological laboratory automation can reduce repetitive manual work and improve reproducibility, but reliable embodied execution in wet-lab environments remains challenging. Protocols are often unstructured, labware is frequently transparent or reflective, and multi-step procedures require state-aware execution beyond one-shot instruction following. Existing robotic systems often rely on costly hardware, fixed workflows, dedicated instruments, or robotics-oriented interfaces. Here, we introduce BioProVLA-Agent, an affordable, protocol-driven, vision-enhanced embodied multi-agent system enabled by Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models for biological manipulation. The system uses protocols as the task interface and integrates protocol parsing, visual state verification, and embodied execution in a closed-loop workflow. A Tailored LLM Protocol Agent converts protocols into verifiable subtasks; a VLM-RAG Verification Agent assesses readiness and completion using observations, robot states, retrieved knowledge, and success/failure examples; and a VLA Embodied Agent executes verified subtasks through a lightweight policy. To improve robustness under wet-lab visual perturbations, we develop AugSmolVLA, an online augmentation strategy targeting transparent labware, reflections, illumination shifts, and overexposure. We evaluate the system on a hierarchical benchmark covering 15 atomic tasks, 6 composite workflows, and 3 bimanual tasks, including tube loading, sorting, waste disposal, cap twisting, and liquid pouring. Across normal and high-exposure settings, AugSmolVLA improves execution stability over ACT, X-VLA, and the original SmolVLA, especially for precise placement, transparent-object manipulation, composite workflows, and visually degraded scenes. These results suggest a practical route toward accessible, protocol-centered, and verification-capable embodied AI for biological manipulation.

</details>


### [13] [AT-VLA: Adaptive Tactile Injection for Enhanced Feedback Reaction in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07308)
*Xiaoqi Li,Muhe Cai,Jiadong Xu,Juan Zhu,Hongwei Fan,Yan Shen,Guangrui Ren,Hao Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AT-VLA通过自适应触觉注入机制和触觉反应双流机制，解决了VLA模型在接触丰富操作任务中触觉引入干扰预训练能力及推理速度慢的问题，实现了实时闭环响应（0.04秒内）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在接触丰富操作场景中存在两大挑战：(1) 微调阶段引入触觉信号会干扰预训练的表征能力；(2) VLA模型推理速度慢，无法实现触觉反馈的实时响应和动作调整。

Method: 提出两种核心机制：(1) 自适应触觉注入机制——动态判断何时何地注入触觉信号，仅在触觉对动作生成有显著贡献时引入，最小化对预训练表征的干扰；(2) 触觉反应双流机制——将感知处理解耦为慢速视觉-语言流（低频感知推理）和快速触觉控制流（高频物理交互理解），实现0.04秒内的实时闭环响应。

Result: 在真实世界的接触丰富操作任务中验证了AT-VLA的有效性。

Conclusion: AT-VLA通过自适应触觉注入和双流解耦设计，有效解决了触觉引入对预训练能力的干扰问题，同时实现了高速触觉响应，提升了VLA在精细物理交互任务中的表现。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have significantly advanced the capabilities of robotic agents in executing diverse tasks; however, they still face challenges in contact-rich manipulation scenarios that require precise physical interactions. To address this limitation, recent studies have attempted to incorporate tactile signals during downstream tasks, enabling pretrained VLAs to interpret tactile feedback. Nevertheless, introducing new modalities during finetuning, which are rarely present in the pretrain stage, may disrupt the pretrained capabilities of VLAs. In addition, the inherently slow inference speed of VLAs hampers real-time responsiveness and limits the effective utilization of tactile feedback for action adjustment. To overcome these challenges, we propose Adaptive Tactile Vision-Language-Action (AT-VLA), which introduces a novel Adaptive Tactile Injection mechanism. This mechanism dynamically determines the appropriate timing and locations for tactile injection, incorporating only when it significantly contributes to action generation, thereby minimizing interference with pretrained representations. Furthermore, to enable rapid and accurate tactile responses, we propose a Tactile Reaction Dual-Stream mechanism, which decouples sensory processing into a slow visual-language stream for low-frequency perceptual reasoning and a fast tactile control stream for high-frequency physical interaction understanding, achieving real-time close-loop responses within 0.04 s. Real-world experiments thoroughly validate the effectiveness of AT-VLA in contact-rich manipulation tasks. The project page is available at: https://sites.google.com/view/at-vla.

</details>


### [14] [CSR: Infinite-Horizon Real-Time Policies with Massive Cached State Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07325)
*Robin Karlsson,Go Suzui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CSR框架和ASR算法，通过最优KV缓存复用和异步状态内存驱逐，将大规模LLM作为机器人连续认知引擎的TTFT延迟从14.67s降至0.56s（26倍提升），实现>2Hz的高频具身策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模LLM部署为机器人连续认知引擎时，处理大量状态历史所需的TTFT延迟成为性能瓶颈。现有方案（如RAG或滑动窗口）要么牺牲全局上下文，要么带来高昂的重计算成本。

Method: 首先形式化最优任务结构并理论证明前缀稳定性、增量可扩展性和异步状态协调是实现实时性能的必要条件；然后提出CSR框架作为这些性质的实际实例化，确保最优KV缓存复用；进一步提出ASR算法，将状态内存驱逐卸载到并行计算资源以消除延迟尖峰。

Result: 物理机器人实验：120K token上下文+235B参数模型，延迟从14.67s降至0.56s（26倍）；具身AI基准测试：召回率SOTA（0.836 vs 0.459），同时保持RAG级延迟；ASR在连续真实操作中经10次驱逐周期验证保持无尖峰有界TTFT。

Conclusion: CSR和ASR联合使大规模LLM能够作为连续运行、高频（>2Hz）的具身策略运行，解决了LLM部署为机器人实时认知引擎的关键延迟瓶颈。

Abstract: Deploying massive large language models (LLMs) as continuous cognitive engines for robotics is bottlenecked by the time-to-first-token (TTFT) latency required to process extensive state histories. Existing solutions like RAG or sliding windows compromise global context or incur prohibitive re-computation costs. We formalize the optimal task structure for minimizing latency and theoretically prove that prefix stability, incremental extensibility, and asynchronous state reconciliation are necessary conditions for real-time performance. Building on these proofs, we introduce the Cached State Representation (CSR) framework as the practical instantiation of these properties, ensuring optimal KV-cache reuse. To sustain these properties over infinite horizons, we further propose an Asynchronous State Reconciliation (ASR) algorithm that offloads state memory eviction to a parallel computational resource to eliminate latency spikes. On a physical robot wirelessly connected to an on-premise GPU server, CSR achieves a 26-fold latency reduction (14.67s to 0.56s) for 120K token contexts with a 235B parameter model compared to a standard baseline. On an embodied AI benchmark, we achieve SOTA recall (0.836 vs. 0.459) while maintaining RAG-level latency. ASR is validated to sustain bounded, spike-free TTFT over 10 eviction cycles in continuous real-world operation. Together, CSR and ASR enable massive LLMs to function as continuously operating, high-frequency (> 2 Hz) embodied policies.

</details>


### [15] [Weather-Robust Scene Semantics with Vision-Aligned 4D Radar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07367)
*Kali Hamilton,Christoffer Heckman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将毫米波雷达编码器与冻结的SigLIP视觉嵌入对齐，并通过冻结的视觉语言模型解码结构化场景描述，仅需约7M可训练参数。在K-RADAR数据集的雾、小雪、大雪场景中，所有雷达配置均优于相机基线（后者幻觉率超90%）。研究识别出token-norm不匹配是连接雷达与冻结VLM的主要故障模式，并通过投影层输出LayerNorm解决。此外还分析了编码器复杂度、字幕格式和池化策略的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机和激光雷达在雨、雾、雪等恶劣天气下性能严重退化，而毫米波雷达基本不受影响。现有自动驾驶感知系统依赖视觉或激光雷达，在恶劣天气下不可靠，因此需要探索利用毫米波雷达进行场景理解的方法。

Method: 将雷达编码器输出对齐到冻结的SigLIP视觉嵌入空间，然后通过冻结的视觉语言模型（VLM）解码生成结构化场景描述。仅训练约7M参数（雷达编码器+投影层）。针对token-norm不匹配问题，在投影层输出端添加LayerNorm进行修复。

Result: 在K-RADAR数据集的雾、小雪、大雪场景中，所有雷达配置均优于相机基线。相机基线在恶劣天气下幻觉率超过90%。识别出token-norm不匹配是主要故障模式，LayerNorm可有效解决。

Conclusion: 毫米波雷达结合冻结VLM在恶劣天气场景理解中具有显著优势。未来雷达-VLM管道设计需考虑编码器复杂度、字幕格式和池化策略的权衡。

Abstract: Cameras and LiDAR degrade in rain, fog, and snow, while millimeter-wave radar remains largely unaffected. We align a radar encoder to frozen SigLIP vision
  embeddings and decode structured scene captions through a frozen vision-language model (VLM) with approximately 7M trainable parameters. On K-RADAR with
  held-out fog, light snow, and heavy snow sequences, all radar configurations outperform a camera baseline that collapses to over 90% hallucination. We
  identify a token-norm mismatch as the dominant failure mode when bridging radar to a frozen VLM and show that projector-output LayerNorm resolves it.
  Analysis of encoder complexity, caption format, and pooling strategy reveals tradeoffs that inform future radar-VLM pipeline design.

</details>


### [16] [Many-to-Many Multi-Agent Pickup and Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07835)
*Ethan Schneider,Jingkai Chen,Tianyi Gu,Kunlei Lian,Seth Hutchinson,Sonia Chernova*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对仓库中多对多取送任务（Many-to-Many MAPD）这一NP难的四维分配问题，提出M2M算法及其变体M2M-wSKU，在8小时仿真中相比现有方法平均多完成最多22,000个任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MAPD研究多聚焦一对一的取送任务，而真实仓库中同一SKU可能存放于多个位置，形成多对多的取送场景，该问题为NP难的四维分配问题，亟需高效求解算法。

Method: 提出M2M算法，包含两个变体：M2M（最小化预估任务时长）和M2M-wSKU（将SKU分布纳入目标函数）。通过8小时仓库运营仿真进行验证。

Result: 在不同环境和仓库库存密度下，M2M及其变体一致达到或超越现有最优方法，平均多完成最多22,000个任务。

Conclusion: M2M算法有效解决了多对多MAPD问题，能显著提升仓库多机器人系统的任务吞吐量。

Abstract: Multi-robot systems in automated warehouses must manage continuous streams of pickup-and-delivery tasks while ensuring efficiency and safety. Prior work on Multi-Agent Pickup-and-Delivery (MAPD) has largely focused on the one-to-one variant, where each task has a fixed pickup and delivery location. In contrast, real warehouses often present many-to-many MAPD scenarios, where items, tracked by stock keeping unit (SKU) identifiers, can be retrieved from or stored at multiple locations, resulting in an NP-hard four-dimensional assignment problem. To solve the many-to-many MAPD problem, we contribute our algorithm: Many-to-Many Multi-Agent Pickup and Delivery (M2M). We experiment with two variants of our algorithm: one that minimizes estimated task durations (M2M), and one which incorporates SKU distribution into the objective function (M2M-wSKU). Simulation results over 8-hour warehouse operations show that our method consistently matches or outperforms prior state of the art, with M2M completing up to 22,000 more tasks on average across different environments and warehouse inventory densities.

</details>


### [17] [Escaping the Diversity Trap in Robotic Manipulation via Anchor-Centric Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07381)
*Yanzhe Chen,Kevin Yuchen Ma,Qi Lv,Yiqi Lin,Zechen Bai,Chen Gao,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出锚点中心自适应（ACA）框架，通过重复演示稳定策略骨架再选择性扩展覆盖范围，解决了机器人VLA模型在有限数据预算下因多样性陷阱导致的覆盖-密度权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人演示数据成本高昂，VLA模型在特定硬件上部署存在具身差距，需要在严格数据预算下进行现实世界适应。标准启发式"最大化覆盖"通过收集多样化的单次演示可能导致非渐消估计噪声，形成多样性陷阱。

Method: 首先形式化覆盖-密度权衡，将策略误差分解为估计（密度）和泛化（覆盖）项，刻画固定预算下独特条件的最优内部分配。提出ACA两阶段框架：阶段一在核心锚点通过重复演示稳定策略骨架；阶段二通过教师强迫错误挖掘和约束残差更新选择性扩展高风险边界覆盖。

Result: 真实机器人实验验证了覆盖-密度权衡框架，表明在相同预算下，ACA相比标准多样采样策略显著提升了任务可靠性和成功率。

Conclusion: 本文从理论上揭示了机器人策略适应中覆盖与密度的内在权衡，并提供了实际可行的自适应方法，挑战了"最大化覆盖"的传统直觉。

Abstract: While Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models offer broad general capabilities, deploying them on specific hardware requires real-world adaptation to bridge the embodiment gap. Since robot demonstrations are costly, this adaptation must often occur under a strict data budget. In this work, we identify a critical diversity trap: the standard heuristic of "maximizing coverage" by collecting diverse, single-shot demonstrations can be self-defeating due to non-vanishing estimation noise. We formalize this phenomenon as a Coverage--Density Trade-off. By decomposing the policy error into estimation (density) and extrapolation (coverage) terms, we characterize an interior optimal allocation of unique conditions for a fixed budget. Guided by this analysis, we propose Anchor-Centric Adaptation (ACA), a two-stage framework that first stabilizes a policy skeleton through repeated demonstrations at core anchors, then selectively expands coverage to high-risk boundaries via teacher-forced error mining and constrained residual updates. Real-robot experiments validate our trade-off framework and demonstrate that ACA significantly improves task reliability and success rates over standard diverse sampling strategies under the same budget.

</details>


### [18] [PathPainter: Transferring the Generalization Ability of Image Generation Models to Embodied Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07496)
*Yijin Wang,Yuru Tian,Xijie Huang,Weiqi Gai,Mo Zhu,Xin Zhou,Yuze Wu,Fei Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用鸟瞰图作为全局先验的地面及近地机器人导航系统。该系统通过图像生成模型解析自然语言指令确定目标，生成可通行性掩码，并引入跨视图定位技术对齐里程计与BEV地图以消除漂移。在无人机平台上仅使用局部运动规划器即完成160米室外长距离导航任务，证明了基础模型的世界理解能力可迁移至具身导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BEV图像能为导航提供有价值的全局先验信息，但存在两个关键挑战：如何充分利用全局信息，以及如何在执行过程中可靠地使用这些信息。

Method: 1) 使用图像生成模型从自然语言中解析人类意图，识别目标位置并生成可通行性掩码；2) 引入跨视图定位（cross-view localization）技术，将机器人里程计与BEV地图对齐，缓解传统里程计的长时漂移问题。

Result: 在无人机平台上，仅使用传统局部运动规划器，成功完成了160米室外长距离导航任务。

Conclusion: 基础模型的世界理解能力可以迁移至具身导航任务，使机器人受益于现有图像生成模型的强泛化能力。

Abstract: Bird's-eye-view (BEV) images have been widely demonstrated to provide valuable prior information for navigation. Given the global information provided by such views, two key challenges remain: how to fully exploit this information and how to reliably use it during execution. In this paper, we propose a navigation system that uses BEV images as global priors and is designed for ground and near-ground robotic platforms. The system employs an image generation model to interpret human intent from natural language, identify the target destination, and generate traversability masks. During execution, we introduce cross-view localization to align the robot's odometry with the BEV map and mitigate long-term drift in conventional odometry. We conduct extensive benchmark experiments to evaluate the proposed method and further validate it on a UAV platform. Using only a conventional local motion planner, the UAV successfully completes a 160-meter outdoor long-range navigation task. This work demonstrates how the world-understanding capabilities of foundation models can be transferred to embodied navigation, enabling robots to benefit from the strong generalization ability of existing image generation models.

</details>


### [19] [Is the Future Compatible? Diagnosing Dynamic Consistency in World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07514)
*Bo-Kai Ruan,Teng-Fang Hsiao,Ling Lo,Hong-Han Shuai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出动作-状态一致性（action-state consistency）作为评估世界动作模型（WAMs）可靠性的新维度，并基于此设计了一种无需价值函数的测试时选择策略，有效提升了规划成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WAMs生成的未来预测仅关注视觉合理性，但缺乏对其与动作序列动态兼容性的评估，即预测的动作与状态转移之间是否一致。

Method: 系统研究了联合预测模型和逆动力学模型中的动作-状态一致性；识别了背景坍缩（background collapse）作为边界条件；提出了基于预测未来一致性的无价值共识策略（value-free consensus strategy）用于测试时选择。

Result: 动作-状态一致性在多个任务中系统地区分成功与失败的 rollout，并与学习到的价值估计具有相似的成功-失败趋势；背景坍缩会导致低动态失败的轨迹具有欺骗性的一致性；无价值共识策略在RoboCasa和RoboTwin 2.0上提升了成功率。

Conclusion: 动作-状态一致性既是评估WAM可靠性的诊断工具，也是实现无价值规划的实际信号。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) enable decision-making through imagined rollouts by predicting future observations and actions. However, the reliability of these imagined futures remains under-examined: is a generated future merely visually plausible, or is it dynamically compatible with the action sequence it claims to model? In this work, we identify action-state consistency, the alignment between predicted actions and induced state transitions, as a missing reliability axis for WAMs. Through a systematic study across representative joint-prediction and inverse-dynamics models, we find that action-state consistency systematically separates successful and failed rollouts across many tasks and follows similar success-failure trends as learned value estimates. These results suggest that consistency captures decision-relevant structure beyond visual realism. We further identify background collapse as an important boundary condition, where low-dynamics failed trajectories can become deceptively consistent because static futures are easier to predict. Building on these findings, we introduce a value-free consensus strategy for test-time selection, which ranks candidate rollouts by agreement among predicted futures. This strategy improves success rates on RoboCasa and RoboTwin 2.0 without additional training or reward modeling. Taken together, our findings establish action-state consistency as both a diagnostic tool for evaluating WAM reliability and a practical signal for value-free planning.

</details>


### [20] [Search-based Robustness Testing of Laptop Refurbishing Robotic Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07530)
*Erblin Isaku,Hassan Sartaj,Shaukat Ali,Malaika Din Hashmi,Francois Picard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PROBE方法，一种基于多目标优化的搜索式鲁棒性测试方法，用于发现笔记本电脑翻新机器人中目标检测模型的失败案例，相比随机搜索效果提升3-7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 笔记本电脑翻新机器人中的目标检测模型对微小输入变化敏感，可能导致翻新过程中对笔记本电脑造成损坏，因此需要有效的鲁棒性测试方法。

Method: 提出PROBE方法，采用NSGA-II多目标优化算法系统探索扰动空间，优化目标包括诱导失败（定位和置信度）以及扰动幅度，同时发现多样化的失败案例。

Result: PROBE比随机搜索有效3-7倍，能以更小的扰动幅度生成失败诱导扰动，且生成的扰动可在模型间迁移。

Conclusion: PROBE能有效发现目标检测模型的鲁棒性问题，变体关系分析可提供模型稳定性的额外洞察。

Abstract: The Danish Technological Institute (DTI) focuses on transferring advanced technologies (including robots) to the industry and the public sector. One key application is laptop refurbishment using specialized robots, aimed at promoting reuse, reducing electronic waste, and supporting the European Circular Economy Action Plan. The software of such robots often includes features that use object detection models to detect objects for various purposes, such as identifying screws for laptop disassembly or detecting stickers to remove them. Ensuring the robustness of such models to small input variations remains a critical challenge, and addressing it is important to avoid potential damage to laptops during refurbishment. In this paper, we propose PROBE, a search-based robustness testing approach that leverages multi-objective optimization to identify minimal, localized perturbations that expose failures in object detection models used in the software of laptop refurbishing robots. PROBE employs NSGA-II to systematically explore the perturbation space, optimizing for failure induction considering both localization and confidence, and perturbation magnitude, while enabling the discovery of diverse failure cases. Results show that PROBE is 3$\times$ to 7$\times$ more effective than random search in generating failure-inducing perturbations, while requiring smaller perturbation magnitudes, and that the generated perturbations transfer across models. We further show that metamorphic relations provide additional insights into model robustness, enabling the assessment of stability even in non-failing cases.

</details>


### [21] [How to utilize failure demo data?: Effective data selection for imitation learning using distribution differences in attention mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07560)
*Kana Miyamoto,Kanata Suzuki,Tetsuya Ogata*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用人类数据采集过程中产生的失败数据改进模仿学习的方法，通过学习成功-失败差异的隐表征并将其融入注意力机制，在推理时从初始观察中选择合适的隐模式以提升动作稳定性，同时引入后训练指标量化失败样本与成功演示的注意力差异以筛选有益失败数据。仿真实验表明该方法在混合训练中提高了任务成功率，并能有效识别对学习有帮助的失败样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模仿学习方法主要依赖于成功演示训练策略，但人类数据采集过程中失败难以避免。现有利用失败数据的方法需要额外数据处理或自主 rollout 迭代更新策略，难以直接稳定地利用采集过程中积累的失败数据。

Method: 1) 学习成功-失败差异的隐表征，将其融入注意力机制；2) 推理时从初始观察中选择合适的隐模式以提升动作稳定性；3) 提出后训练指标，量化每个失败样本与成功演示之间的注意力差异，用于筛选失败数据。

Result: 仿真结果表明：所提方法在使用失败数据训练时提高了任务成功率；所提指标能够识别出与成功演示结合时对学习有益的失败样本。

Conclusion: 该方法能够更高效地利用机器人数据采集流程中收集的演示数据，支持在实际采集管道中更有效地使用失败样本。

Abstract: Imitation learning for robotic tasks has relied primarily on policies trained only on successful demonstrations, although failures are unavoidable during human data collection. Many existing approaches for exploiting failure data require additional data processing or iterative policy updates through autonomous rollouts, making it difficult to directly and stably utilize failure data accumulated during data collection. In this work, we propose a method that learns latent representations of success-failure discrepancies and incorporates them into the attention mechanism. During inference, an appropriate latent mode is selected from the initial observation to improve action stability. Furthermore, we introduce a post-training metric that quantifies the attention discrepancy between each failure sample and successful demonstrations to select failure data. Simulation results show that the proposed method improves task success rates when trained with failure data and that the proposed metric identifies failure samples that are beneficial for learning when combined with successful demonstrations. These results suggest that the proposed method can support more efficient use of collected demonstrations in robotic data collection pipelines.

</details>


### [22] [BrickCraft: Visuomotor Skill Composition with Situated Manual Guidance for Long-Horizon Interlocking Brick Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07605)
*Jichuan Yu,Bowei Li,Zhenran Tang,Guanxing Lu,Chuxiong Hu,Ruixuan Liu,Changliu Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BrickCraft是一个用于长时序、可泛化的互锁积木自主机器人装配的组合框架，通过相对公式化建模将复杂任务分解为有限的可重用原始技能，利用情境化手册桥接高层装配计划与物理执行，并采用组合执行流水线链式执行空间定位技能，从有限演示中习得熟练装配能力并展现出对未见结构的强大组合泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人装配互锁积木需要将长时序任务推理、空间定位和精细操控无缝集成，现有方法难以同时满足这三方面需求。

Method: 提出BrickCraft组合框架：1）采用相对公式化建模，将每一步锚定到部分结构中的参考积木，将复杂任务分解为有限的可重用原始技能；2）通过情境化手册，将装配意图投影到实时机器人观测上，桥接高层装配计划与物理执行；3）采用组合执行流水线，链式执行这些空间定位的技能以完成长时序装配任务。

Result: 从有限演示中习得熟练的装配技能，并对未见结构展现出强大的组合泛化能力。

Conclusion: BrickCraft成功实现了长时序、可泛化的自主积木装配，验证了组合框架在机器人装配任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Autonomous robotic assembly of interlocking bricks demands seamless integration of long-horizon task reasoning, spatial grounding, and fine-grained manipulation. This paper presents BrickCraft, a compositional framework designed for long-horizon and generalizable interlocking brick assembly. BrickCraft models the assembly process using a relative formulation, where each step is anchored to a reference brick within the partial structure, thereby decomposing complex tasks into a finite set of reusable primitive skills. BrickCraft bridges the gap between high-level assembly plans and physical execution through situated manuals, which provide explicit spatial guidance for learned visuomotor skills by projecting the assembly intent onto real-time robot observations. Finally, BrickCraft employs a compositional execution pipeline that chains these spatially grounded skills to accomplish long-horizon assembly tasks. Extensive experimental validations demonstrate that BrickCraft acquires proficient assembly skills from a limited set of demonstrations and exhibits strong compositional generalization to unseen structures. The project website is available at https://intelligent-control-lab.github.io/BrickCraft.

</details>


### [23] [Offline-Online Hierarchical 3D Global Relocalization With Synthetic LiDAR Sensing and Descriptor-Space Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07741)
*Jiahua Ren,Kai Shen,Muhua Zhang,Lei Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种离线-在线分层框架用于3D全局重定位，通过解耦搜索空间，在离线阶段生成候选位置及几何描述子索引，在线阶段先进行全局检索获取粗略位姿，再通过点云配准输出精确6-DoF位姿。实验表明，该方法平均重定位时间3秒，定位精度8厘米，相比现有方法计算效率提升一个数量级且精度相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D全局重定位方法在大尺度空间中面临位姿搜索空间庞大和计算开销高的问题，导致在线重定位时间过长，亟需提高计算效率。

Method: 提出离线-在线分层框架：离线阶段在网格地图中模拟LiDAR扫描生成候选位置及对应几何描述子索引；在线阶段先通过全局检索获得粗略位姿估计，再通过点云配准输出精确6-DoF位姿。

Result: 在真实3D环境中，平均重定位时间3秒，平均定位精度8厘米；相比现有全局重定位方法，计算效率提升一个数量级，重定位精度相当。

Conclusion: 该离线-在线分层框架有效解耦了搜索空间，在大尺度3D空间中实现了高效且精确的全局重定位，在计算效率上有显著提升。

Abstract: 3D global relocalization is one of the key capabilities for mobile robots in practical applications. However, in large scale spaces, existing methods often suffer from prolonged online relocalization time due to factors such as the massive pose search space and high computational overhead. To address these issues, this paper proposes an offline-online hierarchical framework that decouples the search space. In the offline phase, candidate positions and their corresponding geometric descriptor indices are generated in the map by simulating LiDAR scans within the grid map. In the online phase, a coarse pose estimate is first obtained via global retrieval, followed by point cloud registration to output precise 6-DoF pose estimates. Real-world experiments demonstrate that the proposed method achieves an average relocalization time of 3 s and an average localization accuracy of 8 cm in 3D environments. Compared with existing global relocalization methods, the proposed method achieves an order-of-magnitude improvement in computational efficiency while delivering comparable relocalization accuracy.

</details>


### [24] [Sensitivity-Based Robust NMPC for Close-Proximity Offshore Wind Turbine Inspection with a Tilted Multirotor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07771)
*Giuseppe Silano,Martin Saska*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于灵敏度的鲁棒NMPC方法，通过在线约束收紧解决倾转多旋翼在近距风机塔筒检测中的安全避碰问题，在500次蒙特卡洛测试中完全消除名义NMPC的净空违规，计算时间仅适度增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近距海上风机检测要求严格的塔筒净空控制，但名义NMPC在质量、惯量、推力效率、阻力或风况偏离标称值时可能违反安全约束。

Method: 采用基于灵敏度的鲁棒NMPC：利用一阶参数状态灵敏度提供结构不确定性裕度，结合阶段依赖的附加裕度处理阵风扰动，在名义NMPC基础上仅增灵敏度传播和裕度评估，保持优化结构不变。

Result: 在边界关键的螺旋检测轨迹上，500次不确定性实现下的蒙特卡洛评估表明，所提控制器完全消除了名义NMPC出现的净空违规，求解时间仅适度增加。

Conclusion: 基于灵敏度的约束收紧方法能有效提升倾转多旋翼在模型失配和风扰下的近距检测安全性，计算代价可控。

Abstract: Close-proximity offshore wind turbine inspection requires strict clearance control around large cylindrical structures under wind and model mismatch. Nominal Nonlinear Model Predictive Control (NMPC) may violate safety constraints when mass, inertia, thrust effectiveness, drag, or wind conditions differ from nominal assumptions. We propose a sensitivity-based robust NMPC for a tilted multirotor that robustifies the tower-clearance constraint via online constraint tightening. First-order parametric state sensitivities provide a structured-uncertainty margin, while bounded gusts are handled by a stage-dependent additive margin. The formulation augments the nominal NMPC with sensitivity propagation and margin evaluation only, leaving the receding-horizon optimization structure unchanged. Monte-Carlo evaluation over 500 uncertainty realizations on a boundary-critical helical inspection trajectory shows that the proposed controller eliminates the clearance violations observed under nominal NMPC at the cost of a moderate increase in solve time.

</details>


### [25] [Melding LLM and temporal logic for reliable human-swarm collaboration in complex scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07877)
*Junfeng Chen,Yuxiao Zhu,An Zhuo,Xintong Zhang,Shuo Zhang,Guanghui Wen,Xiwang Dong,Meng Guo,Zhongkui Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种神经符号框架，将时序逻辑形式化约束与LLM上下文推理相结合，实现动态环境中长时域人-群机器人系统的可靠任务规划，并通过不确定性感知调度和事件触发交互降低操作者认知负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在动态场景下，长时域人-群机器人协作面临两大挑战：一是突发事件和变化条件需要持续适应性调整，二是操作者持续监管带来巨大认知负担。现有基于LLM的规划工具容易产生无效任务排序和不可行机器人动作，导致频繁人工干预。

Method: 1) 将任务目标与操作规则形式化为时序逻辑公式，将可行的任务排序建模为任务自动机；2) 在此基础上，结合实时感知上下文，让LLM生成满足规则且情境可执行的子任务序列；3) 采用不确定性感知调度器在异构群机器人间分配子任务，最大化并行性并抵御干扰；4) 设计事件触发交互协议，将操作者介入限制在稀疏的高层确认与指导上。

Result: 在异构机器人集群上的部署结果表明，该方法能在保持与已有方案相似性能的同时，对硬件特定的执行与通信不确定性保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该工作为动态环境下可靠且低认知负荷的人-群机器人协作提供了一种形式化、可扩展的范式。

Abstract: Robot swarms promise scalable assistance in complex and hazardous environments. Task planning lies at the core of human-swarm collaboration, translating the operator's intent into coordinated swarm actions and helping determine when validation or intervention is required during execution. In long-horizon missions under dynamic scenarios, however, reliable task planning becomes difficult to maintain: emerging events and changing conditions demand continual adaptation, and sustained operator oversight imposes substantial cognitive burden. Existing LLM-based planning tools can support plan generation, yet they remain susceptible to invalid task orderings and infeasible robot actions, resulting in frequent manual adjustment. Here we introduce a neuro-symbolic framework for long-horizon human-swarm collaboration that tightly melds verifiable task planning with context-grounded LLM reasoning. We formalize mission goals and operational rules as temporal logic formulas and admissible task orderings as task automata. Conditioned on these formal constraints and live perceptual context, LLMs generate executable subtask sequences that satisfy mission rules and remain grounded in the current scene. An uncertainty-aware scheduler then assigns subtasks across the heterogeneous swarm to maximize parallelisms while remaining resilient to disruptions. An event-triggered interaction protocol further limits operator involvement to sparse, high-level confirmation and guidance. Deployment on a heterogeneous robotic fleet yields similar results while remaining robust to hardware-specific actuation and communication uncertainties. Together, these results support a formal and scalable paradigm for reliable and low-overhead human-swarm collaboration in dynamic environments

</details>


### [26] [AERO-VIS: Asynchronous Event-based Real-time Onboard Visual-Inertial SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07885)
*Yannick Burkhardt,Sebastián Barbas Laina,Simon Boche,Leonard Freißmuth,Stefan Leutenegger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AERO-VIS是一个基于立体事件相机和IMU的SLAM系统，采用异步事件处理、数据驱动的关键点检测器，在无人机上实现了首个纯事件驱动的闭环控制和大规模状态估计，并开源了代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机具有高动态范围和抗运动模糊的优势，但现有事件里程计方法仍采用固定频率处理，限制了延迟和吞吐量。本文旨在构建一个异步、低延迟、高精度的纯事件惯性SLAM系统，并首次实现无人机闭环控制。

Method: 提出AERO-VIS系统，包含：1) 立体事件相机+IMU的SLAM框架；2) 异步事件流处理机制，动态适应下游运行需求；3) 集成数据驱动、鲁棒且性能优化的关键点检测器。

Result: 在无人机上实现了前所未有的机载事件SLAM精度，并首次展示了纯事件惯性SLAM系统支持无人机闭环控制和大规模状态估计，所有计算均在机载完成。

Conclusion: AERO-VIS证明了纯事件惯性SLAM在真实无人机平台上实现闭环控制的可行性，为事件相机在机器人领域的应用提供了新范式，代码已开源。

Abstract: The robustness of event cameras to high dynamic range and motion blur holds the potential to improve visual odometry systems in challenging environments. Although their high temporal resolution does not require synchronous processing, most event-based odometry methods still run at fixed rates, which simplifies system design but restricts latency and throughput. In this work, we present AERO-VIS, a stereo event-inertial SLAM system with an integrated, data-driven, robust, and performance-optimized keypoint detector. By processing the event stream asynchronously, the system dynamically adapts to downstream runtime demands, ensuring low-latency and real-time performance. When deploying AERO-VIS on a UAV, we achieve unprecedented accuracy in onboard event-based SLAM. These unique characteristics enable us to present the first purely event-based inertial SLAM system that demonstrates closed-loop UAV control and large-scale state estimation while relying solely on onboard compute. A video of the experiments and the source code are available at ethz-mrl.github.io/AERO-VIS.

</details>


### [27] [TAVIS: A Benchmark for Egocentric Active Vision and Anticipatory Gaze in Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07943)
*Giacomo Spigler*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TAVIS是一个面向主动视觉模仿学习的标准化评估框架，包含两个任务套件（TAVIS-Head和TAVIS-Hands）、三个评估原语（配对头戴vs固定相机协议、GALT度量、程序化ID/OOD划分）以及两个人体形态平台上的基准实验。实验表明主动视觉通常有益但效果任务相关，多任务策略在分布偏移下性能急剧下降，且纯模仿即可产生预期性注视。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动视觉在模仿学习中已展现出显著优势，但缺乏统一的基准来比较不同方法、量化其贡献及适用条件。

Method: TAVIS框架包含：1) TAVIS-Head（5个全局搜索任务，使用云台颈）和TAVIS-Hands（3个局部遮挡任务，使用腕部相机）两个互补任务套件；2) 三种评估原语——配对头戴vs固定相机协议、基于认知科学和人机交互的GALT度量、程序化ID/OOD划分；3) 在GR1T2和Reachy2两种人体形态平台上基于IsaacLab构建。

Result: 基于Diffusion Policy和π₀的基线实验表明：(i) 主动视觉通常有帮助但效果任务相关而非统一；(ii) 多任务策略在受控分布偏移下性能急剧下降；(iii) 纯模仿即可产生预期性注视，中位领先时间与人类遥操作员参考相当。

Conclusion: TAVIS为主动视觉模仿学习提供了首个标准化基准和评估工具，有助于社区系统地比较和推进该领域研究。

Abstract: Active vision -- where a policy controls its own gaze during manipulation -- has emerged as a key capability for imitation learning, with multiple independent systems demonstrating its benefits in the past year. Yet there is no shared benchmark to compare approaches or quantify what active vision contributes, on which task types, and under what conditions. We introduce TAVIS, evaluation infrastructure for active-vision imitation learning, with two complementary task suites -- TAVIS-Head (5 tasks, global search via pan/tilt necks) and TAVIS-Hands (3 tasks, local occlusion via wrist cameras) -- on two humanoid torso embodiments (GR1T2, Reachy2), built on IsaacLab. TAVIS provides three evaluation primitives: a paired headcam-vs-fixedcam protocol on identical demonstrations; GALT (Gaze-Action Lead Time), a novel metric grounded in cognitive science and HRI that quantifies anticipatory gaze in learned policies; and procedural ID/OOD splits. Baseline experiments with Diffusion Policy and $π_0$ reveal that (i) active-vision generally helps, but benefits are task-conditional rather than uniform; (ii) multi-task policies degrade sharply under controlled distribution shifts on both suites; and (iii) imitation alone yields anticipatory gaze, with median lead times comparable to the human teleoperator reference. Code, evaluation scripts, demonstrations (LeRobot v3.0; ~2200 episodes) and trained baselines are released at https://github.com/spiglerg/tavis and https://huggingface.co/tavis-benchmark.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文通过仿真实验研究了四足机器人Silver Badger在矢状面配备1-DOF主动脊柱对学习敏捷运动能力的影响，结果显示主动脊柱能在高速奔跑、爬楼梯、爬坡、跨越障碍和匍匐穿越等场景中提升机器人的敏捷性和通过能力。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 在MuJoCo仿真环境中，使用MAB Robotics的Silver Badger机器人（配备矢状面1-DOF主动脊柱），通过对比有无脊柱的情况，在高速奔跑、爬楼梯、爬高角度斜坡、跨越障碍和匍匐五种场景下进行实证研究。

Result: 主动脊柱使机器人能够克服更高的台阶、更陡的斜坡、更高的障碍物和更狭窄的通道，整体敏捷性显著提升。

Conclusion: 矢状面主动脊柱对四足机器人的敏捷运动能力有积极贡献，尤其在需要高通过性的复杂地形场景中表现出明显优势。

Abstract: The spine plays a crucial role in the dynamic locomotion of quadrupedal animals, improving the stability, speed, and efficiency of their gait, especially for fast-paced and highly agile movements. Therefore, the spine is also a promising and natural way to extend the capabilities of quadruped robots. This paper empirically investigates the benefits of an actuated spine for learning agile quadruped locomotion. We evaluate whether the use of the spine brings benefits in terms of high-speed running, climbing stairs, climbing high-angle slopes, hurdling, and crawling scenarios. We conducted an empirical study in MuJoCo simulation using the Silver Badger robot from MAB Robotics with an actuated 1-DOF spine in the sagittal plane. The obtained results show that the use of the spine provides the robot with increased agility and allows it to overcome higher stairs, steeper slopes, higher obstacles, and smaller passages.

</details>

Result: 该方法能够有效推断腿式机器人的关节级和全局本体参数，通过主动交互提升本体感知的准确性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 主动信息寻求与显式本体预测的联合学习能有效提升腿式机器人的本体感知能力，为机器人在未知环境中的自适应提供重要基础。

Abstract: We present an active embodiment identification method for legged robots that jointly learns information-seeking behavior and explicit embodiment prediction. Using a history-augmented URMA architecture, the method infers joint-level and global embodiment parameters through interaction with the environment in simulation across different morphologies.

### [30] [123D: Unifying Multi-Modal Autonomous Driving Data at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08084)
*Daniel Dauner,Valentin Charraut,Bastian Berle,Tianyu Li,Long Nguyen,Jiabao Wang,Changhui Jing,Maximilian Igl,Holger Caesar,Boris Ivanovic,Yiyi Liao,Andreas Geiger,Kashyap Chitta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 123D是一个开源框架，通过统一的API整合多模态驾驶数据，将8个真实世界数据集（3300小时/9万公里）和1个合成数据集统一为独立时间戳事件流格式，支持跨数据集的同步/异步访问、分析和可视化，并展示了跨数据集3D检测迁移和规划强化学习等应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动驾驶数据集规模庞大但碎片化严重：不同数据集采用不同2D/3D传感器模态、采样率和同步方案，格式不兼容且依赖复杂，标注规范不一致，阻碍了跨数据集训练和泛化能力评估。

Method: 提出123D开源框架，核心创新是将每种模态存储为独立的时间戳事件流（无预设采样率），通过单一API实现统一访问。框架整合了8个真实世界数据集（3300小时/9万公里）和1个可配置合成数据集，并提供数据分析和可视化工具。

Result: 系统性地比较了各数据集的标注统计信息、位姿和标定精度；展示了两个应用案例：跨数据集3D目标检测迁移学习和规划任务的强化学习。

Conclusion: 123D有效解决了多数据集统一管理问题，并为未来自动驾驶数据标准化和跨数据集研究提供了建议。

Abstract: The pursuit of autonomous driving has produced one of the richest sensor data collections in all of robotics. However, its scale and diversity remain largely untapped. Each dataset adopts different 2D and 3D modalities, such as cameras, lidar, ego states, annotations, traffic lights, and HD maps, with different rates and synchronization schemes. They come in fragmented formats requiring complex dependencies that cannot natively coexist in the same development environment. Further, major inconsistencies in annotation conventions prevent training or measuring generalization across multiple datasets. We present 123D, an open-source framework that unifies such multi-modal driving data through a single API. To handle synchronization, we store each modality as an independent timestamped event stream with no prescribed rate, enabling synchronous or asynchronous access across arbitrary datasets. Using 123D, we consolidate eight real-world driving datasets spanning 3,300 hours and 90,000 kilometers, together with a synthetic dataset with configurable collection scripts, and provide tools for data analysis and visualization. We conduct a systematic study comparing annotation statistics and assessing each dataset's pose and calibration accuracy. Further, we showcase two applications 123D enables: cross-dataset 3D object detection transfer and reinforcement learning for planning, and offer recommendations for future directions. Code and documentation are available at https://github.com/kesai-labs/py123d.

</details>

### [31] [When to Use Wireless Challenge-Response Physical Layer Authentication: Design of a Measurable Guideline for OFDM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06750)
*Haiyun Liu,Shangqing Zhao,Yao Liu,Zhuo Lu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 针对OFDM无线信道响应相关性导致物理层认证（PLA）安全性不足的问题，提出最大差分似然生成器（MDLG）攻击模型，利用信道弱相关性发起有效攻击，并建立基于随机性测试的可量化准则来判定PLA的适用条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PLA研究假设无线信道具有充分随机的衰落条件，但实际场景中OFDM子信道响应存在相关性，导致该假设不成立，PLA安全性面临威胁。

Method: 提出MDLG（最大差分似然生成器） adversary模型，利用实际无线信道的弱相关特性对PLA发起攻击；并基于随机性测试建立可量化的判定准则，评估何时PLA可在实际信道条件下安全使用。

Result: 大量真实实验验证了MDLG攻击的有效性，并展示了所提出的判定准则能够帮助保护PLA的安全性。

Conclusion: 实际信道相关性会破坏PLA的安全性，MDLG攻击可有效利用此弱点；所提随机性测试准则能为PLA的工程部署提供安全决策依据。

Abstract: The security of wireless challenge-response Physical Layer Authentication (PLA) based on Orthogonal Frequency Division Multiplexing (OFDM) relies on a sufficiently random fading channel condition, which is commonly assumed in existing studies. However, in practical scenarios, such a condition is not always guaranteed and the responses of OFDM subchannels may exhibit correlation.} Consequently, ensuring the security of such PLA systems remains an unsolved problem. In this paper, we propose a novel adversary model, called Maximum Differential Likelihood Generator (MDLG), which exploits the weak correlation property in practical wireless channel to launch effective attacks against PLA. Based on this model, we create a measurable guideline using randomness testing to decide when we can in fact use PLA in a practical wireless channel condition. Extensive real-world experiments validate the effectiveness of the MDLG attack and demonstrate how the proposed guideline can help protect the security of PLA.

</details>


### [32] [Unconsented Sensing: A Sociotechnical Governance Framework for 6G ISAC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07328)
*Anass Sedrati*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文指出6G通感一体化（ISAC）的隐私与可信赖性问题。现有标准化工作过度依赖加密匿名化和物理层安全等技术方案，忽视了下游机器学习模型带来的社会技术和法律复杂性，与现行数字权利立法（如欧盟AI法案和GDPR）产生冲突。作者提出将ISAC可信赖性重新定义为强制性监管与社会技术合规，并构建了以目的绑定感知激活、公民透明度机制和算法问责制三大支柱为核心的治理框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有6G ISAC标准化工作对隐私和可信赖性的应对措施过于技术中心主义，低估了机器学习模型处理原始感知数据所引入的社会技术及法律复杂性，导致与现行数字权利法规产生冲突。

Method: 以欧盟AI法案和GDPR为监管基线，识别ISAC持续监控与数字权利体制之间的法律摩擦点，提出目的绑定感知激活、公民透明度机制和算法问责制三大支柱的治理框架。

Result: 明确了ISAC技术与现行数字权利法规之间的具体法律冲突点，并提出了一个包含三大支柱的监管合规治理框架。

Conclusion: 技术安全并不足够，ISAC可信赖性必须重新定义为强制性监管与社会技术合规，该监管路线图可防止6G感知基础设施的非法部署，确保其在物理部署前具备合规可行性。

Abstract: The forthcoming deployment of 6G Integrated Sensing and Communication (ISAC) will transform cellular infrastructure into pervasive, continuous environmental and biometric sensing grids. While current telecom standardization efforts (e.g., 3GPP, ETSI) have formally recognized privacy and trustworthiness as critical pillars for 6G, their proposed mitigations remain overwhelmingly technocentric, relying on cryptographic anonymization and physical layer security. This approach critically underestimates the sociotechnical and legal complexities of the downstream machine learning (ML) models required to interpret raw sensing data, creating a profound collision with existing digital rights legislation. This position paper argues that technical security is insufficient. ISAC trustworthiness must be redefined as mandatory regulatory and sociotechnical compliance. We identify the specific legal friction points between continuous ISAC surveillance and the mandates of emerging global digital rights regimes, using the stringent requirements of the EU AI Act and GDPR as our primary regulatory baselines. To bridge this gap, we propose a governance framework centered on three pillars: Purpose-bound sensing activation, citizen transparency mechanisms, and algorithmic accountability for ISAC-driven ML models. Ultimately, this paper provides a regulatory roadmap to prevent the illegal deployment of 6G sensing infrastructures and ensure they remain viable before physical deployment.

</details>

### [33] [Broken-symmetry shape discrimination on a driven Duffing ring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07475)
*Kaspar Anton Schindler*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文在环图（cycle graph）这一最简单的连续对称闭合基底上，研究了分布式计算基元——捆绑（bundling）与绑定（binding）——的动力学。通过单一主方程的两个参数区间（线性与Duffing非线性），展示了特征表示与形状相关谐波生成的机制，并识别出一个单标量可观测量φ₀，其在耗散破缺时间反演对称性的条件下，编码了输入形状的绑定与耗散联合响应，且在低信噪比下仍保持信息内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解分布式计算基元（捆绑与绑定）在具有连续对称性的最简单闭合基底上的运作机制，探索线性与非线性动力学如何通过基底对称性约束实现特征表示与信息编码。

Method: 采用环图上单一主方程，在线性区间利用U(1)对称性将时间输入映射到本征模上，实现与加窗FFT对标的多特征表示；在Duffing非线性区间激活受对称性约束的立方模混合，产生整数波数稀疏选择规则下的谐波含量。通过单标量可观测量φ₀分析对称性结构（π周期性与耗散破缺的时间反演对称性）。

Result: 线性区在高信噪比下匹配加窗FFT基线，在低信噪比瞬态信号上略优；Duffing区间产生线性区间无法实现的形状相关谐波；φ₀在0 dB输入信噪比下仍保持高于对称吸引子均值的信息内容。

Conclusion: φ₀作为有意义的单标量可观测量，其对称性分析揭示了绑定与耗散联合响应机制；框架目前基于合成信号，向更丰富基底、更复杂驱动及真实生物信号的扩展是后续开放问题。

Abstract: Distributed computational substrates rely on two elementary operations: bundling, the act of populating a shared physical medium with independently retrievable components, and binding, the act of composing components into outputs whose identity depends on their relations. We study these two primitives on the simplest closed substrate carrying a continuous symmetry, a cycle graph of N nodes, in two parameter regimes of a single master equation of motion. The linear regime sorts a temporal input across the substrate's U(1)-organised eigenmodes, providing a feature representation that matches a windowed-FFT baseline at high signal-to-noise ratio and modestly outperforms it for transient signals at low SNR. The Duffing regime activates a cubic mode-mixing operation constrained by the substrate's symmetry into a sparse selection rule on integer wavenumbers, generating shape-dependent harmonic content that the linear regime cannot produce. We identify a single-number observable, $φ_0$, that summarises the bound representation's response to input shape, and we analyse its symmetry structure: a $π$-periodicity in the shape parameter is exact, while a time-reversal symmetry that would render $φ_0$ degenerate is broken by the substrate's dissipation. The asymmetric status of these two symmetries is what licenses $φ_0$ as a meaningful single-number observable; its trajectory across the quotient domain encodes the joint response of binding and dissipation to the input shape. Numerical experiments confirm that $φ_0$ retains its information content under additive band-limited noise, with seed-averaged means staying clearly above the symmetric-attractor value down to 0 dB input SNR. The framework is developed on synthetic signals only; extensions to richer substrates, more elaborate drives, and real biological signals are open questions for the work that follows.

</details>

### [34] [Relating the Computational and Logical Difficulty of Solving ODEs: From Polynomial to Discontinuous Right-Hand Sides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07128)
*Olivier Bournez,Alonso Núñez*

Main category: cs.SC

TL;DR: 本文提出用逆数学作为不依赖于表示的透镜，将常微分方程的计算复杂性分层为从多项式时间可解到超限计算的不同层级，并证明Big Five层次中的每一层都对应经典ODE理论中的自然陈述，从而沟通了符号计算、模拟计算和可计算分析三种传统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机代数系统无法求解ODE时，根本原因是实现局限还是计算固有障碍？三种传统（现代计算机代数算法、模拟计算模型、可计算分析）各有洞见却因表示方式不同而难以直接比较，亟需一个表示不变的框架来区分本质障碍与技术缺陷。

Method: 采用逆数学（Reverse Mathematics）作为表示不变的分析透镜，将古典ODE理论中的初值问题映射到Big Five层次结构的各个层级，证明f的正则性是一个内在算法不变量，将y'=f(t,y)的计算复杂性从多项式时间到超限计算进行分层。

Result: 证明了Big Five层次的每一层都对应一个经典ODE理论中的自然陈述（精确等价关系），建立了基于f正则性的计算复杂度分层，该分层适用于三种传统，并能区分本质障碍与符号求解器技术缺陷、模拟编码伪迹及可计算分析有效性约束。

Conclusion: 通过抽象掉表示方式，逆数学提供了一个统一的诊断框架，能够识别恢复可解性的内在参数（长度界、收敛半径、连续模），从而分离根本性计算障碍与技术性实现限制。

Abstract: When a computer algebra system fails to solve an Ordinary Differential Equation, is this a limitation of its implementation, or a genuine computational barrier?
  Three traditions bear on the question. Modern computer algebra algorithms can be extremely efficient: Newton-type methods solve polynomial ODEs over $\mathbb{Q}[[X]]$ in quasi-linear time. Analog models of computation has shown that polynomial ODEs and Turing machines are two presentations of the same phenomenon, with solution length acting as time and precision as space. Computable analysis shows that ODEs can be intrinsically hard -- undecidable, even $\mathsf{PSPACE}$-complete, over compact domains. Comparing these traditions is natural and necessary, yet such comparisons routinely reduce to comparisons of encodings rather than of underlying algorithmic content.
  We argue that reverse mathematics provides a representation-invariant lens in which algorithmic content is compared directly. We prove that every level of the Big Five hierarchy is inhabited by a natural statement from classical ODE theory, as an exact equivalence: the regularity of $f$ is an intrinsic algorithmic invariant placing the initial value problem $y'(t)=f(t,y(t))$, $y(t_0)=y_0$, into one of several computational strata, ranging from polynomial-time solvability to transfinite computation.
  The resulting stratification acts as a practical diagnostic common to the three traditions. By abstracting from representation, it separates fundamental barriers from the technical shortcomings of symbolic solvers, the artefacts of analog encodings, and the effectivity constraints of computable analysis, identifying the intrinsic parameters (length bounds, radii of convergence, moduli of continuity) under which feasibility is restored.

</details>

### [35] [Bounding Fixed Points of Non-Monotone Processes: Theory to Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06803)
*Abdullah H. Rasheed,Vijay K. Garg*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文针对非单调推理中的不动点近似问题，提出了基于抽象解释的改进方法，通过引入受控的不完全性来优化AFT的最佳近似，设计了可终止且多项式时间复杂度的算法，并在回答集编程和推测性程序分析中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有近似不动点理论(AFT)在非单调计算中提供的近似解往往过于粗糙且计算开销大，难以在实际的编程语言场景中实用。目标是同时解决AFT近似方法的低效性和近似精度不足的问题。

Method: (1) 证明了基于抽象解释近似算子的合理性；(2) 引入可控的不完全性，设计了一种分区细化AFT最佳近似的实用算法；(3) 该算法具有合理性、任意时刻可中断性，并在有限高格上保证终止；(4) 进一步改进确保多项式时间复杂度。

Result: 在回答集编程中作为收敛加速预处理器；在推测性程序分析中减少回滚同时保持合理性。两个场景的实现在实现层面展示了方法的实用性。

Conclusion: 通过结合抽象解释和可控不完全性，成功将AFT近似方法转化为可用于编程语言实践的高效工具，在加速非单调计算的同时保持了合理的精度。

Abstract: Many modern solvers and program analyzers rely on non-monotone reasoning (e.g. negation-as-failure, speculative updates, backtracking) for which classical monotone fixed-point methods do not apply. The general problem of finding the fixed points of these processes is a difficult one. For this reason, there have been theoretical efforts in existing Approximation Fixpoint Theory (AFT) from the domain of logic programming to approximate fixed points of non-monotone operators. Tight approximations of these fixed points are highly useful for accelerating non-monotonic computations by restricting the search space. In practice, however, even the best approximations obtained through AFT can be coarse and computationally expensive. We aim to address both issues to make AFT approximation methods practical for use in programming languages (PL) settings. To mitigate inefficiency, we prove the soundness of an abstract interpretation for approximating operators. To improve upon coarse approximations, we carefully introduce controlled unsoundness to design an effective yet practical algorithm for partitioning and tightening AFT's best approximations. This algorithm is sound, anytime, and guarantees termination on finite-height lattices. We further present a modification that ensures polynomial-time complexity. We instantiate these methods in two settings: (1) answer set programming, where it serves as a convergence-accelerating pre-processor, and (2) speculative program analysis, where it reduces rollback while preserving soundness. In both settings, we focus on implementation-level details to demonstrate the practical applicability of our methods.

</details>

*Wolfram Pfeifer,Mattias Ulbrich,Daniel Drodt*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的程序验证交互概念，允许用户在源代码和规格层面检查和操作证明状态，最小化心理鸿沟，并通过KeY引擎的插件实现和用户研究证明了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有程序验证器要么需要用户在源代码中编写注解但无法检查证明状态或干预失败尝试，要么允许在比用户提供的规格更低层次的逻辑编码上进行交互，存在心理鸿沟。

Result: 通过用户研究表明，该原型有助于用户理解证明状态并发现源代码或规格中的缺陷。

Abstract: Fully functional program verification is an undecidable$\unicode{x2014}$and, hence, inherently difficult$\unicode{x2014}$task, that is not automatically solvable but typically requires user interaction and guidance. Existing verifiers either work autoactively, requiring the user to write annotations in source code, without the possibility to inspect the proof state or intervene in case of an unsuccessful attempt, or allow interactions on a logical encoding that is on a lower level than the user-provided specifications. We present a novel interaction concept which allows the user to inspect and interact with the proof state on source code and specification level. This minimizes the mental gap between the representations. We provide an implementation of the concept as a plugin for the Java verification engine KeY, and show with a user study that this prototype can be beneficial for users to understand the proof state and find defects in source code or specifications.

</details>
