### [37] [A Unified Measure-Theoretic View of Diffusion, Score-Based, and Flow Matching Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06829)
*Aditya Ranganath,Mukesh Singhal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该综述统一了基于连续时间生成建模的扩散模型、基于分数的生成模型和流匹配方法，通过将三者归结为学习一个诱导边际分布族的时间相关向量场，并对比了目标函数、采样方案和离散化误差，连接了薛定谔桥和熵最优输运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前扩散模型、分数匹配和流匹配等方法虽然方法论趋于收敛，但零散的符号体系和竞争性的推导方式掩盖了共享结构和实际权衡（如采样、稳定性和计算），因此亟需一个统一的理论框架来梳理这些方法的内在联系。

Method: 构建统一框架：(i) 将扩散和分数模型的逆时采样推导为受控随机动力学；(ii) 证明概率流ODE产生相同的边际分布，并连接扩散模型与基于似然的归一化流；(iii) 将流匹配解释为在选定插值下对速度场的直接回归，阐明其与分数匹配训练的重合与差异。

Result: 在统一符号体系下比较了不同方法的目标函数、采样方案和离散化误差；讨论了与薛定谔桥和熵最优输运的联系；总结了关于逼近、稳定性和可扩展性的理论保证与开放问题。

Conclusion: 该统一框架厘清了扩散模型、分数模型和流匹配之间的共享结构与关键差异，为后续理论分析和实践中的权衡选择提供了清晰的指导，同时指出了该领域仍有待解决的理论问题。

Abstract: We survey continuous-time generative modeling methods based on transporting a simple reference distribution to a data distribution via stochastic or deterministic dynamics. We present a unified framework in which diffusion models, score-based generative models, and flow matching are instances of learning a time-dependent vector field that induces a family of marginals $(ρ_t)_{t \in [0,1]}$ governed by continuity and Fokker-Planck equations. Such a unified theory is timely because these methods are converging methodologically, yet fragmented notation and competing derivations continue to obscure their shared structure and the practical tradeoffs governing sampling, stability, and computation. Within this framework, we (i) derive reverse-time sampling for diffusion and score-based models as controlled stochastic dynamics, (ii) show that the probability flow ODE yields identical marginals and connects diffusion to likelihood-based normalizing flows, and (iii) interpret flow matching as direct regression of the velocity field under a chosen interpolation, clarifying when it coincides with or differs from score-based training. We compare objectives, sampling schemes, and discretization errors under unified notation, discuss connections to Schrodinger bridges and entropic optimal transport, and summarize theoretical guarantees and open problems on approximation, stability, and scalability.

</details>


### [38] [RateQuant: Optimal Mixed-Precision KV Cache Quantization via Rate-Distortion Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06675)
*Fei Zuo,Zikang Zhou,Hao Cong,Xiaoyan Xi,Ho Fai Leung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对KV缓存量化的混合精度分配问题，本文发现不同量化器的失真模型不匹配会导致分配顺序反转、性能劣于均匀量化，提出RateQuant方法，通过校准集拟合每量化器的失真模型并利用率失真理论的逆向注水算法闭式求解比特分配，在Qwen3-8B上2.5平均比特下将KIVI的困惑度从49.3降至14.9（降幅70%），校准仅需1.6秒且推理零开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KV缓存随序列长度线性增长成为服务端主要内存瓶颈，现有量化方法对所有注意力头分配相同比特数，忽略了不同头部的重要性差异；而混合精度分配存在隐藏陷阱：不同量化器的失真曲线衰减率差异大（3.6~5.3），误用失真模型会导致分配顺序反转、性能劣于均匀量化。

Method: 提出RateQuant方法：从小型校准集拟合每个量化器的失真模型D(b)=alpha*beta^{-b}，然后基于率失真理论的逆向注水算法（reverse waterfilling）闭式求解比特分配问题。

Result: Qwen3-8B在2.5平均比特下，RateQuant将KIVI的困惑度从49.3降至14.9（70%下降），比QuaRot提升6.6 PPL；校准仅需单GPU 1.6秒，推理零开销。

Conclusion: 混合精度KV缓存量化必须考虑量化器自身的失真模型特性，直接套用其他量化器的失真曲线会导致分配失效；RateQuant通过校准拟合失真模型+逆向注水算法，有效解决了这一失配问题，实现了显著性能提升且开销极小。

Abstract: Large language models cache all previously computed key-value (KV) pairs during generation, and this KV cache grows linearly with sequence length, making it a primary memory bottleneck for serving. Quantizing the KV cache to fewer bits reduces this cost, yet all current quantizers assign the same bit-width to every attention head, ignoring the large variation in head importance. A natural idea is to allocate more bits to important heads and fewer to the rest. We show, however, that such mixed-precision allocation has a hidden pitfall: each quantizer follows a different distortion curve D(b)=alpha*beta^{-b}, and the decay rate beta varies from 3.6 to 5.3 across quantizer designs. Applying one quantizer's distortion model to another inverts the allocation order and makes performance worse than uniform quantization. We call this failure mode distortion model mismatch and propose RateQuant to resolve it. RateQuant fits a per-quantizer distortion model from a small calibration set, then solves the resulting bit-allocation problem in closed form via reverse waterfilling from rate-distortion theory. On Qwen3-8B at 2.5 average bits, calibrated RateQuant reduces KIVI's perplexity from 49.3 to 14.9 (70% reduction) and improves QuaRot by 6.6 PPL. The entire calibration takes 1.6 s on a single GPU and adds zero overhead at inference time.

</details>


### [39] [Physical Simulators as Do-Operators: Causal Discovery under Latent Confounders for AI-for-Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07467)
*Tsuyoshi Okita*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CFM-SD是一种利用第一性原理物理模拟器作为do-算子的因果流匹配方法，能够在存在潜在混淆变量的情况下处理真实干预数据，在合成数据上F1=0.800（远超基线0.127–0.562），并在分子毒性预测和电池电解质优化中实现57–58%的偏差降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果发现方法（IGSP、DCDI、ENCO）假定因果充分性（无潜在混淆变量）且依赖合成模拟器中的虚拟干预，但在AI for Science场景（如分子设计、材料科学）中潜在混淆变量普遍存在，且真实干预（如基于物理的模拟）耗时极长（数小时到数天），亟需一种能同时处理潜在混淆变量和真实干预数据的方法。

Method: 提出CFM-SD（Causal Flow Matching with Simulation Data），将第一性原理物理模拟器作为Pearl干预演算中的do-算子，通过因果流匹配同时处理潜在混淆变量和真实干预数据。理论上证明了在物理可实现约束下，d变量因果结构只需O(d)次单变量干预即可识别。

Result: 内在评估：合成数据（γ=0.2–0.8）上CFM-SD平均F1=0.800，所有基线F1仅0.127–0.562。外在评估：在分子毒性预测和电池电解质优化两个真实科学数据任务中，分别实现57%和58%的偏差降低。

Conclusion: CFM-SD在存在潜在混淆变量的真实科学场景中显著优于现有方法，证明了超越合成基准的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Existing interventional causal discovery methods -- IGSP, DCDI, ENCO -- assume causal sufficiency (no latent confounders) and rely on virtual interventions in synthetic simulators. In AI-for-Science settings such as molecular design and materials science, latent confounders are ubiquitous and real interventions (e.g., physics-based simulations) require hours to days per data point. We propose CFM-SD (Causal Flow Matching with Simulation Data), which uses first-principles physical simulators as do-operators in Pearl's interventional calculus to simultaneously handle latent confounders and real interventional data. Theoretically, $d$-variable causal structure is identifiable with $O(d)$ single-variable interventions -- the minimum under physical realizability constraints. In Intrinsic Evaluation on synthetic data ($γ=0.2$--$0.8$), CFM-SD achieves average F1$=0.800$ vs. F1$=0.127$--$0.562$ for all baselines. In Extrinsic Evaluation on real scientific data, CFM-SD achieves 57--58\% bias reduction in molecular toxicity prediction and battery electrolyte optimization, demonstrating practical value beyond synthetic benchmarks.

</details>


### [40] [LKV: End-to-End Learning of Head-wise Budgets and Token Selection for LLM KV Cache Eviction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06676)
*Enshuai Zhou,Yifan Hao,Chao Wang,Rui Zhang,Di Huang,Jiaming Guo,Xing Hu,Zidong Du,Qi Guo,Yunji Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LKV 将 KV 缓存压缩建模为端到端可微分优化问题，通过 LKV-H 学习任务优化的全局预算分配、LKV-T 学习内在 KV 重要性，在 LongBench 和 RULER 上实现 SOTA 性能，仅保留 15% KV 缓存即可达到近无损表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 KV 缓存压缩方法依赖启发式规则（如基于统计先验的预算分配和基于查询-键交互或注意力 sink 的选择），导致资源错配，无法与任务目标对齐。

Method: 提出 LKV 框架：LKV-H 学习任务优化的全局预算，LKV-T 在不显式构造注意力矩阵的前提下推导内在 KV 重要性，将压缩建模为端到端可微优化。

Result: 在 LongBench 和 RULER 上达到 SOTA；LongBench 上仅保留 15% KV 缓存即实现近无损性能。

Conclusion: 学习型预算分配是保真度的主导因素，数据驱动的分配策略是克服手工启发式局限性的关键。

Abstract: Long-context inference in Large Language Models (LLMs) is bottlenecked by the linear growth of Key-Value (KV) cache memory. Existing KV cache compression paradigms are fundamentally limited by heuristics: heuristic budgeting relies on statistical priors rather than task objectives, causing resource misallocation, while heuristic selection relies on coupled query-key interactions or static inductive biases (e.g., attention sinks). To address this limitation, we introduce LKV (Learned KV Eviction), which formulates KV compression as an end-to-end differentiable optimization problem. LKV integrates LKV-H to learn task-optimized global budgets, and LKV-T to derive intrinsic KV importance without materializing attention matrices. This design bypasses heuristic proxies, strictly aligning compression with task objectives. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that LKV achieves state-of-the-art performance on both LongBench and RULER benchmarks at high compression rates. In particular, on LongBench, LKV achieves near-lossless performance with only 15\% KV cache retention. Crucially, our analysis identifies learned budgeting as the dominant driver of fidelity, demonstrating that data-driven allocation is essential to overcome the limitations of hand-crafted heuristics.

</details>


### [41] [Cost-Ordered Feasibility for Multi-Armed Bandits with Cost Subsidy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07171)
*Ishank Juneja,Carlee Joe-Wong,Osman Yağan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究带成本补贴的多臂老虎机问题（MAB-CS），其中目标是满足未知最优奖励的质量约束下最小化成本。提出了成本有序可行性（COF）算法，通过智能组合各臂样本来评估低成本臂的可行性，并给出了实例相关的上下界分析，在MovieLens和Goodreads数据集上验证了算法的优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典MAB问题追求奖励最大化，但实际应用中常见目标是在满足最小奖励约束下最小化成本，现有研究对奖励约束相对于未知最优值的设定缺乏深入分析。

Method: 提出Cost-Ordered Feasibility (COF)算法，通过智能组合所有臂的样本信息来评估低成本臂是否满足约束，并建立实例相关的累积成本和质量遗憾的上下界。

Result: 推导了实例相关的下界，严格推广了先前界限；证明了COF的上界在理论上有质的改进；在MovieLens和Goodreads数据集及合成实例上，COF相较于基线方法表现出更优的实证性能。

Conclusion: COF算法在理论上具有更好的遗憾上界，同时在实证中展现了令人信服的性能提升，为带成本补贴的MAB问题提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: The classic multi-armed bandit (MAB) problem tackles the challenge of accruing maximum reward while making decisions under uncertainty. However, in applications, often the goal is to minimize cost subject to a constraint on the minimum permissible reward, an objective captured by multi-armed bandits with cost-subsidy (MAB-CS). Of interest to this paper is the setting where the quality (reward) constraint is specified relative to the unknown best reward and the cost of each arm is known. We characterize the expected sub-optimal samples required by any policy by proving instance-dependent lower bounds that offer new insight into the problem and are a strict generalization of prior bounds. Then, we propose an algorithm called Cost-Ordered Feasibility (COF) that leverages our insight and intelligently combine samples from all arms to gauge the feasibility of a cheap arm. Thereafter, we analyze COF to establish instance-dependent upper bounds on its expected cumulative cost and quality regret, i.e., relative to the cheapest feasible arm. Finally, we empirically validate the merits of COF, comparing it to baselines from the literature through extensive simulation experiments on the MovieLens and Goodreads datasets as well as representative synthetic instances. Not only does our paper develop qualitatively better theoretical regret upper bounds, but COF also convincingly demonstrates improved empirical performance.

</details>


### [42] [Breaking the Illusion: When Positive Meets Negative in Multimodal Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06679)
*Yubo Jiang,Yitong An,Xin Yang,Abudukelimu Wuerkaixi,Xuxin Cheng,Fengying Xie,Zhiguo Jiang,Cao Liu,Ke Zeng,Haopeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PND（正负解码）框架，通过对比正路径（增强视觉证据）和负路径（构建反事实抑制语言先验）的解码输出，无需训练即可减少VLM的物体幻觉问题，在POPE、MME、CHAIR上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM频繁出现物体幻觉，过度依赖语言先验而忽视视觉信息，现有方法需要重训练或修改模型结构。

Method: 提出PND训练-free推理框架：1）发现VLM中视觉特征注意力权重不足的问题；2）构建双路径对比解码——正路径放大视觉证据，负路径构建反事实样本惩罚先验主导生成；3）在解码阶段对比两路输出，引导生成更符合视觉事实的结果。

Result: 在POPE、MME、CHAIR三个标准基准上取得SOTA性能，无需任何重训练。

Conclusion: PND通过直接干预解码过程、利用正负对比路径有效解决了VLM的物体幻觉问题，是一种轻量级、无需训练的通用推理框架。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are frequently undermined by object hallucination, generating content that contradicts visual reality, due to an over-reliance on linguistic priors. We introduce Positive-and-Negative Decoding (PND), a training-free inference framework that intervenes directly in the decoding process to enforce visual fidelity. PND is motivated by our finding of an attention imbalance in VLMs, where visual features are under-weighted. Our framework introduces a dual-path contrast: a positive path that amplifies visual evidence and a negative path that constructs counterfactuals to penalize prior-dominant generation. By contrasting outputs from both paths during decoding, PND steers generation toward visually grounded results. Experiments on POPE, MME, and CHAIR demonstrate state-of-the-art performance without retraining.

</details>


### [43] [On the Role of Strain and Vorticity in Numerical Integration Error for Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06680)
*Chenxi Tao,Seung-Kyum Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了流匹配生成模型中速度场的应变率和涡度对积分误差的不同影响，证明应变控制指数级误差放大而涡度仅贡献线性误差，并提出加权雅可比正则化方法，在低NFE下显著降低积分误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配模型通过积分学习到的速度场生成数据，积分步数(NFE)直接影响推理成本。理解速度场中哪些性质主导积分误差，对于设计更高效的生成模型至关重要。

Method: 将速度雅可比矩阵分解为对称部分S(应变率)和反对称部分Ω(涡度)，理论分析两者对积分误差的不同贡献；提出应变权重α和涡度权重β的加权雅可比正则化方法。

Result: 在2D合成数据上验证理论预测，NFE=5时积分误差降低2.7倍；CIFAR-10上轻量级微调在NFE=10时FID提升14%，同时保持高NFE质量。

Conclusion: 应变率通过对数范数控制指数级误差放大，涡度仅贡献线性局部截断误差；最优输运速度场是无旋的且物质导数为零，可实现二阶欧拉精度。

Abstract: Flow matching generates data by integrating a learned velocity field, where the number of integration steps (NFE) directly determines inference cost. We analyze which properties of the velocity field govern integration error by decomposing the velocity Jacobian into its symmetric part S (strain rate) and antisymmetric part Omega (vorticity). We prove that strain and vorticity play different roles: strain controls exponential error amplification through the logarithmic norm, while vorticity contributes only linearly to the local truncation error. We further show that the optimal transport velocity field is irrotational and has zero material derivative, implying second-order Euler accuracy; for exact displacement interpolation, the associated Lagrangian particle dynamics are integrated exactly by Euler. Motivated by this analysis, we study weighted Jacobian regularization with strain weight alpha and vorticity weight beta. Experiments on 2D synthetic data confirm the main theoretical predictions, showing up to 2.7x lower integration error at NFE=5. Preliminary CIFAR-10 experiments show consistent trends, with a lightweight fine-tuning procedure improving FID by 14 percent at NFE=10 while preserving high-NFE quality.

</details>


### [44] [Same Brain, Different Prediction: How Preprocessing Choices Undermine EEG Decoding Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07212)
*Dengzhe Hou,Zihao Wu,Lingyu Jiang,Zirui Li,Fangzhou Lin,Kazunori D. Yamada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现EEG深度学习模型在不同预处理流程下预测结果高度不稳定（最高42%的试次标签翻转），并提出了三种工具：Walsh-Hadamard分解用于高效优化预处理空间、预处理不确定性（PU）作为与模型置信度互补的诊断指标、以及NA-PGI正则化方法作为缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG深度学习模型通常只在单一且未报告的预处理流程下训练和评估，忽略了预处理选择对预测结果的巨大影响，标准不确定性方法因固定预处理流程而无法量化这种变异性。

Method: 1) 将预处理选择形式化为反事实干预空间，通过Walsh-Hadamard分解对2^7种预处理组合进行近可加性分析；2) 提出预处理不确定性（PU）作为逐试次诊断指标；3) 引入归一化自适应PGI（NA-PGI）图结构正则化器，利用预处理的组合结构进行缓解。

Result: 跨6个数据集、4种范式，仅改变预处理流程即可导致高达42%的试次级预测翻转；Walsh-Hadamard分解表明预处理敏感性在二元干预设计下近似可加；PU捕获了与模型置信度互补的不确定性维度。

Conclusion: EEG预测对预处理选择高度敏感，所提出的三种工具可以分别实现这种不稳定性的可测量、可分解和可降低，其中NA-PGI在一定条件范围内可作为有效的缓解策略。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) is a cornerstone of brain-computer interfaces and clinical neuroscience, yet deep learning models are typically trained and evaluated under a single, unreported preprocessing pipeline. We formalize preprocessing choices as a counterfactual intervention space and show that EEG predictions are surprisingly unstable under this space: across six datasets spanning four paradigms, up to 42% of trial-level predictions flip when only the preprocessing changes, a variability that standard uncertainty methods do not explicitly quantify because they condition on a fixed preprocessing pipeline. We provide three tools to make this instability measurable, decomposable, and reducible. First, a Walsh-Hadamard decomposition of the 2^7 pipeline space reveals that sensitivity is near-additive in practice under the binary intervention design, enabling efficient step-by-step optimization. Second, we introduce Preprocessing Uncertainty (PU), a per-trial diagnostic that captures a dimension of instability complementary to model-based confidence. Third, we study Normalized Adaptive PGI (NA-PGI), a graph-structured regularizer that exploits the compositional structure of preprocessing interventions as one mitigation strategy with clear scope conditions.

</details>

*Lorenzo Riccardo Allegrini,Geremia Pompei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种层次化集成流水线，用于多变量遥测数据中的异常检测，在ESA-ADB基准上展示了强大的泛化能力。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决欧洲空间局（ESA）多变量遥测数据中的异常检测问题，特别是检测现实卫星遥测中细微异常的需求。

Method: 集成基于形状特征和统计特征提取、逐通道建模、通道内堆叠以及最终的跨通道聚合，采用时间序列交叉验证和两级掩码策略防止信息泄露。

Result: 在ESA-ADB挑战基准上取得强泛化性能，有效检测到细微异常。

Conclusion: 层次化建模在检测现实卫星遥测中的细微异常方面效果显著，方法具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: A hierarchical ensemble pipeline is introduced to address anomaly detection in multivariate telemetry data provided by European Space Agency (ESA). The method integrates shapelet-based and statistical feature extraction, per-channel modeling, intra-channel stacking, and a final cross-channel aggregation. The pipeline is trained and validated using time-series cross-validation and two-level masking strategies to prevent information leakage. Results on the European Space Agency Anomaly Detection Benchmark (ESA-ADB) challenge demonstrate strong generalization, highlighting the effectiveness of hierarchical modeling in detecting subtle anomalies in realistic satellite telemetry.

</details>


### [46] [Toeplitz MLP Mixers are Low Complexity, Information-Rich Sequence Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06683)
*Benjamin L. Badger,Ethan Roland*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Toeplitz MLP Mixer (TMM)架构，用三角掩码Toeplitz矩阵乘法替代注意力机制，实现O(dn log n)训练时间和O(dn)推理复杂度，在信息保留、复制能力、上下文学习和信息检索方面优于同类亚二次架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer注意力机制的二次时间与空间计算复杂度(O(n²))限制了长序列场景下的效率，需要更高效的亚二次复杂度架构。

Method: 采用三角掩码Toeplitz矩阵乘法替代注意力，在序列维度上进行高效矩阵乘法；该架构无需复杂的输入调制或状态维护，纯粹依赖Toeplitz矩阵的结构化特性实现亚二次复杂度。

Result: TMM在训练效率（单位计算/内存下的损失下降）、复制能力、信息检索和上下文学习基准上优于同类亚二次架构；训练后的因果非可逆Toeplitz层倾向于接近可逆或可逆。

Conclusion: TMM证明了简化的结构化矩阵（Toeplitz）足以在亚二次复杂度下实现强竞争力性能，且缺乏架构偏置有助于保留更多输入信息；算子指标理论分析揭示训练后的Toeplitz层具有趋向可逆性的反直觉特性。

Abstract: Transformer-based large language models are in some respects limited by the quadratic time and space computational complexity of attention. We introduce the Toeplitz MLP Mixer (TMM), a transformer-like architecture that swaps attention for triangular-masked Toeplitz matrix multiplication over the sequence dimension resulting in $\mathcal{O} (dn \log n)$ time and $\mathcal O(dn)$ space complexity during training and $\mathcal O(dn)$ time and space at inference prefill. Despite the lack of sophisticated input modulation or state maintenance present in other sub-quadratic architectures, TMMs yield greater training efficiency in terms of loss achieved per compute and device memory. We demonstrate that TMMs are capable of retaining more input information resulting in improved copying ability, which we argue results from a lack of architectural biases. Consistent with higher input information retention, TMMs exhibit superior information retrieval and in-context learning benchmark accuracy compared to comparable architectures. We conclude with an analysis from the perspective of operator index theory and show that, counterintuitively, trained Toeplitz layers of causal non-invertible models are more likely to be invertible or nearly so than models that are actually invertible over their inputs.

</details>


### [47] [From Canopy to Collision: A Hybrid Predictive Framework for Identifying Risk Factors in Tree-Involved Traffic Crashes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06684)
*Abdul Azim,Ahmed Hossain,Soumyadip Maitra,Panick Kalambay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用2020-2023年CRSS数据库，通过CatBoost机器学习模型、SHAP解释工具和二元逻辑回归的混合框架，分析了树木碰撞事故中伤害严重程度的风险因素。结果表明不系安全带是最强预测因子（未约束乘员严重伤害概率增加近3倍），车辆年龄、超速和驾驶员损伤也有显著影响，且照明条件与车辆年龄、超速与照明等存在关键交互效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 树木碰撞作为路外碰撞的重要子类，常导致高能量冲击下的致命或严重伤害，但目前缺乏系统性的风险因素识别与量化框架，亟需明确关键因素及交互作用以指导安全干预措施。

Method: 采用四步分析流程：(1) CatBoost分类模型识别关键因素；(2) SHAP工具量化边际效应并可视化；(3) 二元逻辑回归验证因素效应；(4) SHAP交互图分析关键因素间的联合效应。数据源为2020-2023年CRSS数据库，伤害严重程度二分类为KA（致命/失能）与BC（非失能/可能伤害）。

Result: 不系安全带是最强预测因子（未约束乘员严重伤害概率增加近3倍，主要由于抛射风险）；车辆年龄、超速和驾驶员损伤显著影响碰撞严重程度；照明条件与车辆年龄、超速与照明条件、安全带使用与车辆年龄、路面与超速之间存在关键交互效应，表现出加性风险特征。

Conclusion: 研究为基于安全系统的针对性干预提供了关键见解，包括加强安全带执法、在低能见度条件下进行速度管理，以及推动车辆 fleet 现代化更新。

Abstract: Tree-involved crashes represent a critical subset of run-off-road (ROR) collisions, often resulting in fatal or severe injuries due to high-energy impacts. This study develops a comprehensive analytical framework to identify and quantify risk factors contributing to crash severity in tree-involved collisions using the Crash Report Sampling System (CRSS) database spanning 2020-2023. The modeling framework follows a multi-step process. First, a machine learning based classification model (CatBoost) identifies key factors associated with binary crash injury severity (KA: fatal or incapacitating injury versus BC: non-incapacitating or possible injury). Second, SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) tool is used to quantify and visualize the marginal effects of top influential factors on crash severity. Third, a binary logistic regression model estimates factor effects and validates SHAP-derived importance measures. Finally, SHAP interaction plots examine the combined effects of key contributing factors. Results reveal restraint non-use as the most influential predictor, with unrestrained occupants nearly three times more likely to experience severe outcomes due to ejection risk. Vehicle age, speeding violations, and driver impairment demonstrate substantial effects, reflecting reduced crashworthiness, increased impact forces, and reduced control capabilities. Critical interactions emerge between lighting conditions and vehicle age, speeding and lighting conditions, restraint use and vehicle age, and road surface and speeding, demonstrating additive risk effects with specific interactions. These findings provide critical insights for targeted safe system-based interventions, including enhanced seat belt enforcement, speed management in reduced visibility conditions, and vehicle fleet modernization.

</details>


### [48] [Robustness of Refugee-Matching Gains to Off-Policy Evaluation Choices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06686)
*Kirk Bansak,Elisabeth Paulson,Dominik Rothenhäusler,Jeremy Ferwerda,Jens Hainmueller,Michael Hotard*

TL;DR: 本文在难民匹配的背景下，使用多种离策略评估方法（如IPW和AIPW）验证了反事实影响评估结果的稳定性，发现估计值在多种场景下保持一致的量级和统计显著性，且与Bansak等人（2018）的原始结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证难民匹配（refugee matching）在提升难民安置结果方面的反事实影响评估结果是否具有稳定性，以增强对相关政策建议的可靠性。

Method: 采用多种离策略评估方法，包括逆概率加权（IPW）和多种增强逆概率加权（AIPW）变体，并结合不同的建模架构和分配程序设置进行比较。

Result: 所有场景下的影响估计值量级一致，且大多数情况下统计显著；结果与Bansak等人（2018）的原始发现一致。

Conclusion: 在难民匹配的反事实评估中，使用多种评估方法得到的结果具有高度稳定性，证实了该领域既往研究结论的可靠性。

Abstract: Previous research has investigated the potential of refugee matching for boosting refugee outcomes, first considered by Bansak et al. (2018). This paper demonstrates the stability of counterfactual impact evaluation results in the context of refugee matching in the United States using a range of off-policy evaluation methods. In order to estimate counterfactual impact and test the robustness of our results, we employ several evaluation methods, including inverse probability weighting (IPW) and multiple variants of augmented inverse probability weighting (AIPW). We also consider various modifications, including alternative modeling architectures and different assignment procedures. The impact estimates remain consistent in magnitude in all scenarios as well as statistically significant in most cases. Furthermore, the estimates are also consistent with the results originally presented in Bansak et al. (2018).

</details>


### [49] [Conformal Agent Error Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06788)
*Naihe Feng,Yi Sui,Shiyi Hou,Ga Wu,Jesse C. Cresswell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于保形预测（CP）的框架，用于多智能体系统（MAS）中的错误归因。该框架专为智能体轨迹等序列数据设计了基于过滤的CP算法，能够预测连续的序列集合，从而实现高效的错误定位和回滚恢复。该方法具有模型无关性，并提供有限样本、无分布假设的覆盖保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统（MAS）在运行失败时，需要在长交互轨迹中准确定位关键错误发生的位置，以便自动回滚恢复。然而，基于大语言模型的MAS生成长序列交互轨迹，使得错误归因成为一个根本性挑战。

Method: 提出基于保形预测（CP）的框架，并引入新的基于过滤的CP算法，专门针对智能体轨迹等序列数据设计。与传统CP算法不同，该方法预测的集合为连续序列，以支持高效的恢复和调试。

Result: 在多种智能体和数据集上验证了理论保证，展示了误差可以被精确隔离，并利用预测集回滚MAS以纠正自身错误。

Conclusion: 该方法具有模型无关性，为MAS错误归因提供了一个原则性的不确定性层。

Abstract: When multi-agent systems (MAS) fail, identifying where the decisive error occurred is the first step for automated recovery to an earlier state. Error attribution remains a fundamental challenge due to the long interaction traces that large language model-based MAS generate. This paper presents a framework for error attribution based on conformal prediction (CP) which provides finite-sample, distribution-free coverage guarantees. We introduce new algorithms for filtration-based CP designed for sequential data such as agent trajectories. Unlike existing CP algorithms, our approach predicts sets that are contiguous sequences to enable efficient recovery and debugging. We verify our theoretical guarantees on a variety of agents and datasets, show that errors can be precisely isolated, then use prediction sets to rollback MAS to correct their own errors. Our overall approach is model-agnostic, and offers a principled uncertainty layer for MAS error attribution. We release code at https://github.com/layer6ai-labs/conformal-agent-error-attribution.

</details>


### [50] [Conditional generation of antibody sequences with classifier-guided germline-absorbing discrete diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06720)
*Justin Sanders,Luca Giancardo,Lan Guo,Yue Zhao,Kemal Sonmez,Nina Cheng,Melih Yilmaz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于种系吸收扩散的抗体序列设计方法，通过将种系序列作为吸收态而非掩码序列，有效缓解了传统蛋白质语言模型中的种系偏差问题，并将非种系残基预测准确率从26%提升至46%，同时在条件生成任务（疏水性和结合亲和度优化）中显著优于EvoProtGrad。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抗体设计方法存在两个关键局限：一是模型主要记忆种系序列而非学习有生物学意义的体细胞变异；二是对基于分类器的条件生成支持有限。

Method: 提出种系吸收扩散（germline absorbing diffusion），将离散扩散噪声过程中的吸收态从掩码序列改为种系序列，使模型仅学习从种系到观测序列的轨迹，排除遗传变异和V(D)J重组统计信息；同时采用离散扩散微调实现可基于任意现成分类器的条件生成。

Result: 非种系残基预测准确率从26%提升至46%，接近真实生物学变异设定的理论上限；在疏水性和结合亲和度条件生成任务中，模型在类别遵守度和样本质量之间的权衡均显著优于EvoProtGrad。

Conclusion: 种系吸收扩散通过引入生物学先验有效缓解种系偏差，在抗体序列的预测和条件生成任务上均取得显著提升，为抗体药物设计提供了更有效的计算方法。

Abstract: Antibody therapeutics are among the most successful modern medicines, yet computationally designing antibodies with desirable binding and developability properties remains challenging. While protein language models (pLMs) have emerged as powerful tools for antibody sequence design, existing approaches largely suffer from two key limitations: they predominantly memorize germline sequences rather than modeling biologically meaningful somatic variation, and they offer limited support for flexible classifier-guided conditional generation. We address these challenges through two primary contributions. First, we demonstrate that discrete diffusion fine-tuning achieves strong language modeling performance on antibody sequences while allowing for generation conditioned on any off-the-shelf classifier. Second, we introduce germline absorbing diffusion, a novel modification of the discrete diffusion noise process in which the germline sequence - rather than a masked sequence - serves as the absorbing state. This biologically motivated inductive bias restricts the model to learning the trajectory from germline to observed sequence, effectively excluding genetic variation and V(D)J recombination statistics from the learned distribution and dramatically mitigating germline bias. We show that germline diffusion improves non-germline residue prediction accuracy from 26 percent to 46 percent, approaching the theoretical upper bound set by true biological variability. We then demonstrate the utility of our germline diffusion model on the conditional generation tasks of sampling antibodies with improved hydrophobicity and predicted binding affinity. On both tasks our model shows an improved tradeoff between class adherence and sample quality, significantly outperforming EvoProtGrad, a popular strategy to sample from pLMs with gradient-based discrete Markov Chain Monte Carlo.

</details>


### [51] [MAGIQ: A Post-Quantum Multi-Agentic AI Governance System with Provable Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06933)
*Sepideh Avizeh,Tushin Mallick,Alina Oprea,Cristina Nita-Rotaru,Reihaneh Safavi-Naini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MAGIQ是一个为多智能体AI系统设计的框架，利用新型高效量子抵抗密码协议实现策略定义与执行，支持智能体间通信策略、访问控制预算、会话级执行及消息归属问责，并通过UC框架证明其安全性与正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前计算生态面临两大变革：自主AI智能体的广泛部署需要安全治理架构以确保智能体遵守通信策略并可被问责；同时量子计算威胁现有密码系统，NIST已建议2030年起逐步淘汰RSA、DH、ECC等公钥算法。亟需为智能体AI系统设计量子抵抗的安全解决方案。

Method: 提出了MAGIQ框架，核心方法包括：(i) 定义丰富的通信与访问控制策略预算，支持一对多智能体会话的全局预算；(ii) 采用后量子密码原语执行策略；(iii) 支持智能体间及一对多会话的策略执行；(iv) 通过消息归属实现智能体对用户的问责。使用通用可组合（UC）框架对系统正确性和安全性进行形式化建模与证明。

Result: 对MAGIQ的计算和通信开销进行了评估，并与当前最先进的智能体AI框架SAGA进行了对比分析。实验表明MAGIQ在提供量子抵抗安全性的同时具有可接受的性能开销。

Conclusion: MAGIQ是迈向后量子安全智能体AI系统的第一步，为多智能体环境中的通信策略执行和问责提供了具有形式化安全保证的解决方案，填补了量子抵抗密码学与自主AI系统交叉领域的研究空白。

Abstract: Our computing ecosystem is being transformed by two emerging paradigms: the increased deployment of agentic AI systems and advancements in quantum computing. With respect to agentic AI systems, one of the most critical problems is creating secure governing architectures that ensure agents follow their owners' communication and interaction policies and can be held accountable for the messages they exchange with other agents. With respect to quantum computing, existing systems must be retrofitted and new cryptographic mechanisms must be designed to ensure long-term security and quantum resistance. In fact, NIST recommends that standard public-key cryptographic algorithms, including RSA, Diffie-Hellman (DH), and elliptic-curve constructions (ECC), be deprecated starting in 2030 and disallowed after 2035.
  In this paper, we present MAGIQ, a framework for policy definition and enforcement in multi-agent AI systems using novel, highly efficient, quantum-resistant cryptographic protocols with proven security guarantees. MAGIQ (i) allows users to define rich communication and access-control policy budgets for agent-to-agent sessions and tasks, including global budgets for one-to-many agent sessions; (ii) enforces such policies using post-quantum cryptographic primitives; (iii) supports session-based enforcement of policies for agent-to-agent and one-to-many agent sessions; and (iv) provides accountability of agents to their users through message attribution. We formally model and prove the correctness and security of the system using the Universal Composability (UC) framework. We evaluate the computation and communication overhead of our framework and compare it with the state-of-the-art agentic AI framework SAGA. MAGIQ is a first step toward post-quantum-secure solutions for agentic AI systems.

</details>


### [52] [Enabling Unsupervised Training of Deep EEG Denoisers With Intelligent Partitioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06724)
*Qiyu Rao,Haozhe Tian,Homayoun Hamedmoghadam,Danilo Mandic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为iPSD的自监督去噪方法，通过智能划分输入EEG片段为独立的噪声实现，无需干净参考信号即可训练深度学习去噪器，在可穿戴EEG（尤其是低信噪比和强伪影条件下）取得了显著优于现有方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴EEG信号中神经活动微弱且与噪声伪影在频谱上重叠，传统信号处理方法依赖固定或启发式规则无法处理时变伪影，而深度学习方法虽表现优异但训练需要无法获取的干净EEG参考信号。

Method: 提出iPSD（Intelligent Partitioning for Self-supervised Denoising）方法，通过智能划分输入EEG片段为具有相同底层信号但噪声相互独立的多个实现，从而实现深度学习去噪器的自监督训练，支持零样本场景。

Result: 在可穿戴EEG（包括耳内传感器）上验证，iPSD在极低信噪比（低至-10 dB）和挑战性伪影（如EMG）条件下达到最先进性能，频谱保真度比竞争基线高出数个数量级。

Conclusion: iPSD通过自监督学习策略有效解决了可穿戴EEG去噪中缺乏干净参考信号的核心难题，尤其在高噪声和复杂伪影场景下展现出显著优势。

Abstract: Denoising wearable electroencephalogram (EEG) is inherently challenging since neural activity is not only subtle but also inseparable from spectrally overlapping noise artifacts. Classical signal processing methods, relying on fixed or heuristic rules, cannot handle the time-varying pervasive artifacts in wearable EEGs. Deep learning methods, on the other hand, show promise in decomposition-free EEG denoising using highly expressive neural networks, but the training requires artifact-free EEG, which is inherently unobtainable. To address this, we propose Intelligent Partitioning for Self-supervised Denoising (iPSD). Our method eliminates the need for clean references by learning to partition an input EEG segment into independent noisy realizations with the same underlying signal. This enables self-supervision of deep learning denoisers, even in zero-shot settings where only a single EEG segment to be denoised is available. We validate iPSD through extensive experiments, including validations on wearable EEG from in-ear sensors. The results show that iPSD achieves state-of-the-art performance, most notably under extremely low signal-to-noise ratios (down to -10 dB) and challenging artifacts (e.g., EMG), with spectral fidelity orders of magnitude higher than competitive baselines.

</details>


### [53] [Learning Material-Aware Hamiltonian Risk Fields for Safe Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07038)
*Aditya Sai Ellendula,Yi Wang,Chandrajit Bajaj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于上下文能量项的路由感知门控机制，使port-Hamiltonian导航策略在局部场景存在低风险可行机动时选择性激活避险自由度，在无安全替代方案时抑制横向力，通过CVaR尾风险目标优化实现风险感知导航的选择性验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有风险感知导航策略缺乏选择性，即当局部场景不存在更安全的可行机动时，策略仍可能暴露避险自由度，导致不安全的幻觉机动。作者希望设计一种具有可证伪选择性签名的导航策略，仅在真正存在低风险可行方向时激活横向规避力，否则予以抑制。

Method: 在port-Hamiltonian导航策略中添加一个上下文能量项，产生学习力通道，其激活与否由局部风险场决定。当存在可行低风险方向时，上下文力朝向该方向激活；当逃生通道被阻挡或不可用时，路由感知门控抑制横向力。使用CVaR（条件风险价值）尾风险目标，使梯度更新聚焦于罕见但后果严重的风险转换。

Result: 在延迟必需逃生基准中，路由感知CVaR将过早力激活从0.950降至0.180（vs DWA），成功率从0.480提升至0.810，零重规划。RELLIS-3D真实越野地形中，正确激活率0.837，错误激活率0.114（vs 标量风险梯度0.378/0.752）。DFC2018语义地图中，灾难性失败从0.60降至0.10，振荡减少90.7%，路径效率不变。高速公路场景中，车道逃生可行时碰撞从100%降至0%；无逃生路径时横向机动被抑制。

Conclusion: 选择性签名源自上下文能量的梯度结构而非训练时调参，证明了该方法的泛化性和理论基础。

Abstract: Risk-aware navigation should be selective: a policy should expose evasive degrees of freedom only when the local scene admits a lower-risk feasible maneuver, and suppress them when no safer alternative exists. We show that adding one context-energy term to a port-Hamiltonian navigation policy produces a learned force channel with exactly this falsifiable signature. When the local risk field contains a feasible lower-risk direction, the induced context force activates toward it; when the apparent escape is blocked or not yet available, a route-aware gate suppresses lateral force rather than hallucinating an unsafe maneuver. A CVaR tail-risk objective focuses gradient updates on rare but consequential risk transitions. We validate the selectivity signature across four settings. In the primary delayed-required-escape benchmark, route-aware CVaR reduces premature force activation from 0.950 to 0.180 versus DWA while raising success from 0.480 to 0.810 with zero replans. On real off-road terrain (RELLIS-3D), route-aware enrichment achieves correct activation rate 0.837 and false activation rate 0.114, compared to 0.378/0.752 for scalar risk gradients. On static semantic maps (DFC2018), enrichment reduces catastrophic failure from 0.60 to 0.10 and oscillation by 90.7% while preserving path efficiency. In highway traffic, collisions drop from 100% to 0% when a lane escape is feasible; when no escape exists, the policy suppresses the lateral maneuver. The selectivity property follows from the gradient structure of the context energy rather than from training-time tuning.

</details>


### [54] [The E$Δ$-MHC-Geo Transformer: Adaptive Geodesic Operations with Guaranteed Orthogonality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06729)
*Arash Shahmansoori*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EΔ-MHC-Geo Transformer架构，通过数据依赖的Cayley旋转实现无条件正交残差连接，并结合Householder反射处理负特征值，在长程稳定性、近π旋转损失和范数保持等指标上显著优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DDL的Householder算子仅在β∈{0,2}时正交，且Cayley变换无法处理特征值-1（反射）情形，需要一种能覆盖O(n)两个连通分量的通用正交残差连接方法。

Method: 提出数据依赖Cayley旋转Q(x)=(I+(β/2)A(x))^{-1}(I-(β/2)A(x))保持全β正交；引入EΔ-MHC-Geo Hybrid通过门控γ(x)融合Cayley旋转与Householder反射H₂；设计中点坍缩正则化器4γ(1-γ)鼓励边界门决策。

Result: 1.79M参数下，长程稳定性超JPmHC 1.9倍、超GPT 3.8倍；近π旋转损失超JPmHC 4.5倍；范数保持均偏差0.001；反射探针余弦对齐0.96；层数减少33%。

Conclusion: Hybrid架构通过同时访问O(n)两个连通分量，克服了JPmHC有限Cayley残差混合器无法实现精确λ=-1算子的局限，在正交性和表达能力上取得更好平衡。

Abstract: We present the E$Δ$-MHC-Geo Transformer, a novel architecture that unifies Manifold-Constrained Hyper-Connections (mHC), Deep Delta Learning (DDL), and the Cayley transform to obtain input-adaptive, unconditionally orthogonal residual connections. Unlike DDL, whose Householder operator is orthogonal only at $β\in \{0,2\}$, our Data-Dependent Cayley rotation $Q(x)=(I+(β/2)A(x))^{-1}(I-(β/2)A(x))$ preserves orthogonality for all $β$ and all inputs. To handle negation, an eigenvalue $-1$ case that Cayley provably excludes, we introduce the E$Δ$-MHC-Geo Hybrid, which combines Cayley rotation with Householder reflection via a learned operator-selection gate $X'=γ(X)Q(X)X+(1-γ(X))H_2(X)X$. A midpoint-collapse regularizer, $4γ(1-γ)$, encourages boundary gate decisions, where each selected component is orthogonal. In matched-parameter comparisons, with approximately 1.79M parameters per model and mean +/- standard deviation over 3 seeds, against four baselines including the concurrent JPmHC, E$Δ$-MHC-Geo achieves the best long-horizon stability, 1.9x over JPmHC and 3.8x over GPT; the best near-$π$ rotation loss, 4.5x over JPmHC on single-plane; strong norm preservation, with 0.001 mean deviation; and 0.96 negation cosine alignment in a diagnostic reflection probe, all with 33% fewer layers. While JPmHC's wider representation excels on pure rotation, its finite Cayley residual mixer excludes an exact $λ=-1$ operator and has no reflection branch, motivating our hybrid approach for accessing both connected components of $O(n)$.

</details>


### [55] [Semantic State Abstraction Interfaces for LLM-Augmented Portfolio Decisions: Multi-Axis News Decomposition and RL Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06730)
*Likhita Yerra,Remi Uttejitha Allam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出语义状态抽象接口（SSAI），一种将稀疏非结构化文本映射到K个可审计命名坐标的方法模板，用于分离顺序决策系统中的表征假设与优化方差。在30只纳斯达克100股票上的实验表明，四因子组合累积收益307.2%（夏普1.067），但统计上脆弱，未通过覆盖率分层控制检验，且弱于PC1合成因子和FinBERT基线。论文定位SSAI为可解释性-性能诊断工具和可复用协议，而非声称其优于密集替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有顺序决策系统在处理稀疏非结构化文本时，难以区分模型性能提升是来源于更好的表征假设还是优化算法（优化方差），需要一个标准化的方法模板来分离和诊断这两类效应。

Method: 提出SSAI框架：将稀疏非结构化文本映射到K个可审计的命名坐标轴（如情感、风险、置信度、波动率预测），在无新闻日设置中性默认值。具体实验设K=4，在30只纳斯达克100股票上使用FNSPID新闻数据（2019-2023），分别评估直接因子组合、监督岭回归预测器和共享相同固定φ的RL智能体（DP-PPO, SAC）。

Result: 四因子组合累积收益307.2% vs 买入持有243.6%，夏普比率1.067，但（1）未通过覆盖率分层控制检验；（2）在≥0.2%交易成本下收益消失；（3）与情感单因子基线相比统计上脆弱；（4）PC1合成因子和FinBERT组合基线在该场景下产生更强的排名信号。

Conclusion: SSAI应被定位为可解释性-性能诊断工具和可复用协议，而非性能更强的模型。其核心价值在于提供了一种标准化方法来分离和诊断稀疏文本驱动决策系统中的表征假设与优化方差效应。

Abstract: We introduce Semantic State Abstraction Interfaces (SSAI): a methodological template for mapping sparse unstructured text into $K$ auditable, named coordinates with neutral defaults on no-news days, designed to separate representation hypotheses from optimisation variance in sequential decision systems. Our contribution is the framework and its evaluation protocol, not a claim that SSAI outperforms denser alternatives.
  We instantiate SSAI with $K=4$ axes (sentiment, risk, confidence, volatility forecast) on a US-equity panel (30 NASDAQ-100 names, FNSPID news, 2019--2023 test), and evaluate it across direct factor portfolios, supervised ridge forecasters, and RL agents (DP-PPO, SAC) that share the same fixed $φ$. The four-factor factor portfolio reaches 307.2% cumulative return and Sharpe 1.067, but apparent gains versus buy-and-hold (243.6%) fail coverage-stratified controls, reverse at $\geq 0.2$% costs, and are statistically fragile versus a sentiment-only baseline; a PC1 composite and a FinBERT portfolio baseline are stronger ranking signals in this setting. Ridge and RL blocks diagnose representation versus optimiser effects. We position SSAI as an interpretability-performance diagnostic and reusable protocol for sparse-text decision systems.

</details>


### [56] [On Training in Imagination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06732)
*Nadav Timor,Ravid Shwartz-Ziv,Micah Goldblum,Yann LeCun,David Harel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于模型的强化学习方法中，在仅使用学习到的动态模型和奖励模型进行策略更新时，误差如何影响回报和策略优化。主要贡献包括：1) 扩展了Asadi等人的分析至含学习奖励模型的MDP，推导了在幂律缩放假设下最小化回报误差边界的最优样本分配（动态样本与奖励样本的比例），并指出更低的Lipschitz常数可以收紧该边界；2) 分析了REINFORCE策略优化对噪声奖励的容忍性，证明零均值奖励噪声不会导致梯度估计偏差，仅增加随 rollout 数量递减的方差项，并由此引出预算约束下的权衡问题：应购买更多廉价但噪声更大的rollout，还是更少但噪声更小的rollout——该问题可简化为一个一维优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于模型的强化学习方法在策略更新时仅依赖学到的动态模型和奖励模型，而不查询真实环境，这种训练范式下模型误差如何影响最终性能尚缺乏系统性的量化分析。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 1) 理论推导：扩展Asadi等人(2018)的分析框架至含学习奖励模型的MDP，推导最优样本分配；2) 理论分析：研究REINFORCE算法在噪声奖励下的梯度估计性质，包括偏差和方差分析；3) 优化建模：将预算约束下的样本分配问题简化为一个一维优化问题。

Result: 1) 推导了在幂律缩放假设下最小化回报误差边界的最优动态-奖励样本分配比例；2) 发现更低的学习动态、奖励和策略的Lipschitz常数可以收紧回报误差边界；3) 证明零均值奖励噪声下REINFORCE梯度估计无偏，方差随rollout数量增加而减少；4) 将预算约束下的样本-噪声权衡简化为一个可求解的一维优化问题。

Conclusion: 本文为基于模型的强化学习中的样本分配和噪声-预算权衡提供了理论指导，揭示了Lipschitz常数作为表征质量指标的重要性，并为实际中如何在计算预算约束下选择rollout的数量和质量提供了可操作的决策框架。

Abstract: State-of-the-art model-based reinforcement learning methods train policies on imagined rollouts. These rollouts are trajectories generated by a learned dynamics model and are scored by a learned reward model, but without querying the true environment during policy updates. We study this training paradigm by quantifying how errors in learned dynamics and reward models affect returns and policy optimization. First, we extend the analysis of Asadi et al. (2018) to MDPs with learned reward models, and derive the optimal sample allocation--the ratio of dynamics samples to reward samples that minimizes a bound on return error under power-law scaling assumptions. We identify lower Lipschitz constants of the learned dynamics, reward, and policy as a representation desideratum that tightens this bound, and we connect this perspective to the temporal-straightening objective of Wang et al. (2026). Second, we examine how policy optimization with REINFORCE tolerates noisy rewards, which are often cheaper to obtain. We show that zero-mean reward noise leaves the gradient estimator unbiased and adds at most a variance term that decreases with the number of rollouts. This introduces a practical tradeoff: given a fixed budget, should one buy more rollouts with cheaper but noisier rewards, or fewer rollouts with more expensive but less noisy rewards? We reduce this choice to a one-dimensional optimization problem and characterize the optimum.

</details>


### [57] [Gated QKAN-FWP: Scalable Quantum-inspired Sequence Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06734)
*Kuo-Chung Peng,Samuel Yen-Chi Chen,Jiun-Cheng Jiang,Chen-Yu Liu,En-Jui Kuo,Yun-Yuan Wang,Prayag Tiwari,Andrea Ceschini,Chi-Sheng Chen,Yu-Chao Hsu,Chun-Hua Lin,Tai-Yue Li,Antonello Rosato,Massimo Panella,Simon See,Saif Al-Kuwari,Kuan-Cheng Chen,Nan-Yow Chen,Hsi-Sheng Goan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出门控QKAN-FWP框架，结合快速权重编程与量子启发式KAN，使用单量子比特数据重上传电路作为非线性激活（DARUAN），引入标量门控更新规则，在时序基准、MiniGrid强化学习和太阳周期预测中表现优异，并在真实量子处理器上验证了NISQ兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子快速权重编程器（QFWPs）依赖多量子比特架构，在NISQ设备上难以扩展且经典模拟成本高昂，需要更轻量、可扩展的量子启发式序列建模方案。

Method: 1) 将FWP与量子启发式KAN结合，使用单量子比特数据重上传电路（DARUAN）作为可学习非线性激活；2) 提出标量门控快速权重更新规则，并给出自适应记忆核、几何有界性和可并行梯度路径的理论分析。

Result: 在528个月输入、132个月预测的长程太阳周期预测任务中，12.5k参数模型在MSE、峰值幅度误差和峰值时序误差上均优于LSTM（25.9k-89.1k）、WaveNet-LSTM（167k）、Vanilla RNN（11.5k）和Modified ESN（132k）；在IonQ和IBM量子处理器上以1024次测量恢复精度，相对MSE偏差小于0.1%。

Conclusion: 门控QKAN-FWP是一种可扩展、参数高效且NISQ兼容的量子启发式序列建模方法。

Abstract: Fast Weight Programmers (FWPs) encode temporal dependencies through dynamically updated parameters rather than recurrent hidden states. Quantum FWPs (QFWPs) extend this idea with variational quantum circuits (VQCs), but existing implementations rely on multi-qubit architectures that are difficult to scale on noisy intermediate-scale quantum (NISQ) devices and expensive to simulate classically. We propose gated QKAN-FWP, a fast-weight framework that integrates FWP with Quantum-inspired Kolmogorov-Arnold Network (QKAN) using single-qubit data re-uploading circuits as learnable nonlinear activation, known as DatA Re-Uploading ActivatioN (DARUAN). We further introduce a scalar-gated fast-weight update rule that stabilizes parameter evolution, supported by a theoretical analysis of its adaptive memory kernel, geometric boundedness, and parallelizable gradient paths. We evaluate the framework across time-series benchmarks, MiniGrid reinforcement learning, and highlight real-world solar cycle forecasting as our main practical result. In the long-horizon setting with 528-month input window and 132-month forecast horizon, our 12.5k-parameter model achieves lower scaled Mean Square Error (MSE), peak amplitude error, and peak timing error than a suite of classical recurrent baselines with up to 13x more parameters, including Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks (25.9k-89.1k parameters), WaveNet-LSTM (167k), Vanilla recurrent neural network (11.5k), and a Modified Echo State Network (132k). To validate NISQ compatibility, we further deploy the trained fast programmer on IonQ and IBM Quantum processors, recovering forecasting accuracy within 0.1% relative MSE of the noiseless simulator at 1024 shots. These results position gated QKAN-FWP as a scalable, parameter-efficient, and NISQ-compatible approach to quantum-inspired sequence modeling.

</details>


### [58] [Geometric Kolmogorov--Arnold Network (GeoKAN)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06740)
*Abhijit Sen,Bikram Keshari Parida,Giridas Maiti,Mahima Arya,Denys I. Bondar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GeoKAN是一种几何感知的KAN变体，通过学习对角黎曼度量对输入坐标进行几何自适应扭曲，从而在非均匀、尖锐、刚性的科学计算和微分方程问题中实现高效逼近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统KAN模型在固定欧几里得输入坐标下进行逼近，缺乏几何适应性，难以有效处理具有尖锐变化、刚性和强非均匀性的科学与微分方程问题。

Method: 学习一个对角黎曼度量来扭曲输入空间，在基展开和特征混合之前对输入进行几何自适应变形；提出了三个主要变体：GeoKAN-NNMetric、GeoKAN-γ和LM-KAN，其中LM-KAN包含RBF、Wav和Fourier三种基函数版本。

Result: 通过拉伸快速变化区域并压缩平滑区域，GeoKAN以任务相关的方式重新分配表示分辨率，将模型容量集中在最需要的地方。

Conclusion: GeoKAN通过几何归纳偏置有效提升了模型对尖锐、刚性、局部化和强非均匀问题的逼近能力，适用于科学机器学习和微分方程问题。

Abstract: We introduce Geometric Kolmogorov--Arnold Networks (GeoKANs), a family of geometry-aware KAN-type models in which approximation is carried out in learned, geometry-adapted coordinates rather than in fixed Euclidean input coordinates. GeoKAN achieves this by learning a diagonal Riemannian metric that warps the input before basis expansion and feature mixing. The learned metric provides a geometric inductive bias through local length scaling and volume distortion, and in physics-informed settings it also affects the differential structure seen by the model. Within this framework, we develop three main variants, namely GeoKAN-NNMetric, GeoKAN-$γ$, and LM-KAN. For LM-KAN, we further consider three basis-specific versions, LM-KAN-RBF, LM-KAN-Wav, and LM-KAN-Fourier. These variants allow us to study geometry-aware KAN models both as general function approximators and as surrogates in physics-informed learning. By stretching regions with rapid variation and compressing smoother regions, GeoKAN reallocates representational resolution in a task-dependent manner, allowing the model to place capacity where it is most needed. As a result, GeoKAN is well suited to sharp, stiff, localized, and strongly non-uniform regimes arising in scientific machine learning and differential-equation problems.

</details>


### [59] [A Closed-Form Upper Bound for Admissible Learning-Rate Steps in Belief-Space Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06741)
*Zixi Li,Youzhen Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现学习率步长并非超参数，而是可由局部置信空间计算得出的公式：在概率单纯形上的投影前向步进模型中，可容许步长的上界由KL/Bregman几何中的压缩性决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上将学习率步长视为超参数需要手动调优，本文试图揭示其背后存在的理论结构，从而将调优口号转化为精确公式。

Method: 将更新建模为概率单纯形上的投影前向步进，利用KL/Bregman几何中的压缩性条件推导可容许步长的上界公式。

Result: 可容许步长的上界不是一个需要经验调优的口号，而是一个可由局部置信空间计算得到的精确公式。

Conclusion: 学习率步长本质上可由理论公式确定，无需作为独立超参数进行调优，这为自适应学习率方法提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Learning-rate steps are usually treated as hyperparameters. This paper isolates a local beliefspace calculation: when an update is modeled as a projected forward step on the probability simplex, admissibility means contractivity in the natural KL/Bregman geometry. Under this model, the upper bound of an admissible step is not a tuning slogan but a formula.

</details>


### [60] [Gradient Extrapolation-Based Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06755)
*Ismam Nur Swapnil,Aranya Saha,Tanvir Ahmed Khan,Mohammad Ariful Haque,Ser-Nam Lim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GXPO提出了一种基于梯度外推的轻量级多步前瞻策略更新规则，仅需三次反向传播即可近似K步优化效果，在数学推理任务上相比GRPO和SFPO分别提升+1.65~5.00和+0.14~1.28个点的pass@1，同时实现最高4倍步数加速和2.33倍墙钟加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准GRPO仅用当前步更新策略，而完整多步前瞻虽能提供更优更新方向但需要大量反向传播，计算成本过高。作者希望以极小的计算开销（仅三次反向传播）近似多步前瞻的收益。

Method: GXPO在活跃阶段仅需三次反向传播：先执行两步快速优化器步，测量梯度变化，基于梯度外推预测虚拟K步前瞻点，将策略参数部分移向该点，然后用新位置的真实梯度做修正更新。当外推信号不稳定时自动回退到标准单步GRPO。整个流程复用同一批rollout、奖励、优势和GRPO损失，无需在新位置重新采样。

Result: 在Qwen2.5和Llama的数学推理实验中，GXPO平均提升pass@1相比GRPO高+1.65~5.00点，相比最强SFPO设置高+0.14~1.28点；活跃阶段计算成本固定为三次反向传播；达到GRPO峰值精度时最高实现4.00倍步数加速、2.33倍墙钟加速和1.33倍反向传播加速。

Conclusion: GXPO是一种即插即用的轻量级策略更新规则，在不增加rollout和奖励计算的前提下，通过梯度外推近似多步前瞻，显著提升GRPO风格推理RL的训练效率和最终性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is widely used to improve the reasoning ability of large language models, especially when answers can be automatically checked. Standard GRPO-style training updates the model using only the current step, while full multi-step lookahead can give a better update direction but is too expensive because it needs many backward passes. We propose Gradient Extrapolation-Based Policy Optimization (GXPO), a plug-compatible policy-update rule for GRPO-style reasoning RL. GXPO approximates a longer local lookahead using only three backward passes during an active phase. It reuses the same batch of rollouts, rewards, advantages, and GRPO loss, so it does not require new rollouts or reward computation at the lookahead points. GXPO takes two fast optimizer steps, measures how the gradients change, predicts a virtual K-step lookahead point, moves the policy partway toward that point, and then applies a corrective update using the true gradient at the new position. When the lookahead signal becomes unstable, GXPO automatically switches back to standard single-pass GRPO. We also give a plain-gradient-descent surrogate analysis that explains when the extrapolation is exact and where its local errors come from. Across Qwen2.5 and Llama math-reasoning experiments, GXPO improves the average sampled pass@1 by +1.65 to +5.00 points over GRPO and by +0.14 to +1.28 points over the strongest SFPO setting, while keeping the active-phase cost fixed at three backward passes. It also achieves up to 4.00x step speedup, 2.33x wall-clock speedup, and 1.33x backward-pass speedup in reaching GRPO's peak accuracy.

</details>


### [61] [Sparse Attention as a Range Searching Problem: Towards an Inference-Efficient Index for KV Cache](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06763)
*Mohsen Dehghankar,Abolfazl Asudeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Louver，一种基于半空间范围搜索的KV缓存索引结构，在稀疏注意力中保证零假阴性（即关键KV条目不会遗漏），兼具理论保证与硬件加速，在准确性和运行时间上优于现有稀疏注意力方法，甚至快于FlashAttention等密集注意力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏注意力方法虽然提升了LLM推理效率，但无法保证零假阴性——即可能遗漏关键KV条目，导致严重精度下降。尤其在长推理任务中，相关token集随解码步骤动态变化，现有方法缺乏对此变化的适应性保证。

Method: 将稀疏注意力重新形式化为半空间范围搜索问题，并设计Louver索引结构。Louver在理论和实践上保证对指定阈值的零假阴性，轻量级集成于现有LLM流水线，并包含CPU和GPU的硬件感知优化。

Result: Louver在准确性和运行时间上均优于现有稀疏注意力方法，且速度快于高度优化的密集注意力（如FlashAttention）。

Conclusion: 召回率保证是稀疏注意力中被忽视的关键维度，Louver开启了构建有理论基础的、高效的KV缓存索引的新方向。

Abstract: Sparse attention improves LLM inference efficiency by selecting a subset of key-value entries, but at the cost of potential accuracy degradation. In particular, omitting critical KV entries can induce substantial errors in model outputs. Existing methods typically operate under fixed or adaptive token budgets and provide empirical robustness or partial theoretical guarantees, yet they do not ensure zero false negatives in decoding steps, particularly since the set of relevant tokens is both query- and step-dependent. Our empirical observations confirm that missing even one critical key can lead to sharp error spikes, especially in long reasoning tasks where the set of important tokens varies throughout decoding. This observation motivates the need for indexing methods that dynamically adapt to these variations across decoding steps while guaranteeing a full recall of the relevant keys above a certain threshold. We address this challenge by reformulating sparse attention as the halfspace range searching problem. However, existing range searching indices are not suitable for modern LLM inference due to their computational and implementation overheads. To overcome this, we introduce Louver, a novel index structure tailored for efficient KV cache retrieval. Louver (i) guarantees zero false negatives with respect to a specified threshold in both theory and practice, (ii) is lightweight to integrate into existing LLM pipelines, and (iii) incorporates hardware-aware optimizations for both CPU and GPU executions. Our experiments demonstrate that Louver outperforms prior sparse attention methods in both accuracy and runtime, and is faster than highly optimized dense attentions such as FlashAttention. These results highlight that recall guarantees are a critical and overlooked dimension of sparse attention, and open a new direction for building theoretically grounded, efficient KV cache indices.

</details>


### [62] [Revisiting Adam for Streaming Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06764)
*Florin Gogianu,Adrian Catalin Lutu,Razvan Pascanu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在完全不使用经验回放或并行采样的纯在线深度强化学习设定下，传统算法（DQN, C51）的表现。发现它们也能表现良好，关键在于优化目标导数有界且权重更新经过方差调整。基于此，提出了Adaptive Q(λ)算法，在55个Atari游戏上超越了现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索无需经验回放或并行采样的纯在线深度强化学习算法，追求更简单、高效和自适应的学习范式，挑战近年来普遍依赖回放缓冲区的做法。

Method: 系统分析在线设定下DQN和C51等已有算法的有效性，研究优化算法（特别是Adam）与更新规则的交互，归纳出两个关键特性：目标函数的导数有界性和权重更新的方差调整。基于此提出Adaptive Q(λ)，结合了资格迹和方差调整技术。

Result: C51在55个Atari游戏中与StreamQ具有竞争力；Adaptive Q(λ)达到双倍人类基线的性能，在所有指标上超越现有方法。

Conclusion: 纯在线深度强化学习是可行的，关键在于确保目标导数有界和方差调整两个特性，Adaptive Q(λ)是这一方向的有效算法。

Abstract: Learning from a sequence of interactions, as soon as observations are perceived and acted upon, without explicitly storing them, holds the promise of simpler, more efficient and adaptive algorithms. For over a decade, however, deep reinforcement learning walked the contrary path, augmenting agents with replay buffers or parallel sampling routines, in an effort to tame learning instability. Recently, this topic has been revisited by Elsayed et al. (2024), focusing on update computation through eligibility traces and modifications to the optimisation routine, resulting in the StreamQ algorithm. In this work we take a step back, investigating the efficacy of established updates, such as those implemented by DQN and C51 within this online setting. Not only do we find that they perform well, but through analysing how the optimisation algorithm generally, and Adam in particular, interacts with these updates, we contend that two properties are essential for robust performance: i) the derivative of the objective is to be bounded and ii) weight updates are variance-adjusted. Rigorous and exhaustive experimentation demonstrates that C51, which exhibits both characteristics, is competitive with StreamQ across a subset of 55 Atari games. Using these insights, we derive a variance-adjusted algorithm based on eligibility traces, termed Adaptive Q$(λ)$, which approaches double the human baseline on the same subset, surpassing existing methods by all performance metrics.

</details>


### [63] [Distributional Process Reward Models: Calibrated Prediction of Future Rewards via Conditional Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06785)
*Rachel Ma,Dylan Hadfield-Menell,Kristjan Greenewald*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将条件最优输运（CondOT）应用于过程奖励模型（PRM）的校准，通过学习单调条件分位数函数，显著改善了PRM在数学推理任务上的校准质量，并在Best-of-N推理时扩展中提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的过程奖励模型（PRM）在校准方面存在严重不足，往往高估成功概率，导致推理时扩展方法的可靠性下降。因此需要一种能提供结构有效且灵活不确定性估计的校准方法。

Method: 提出使用条件最优输运（Conditional Optimal Transport）来校准PRM。修改了Bunne等人提出的CondOT地图学习方法，用于估计PRM隐藏状态条件下、成功概率的单调条件分位数函数，从而得到结构有效的分位数估计，并能高效提取任意置信水平的置信区间，最后将其集成到实例自适应扩展（IAS）框架中。

Result: 在MATH-500（中等难度）和AIME（困难、分布外）数学推理基准上评估。对于排名信号可靠的PRM，该方法在校准上显著优于未校准的PRM和分位数回归。在下游Best-of-N IAS任务中，该方法整体上优于未校准的PRM。

Conclusion: 条件最优输运为PRM校准提供了一种有原则且实用的新方法，具有结构性保证和灵活的不确定性估计能力，是PRM校准的另一种有效途径。

Abstract: Inference-time scaling methods rely on Process Reward Models (PRMs), which are often poorly calibrated and overestimate success probabilities. We propose, to our knowledge, the first use of conditional optimal transport for calibrating PRMs, modifying conditional OT (CondOT) map learning \cite{bunne2022supervised} to estimate a monotonic conditional quantile function over success probabilities estimated by the PRM, conditioned on PRM hidden states. This yields structurally valid quantile estimates and enables efficient extraction of confidence bounds at arbitrary levels, which we integrate into the instance-adaptive scaling (IAS) framework of \cite{park2025know}. We evaluate on mathematical reasoning benchmarks spanning moderate-difficulty problems (MATH-500) and harder out-of-distribution problems (AIME). For PRMs with reliable ranking signals, our method substantially improves calibration over both uncalibrated PRMs and quantile regression. On downstream Best-of-N IAS performance, our method generally improves over uncalibrated PRMs. These results establish conditional optimal transport as another principled and practical approach to PRM calibration, offering structural guarantees and flexible uncertainty estimation.

</details>


### [64] [MIND: Monge Inception Distance for Generative Models Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06797)
*Quentin Berthet,Yu-Han Wu,Clement Crepy,Romuald Elie,Klaus Greff,Michael Eli Sander*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Monge Inception Distance (MIND)指标，利用 sliced Wasserstein 距离替代 FID 的 Fréchet 距离，在样本效率、计算速度和对抗鲁棒性上显著优于 FID。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FID存在样本复杂度差、易受对抗攻击（如矩匹配）等局限性，需要更高效、鲁棒且计算快速的生成模型评估指标。

Method: 使用 sliced Wasserstein 距离，通过排序高效计算一维最优传输距离的平均值，避免估计高维均值和协方差矩阵。

Result: MIND在样本效率上提升一个数量级（5k样本可替代FID的50k），计算速度快两个数量级，对矩匹配攻击具有更强鲁棒性，且与小样本（1k/2k）下仍保持高信息量。

Conclusion: MIND可作为FID的有效替代方案，特别适用于样本有限或需要快速迭代的场景，同时保持与标准基准的高度相关性。

Abstract: We propose the Monge Inception Distance (MIND), a metric for evaluating generative models that addresses key limitations of the widely adopted Fréchet Inception Distance (FID). The MIND metric leverages the sliced Wasserstein distance to compare distributions by averaging one-dimensional optimal transport distances, efficiently computed via sorting. This approach circumvents the estimation of high-dimensional means and covariance matrices, which underlie FID's poor sample complexity and vulnerability to adversarial attacks. We empirically demonstrate three primary advantages: (i) it is more sample-efficient by one order of magnitude, (ii) it is faster to compute by two orders of magnitude, (iii) it is more robust to adversarial attacks such as moment-matching. We show that MIND with 5k samples can replace the evaluation performance of FID with 50k samples, providing high correlation with this standard benchmark and superior discriminative performance. We further demonstrate that even smaller sample sizes (e.g., 1k or 2k) remain highly informative for rapid model iteration.

</details>


### [65] [From Model to Data (M2D): Shifting Complexity from GNNs to Graphs for Transparent Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06814)
*Debolina Halder Lina,Arlei Silva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Model-to-Data (M2D)蒸馏框架，通过将模型复杂度转移到数据空间（增强图结构和特征），使简单学生模型能够匹配复杂教师模型的性能，从而提升GNN的可解释性和透明度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN可解释性方法仅能归因个体预测，无法提供架构层面的透明度，也无法解释简单模型与复杂模型之间的性能差距。

Method: M2D蒸馏：将教师模型的行为和复杂度蒸馏到增强图中（包含丰富特征和结构），使简单学生模型通过学习该增强数据来匹配教师性能。

Result: M2D能够以可解释的方式揭示注意力聚合机制和公平性目标等底层机制，在保持性能的同时增强GNN透明度。

Conclusion: 通过将模型行为物化到数据空间，M2D使人类能够直接检视架构优势，弥合了模型复杂度与人类可理解性之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) achieve high performance but can be opaque to humans, making it difficult to understand and compare the many proposed architectures. While existing explainability methods attribute individual predictions to nodes, edges, or features, they do not provide architectural transparency or explain the fundamental performance gap between simple and more complex models. To address this limitation, we introduce Model-to-Data (M2D) distillation, a new framework that increases transparency by transferring model complexity into the data space. M2D distills the teacher model into an augmented graph with enriched features and structure, enabling a simple student to match the teacher's performance. By materializing model behavior in the data, our approach allows humans to inspect architectural advantages directly. We show that M2D reveals underlying mechanisms such as fairness objectives and attention-based aggregation in an interpretable way, enhancing GNN transparency while preserving performance.

</details>


### [66] [CellScientist: Dual-Space Hierarchical Orchestration for Closed-Loop Refinement of Virtual Cell Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07335)
*Mengran Li,Bo Li,Jiaying Wang,Wenbin Xing,Yixuan Dong,Chengyang Zhang,Hongliang Zhang,Yuzhong Peng,Jinlin Wu,Bob Zhang,Bingo Wing-Kuen Ling,Fuji Yang,Zhen Lei,Jiebo Luo,Zelin Zang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CellScientist框架，通过双空间分层架构（假设空间与执行空间）解决虚拟细胞建模中的精炼-路由问题，将执行失败的结构化信号反馈到假设或实现层面进行定向修正，实现可审计的闭环优化流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM辅助建模工作流存在精炼-路由问题：预测偏差在可执行实现中被观察到，但相关修正可能涉及建模假设、表征设计、实现或任务约束等多个层面，缺乏结构化的反馈传播机制，导致迭代精炼可能只修复代码而忽略根本假设错误。

Method: 提出CellScientist双空间分层框架，包括高层假设空间和低层可执行实现空间；将建模决策表示为结构化状态，在任务和接口约束下实现为可接受程序；将执行偏差路由回目标假设或实现更新，形成假设→实现→假设的闭环。

Result: 在形态学和转录组学基准测试以及单细胞扰动评估中，CellScientist选择的最终可执行模型在固定划分和评估协议下优于参考基线，同时产生可审计的精炼追踪记录。

Conclusion: CellScientist通过结构化反馈传播机制，将模型失败从调试事件转变为定向精炼的结构化信号，实现了更有效的虚拟细胞建模闭环优化。

Abstract: Virtual Cell Modeling (VCM) requires models that not only predict perturbation responses, but also support targeted revision when predictions fail. Current LLM-assisted modeling workflows face a refinement-routing problem: prediction discrepancies are observed through executable implementations, but the relevant revision may involve the modeling assumption, representation design, implementation, or task constraint. Without structured feedback propagation across these levels, iterative refinement may repair code while failing to revise the assumption responsible for the discrepancy. We propose CellScientist, a dual-space hierarchical framework that couples a high-level hypothesis space with a low-level executable implementation space. CellScientist represents modeling decisions as structured states, realizes them as admissible programs under task and interface constraints, and routes execution discrepancies back to targeted hypothesis or implementation updates. This enables a closed Hypothesis -> Implementation -> Hypothesis loop where failures become structured signals for model refinement rather than debugging events. Across morphology and transcriptomic benchmarks, with additional single-cell perturbation evaluations, the final executable models selected by CellScientist improve over reference baselines under fixed split and evaluation protocols, while the workflow produces auditable refinement traces.

</details>


### [67] [A Theory of Online Learning with Autoregressive Chain-of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06819)
*Ilan Doron-Arad,Idan Mehalel,Elchanan Mossel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了自回归生成的在线学习框架，区分两种反馈模式（端到端与思维链），证明在端到端模型中错误界对生成步长M的依赖呈常数到对数增长，而思维链模型可完全消除对M的依赖。同时分析了线性阈值类并解决了前人遗留问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归生成是大语言模型的核心机制，但对其输入-输出映射的可学习性缺乏在线学习视角的理解。本文旨在建立在线类比框架，探究学习错误界如何依赖于生成步长M，以及中间token的观测能否降低这种依赖。

Method: 1. 提出在线自回归学习框架，区分端到端（仅观测最终token）和思维链（观测完整M步轨迹）两种反馈模型。2. 理论分析两种模型下的错误界增长速率。3. 针对线性阈值类进行具体分析并证明最优错误界。

Result: 端到端模型中错误界对M的依赖呈常数到对数增长，且对数上界不可避免；思维链模型中完整轨迹的观测可完全消除对M的依赖。同时获得了线性阈值类的最优错误界及统计设置下的新下界。

Conclusion: 在线自回归学习展现了与统计设置定性相似但定量尺度不同的图景，思维链反馈的优势在于可完全消除生成步长对可学习性的影响。

Abstract: Autoregressive generation lies at the heart of the mechanism of large language models. It can be viewed as the repeated application of a next-token generator: starting from an input string (prompt), the generator is applied for $M$ steps, and the last generated token is taken as the final output. [Joshi et al., 2025] proposed a PAC model for studying the learnability of the input-output maps arising from this process. We develop an online analogue of this framework, focusing on the mistake bound of learning the final output induced by an unknown next-token generator.
  We distinguish between two forms of feedback. In the End-to-End model, after each round the learner observes only the final token produced after $M$ autoregressive steps. In the Chain-of-Thought model, the learner is additionally shown the entire $M$-step trajectory. Our goal is to understand how the optimal mistake bound depends on the generation horizon $M$, and to what extent observing intermediate tokens can reduce this dependence.
  Our main results show that the online theory of autoregressive learning exhibits a qualitative picture analogous to the statistical one found by [Hanneke et al., 2026], but with a different scale of dependence on the generation horizon. In the End-to-End model, we prove a taxonomy of possible mistake-bound growth rates in the generation horizon $M$: essentially any rate between constant and logarithmic can arise. We further show that this logarithmic ceiling is unavoidable. In the Chain-of-Thought model, we show that access to the full generated trajectory eliminates the dependence on $M$ altogether.
  We also analyze autoregressive linear threshold classes, and prove optimal mistake bounds, as well as a new lower bound for the statistical setting. Along the way, our results resolve several questions left open by [Joshi et al., 2025].

</details>


### [68] [Mage: Multi-Axis Evaluation of LLM-Generated Executable Game Scenes Beyond Compile-Pass Rate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07342)
*Hugh Xuechen Liu,Kıvanç Tatar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 编译通过率在多组件领域特定工件生成中是误导性指标，本文通过四轴评估协议Mage证明其与功能正确性反相关，结构IR条件化可恢复领域忠诚结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编译通过率作为LLM代码生成的主流评估信号，在多组件领域特定工件（如可执行游戏场景）中可能具有误导性，现有评估缺乏多维度视角。

Method: 提出四轴评估协议Mage（编译成功、运行时成功、结构保真度、机制遵循度），对4个开源LLM（7B-30B）进行858次生成尝试，对比直接NL-to-C#生成与结构IR条件化生成，并测试两种IR粒度水平。

Result: 直接NL-to-C#生成运行时通过率最高（平均43%），但结构空洞（机制F1≈0.12）；结构IR条件化使运行时通过率减半，但恢复领域忠诚结构（F1最高1.00）；两种IR粒度在统计上无显著差异（McNemar p=1.0），表明输入粒度饱和。

Conclusion: 编译率与功能正确性在该领域存在反相关，多轴评估对检测这种背离至关重要，应避免单一依赖编译通过率指标。

Abstract: Compile-pass rate is the dominant evaluation signal for LLM code generation, yet for multi-component domain-specific artifacts it can be actively misleading. We demonstrate this on executable game scene synthesis with a four-axis evaluation protocol (named `Mage') -- compile success, runtime success, structural fidelity, and mechanism adherence -- applied to 858 generation attempts across four open-weight LLMs (7B--30B), 26~hand-crafted Unity goal pattern playable concepts, and two automatically extracted IR granularity levels. Direct NL-to-C\# generation achieves the highest runtime-pass rate (43\% mean) yet produces structurally vacuous scenes (mechanism $F_1 \approx 0.12$). Structural IR conditioning halves the runtime rate but recovers domain-faithful structure ($F_1$ up to 1.00). Within IR conditioning, behavior-only and full-scene granularity are statistically indistinguishable (McNemar $p = 1.0$), indicating input-level granularity saturation. These results show that compile rate is anti-correlated with functional correctness in this domain and that multi-axis evaluation is necessary to detect the divergence. We release the benchmark, replay logs, and per-record metrics for independent verification.

</details>


### [69] [A Rod Flow Model for Adam at the Edge of Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06821)
*Eric Regis,Sinho Chewi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将"rod flow"模型从梯度下降扩展到Adam等自适应优化方法，在参数和动量联合相空间中对优化器进行建模，并在多种架构上验证了其比传统稳定流更准确地追踪离散迭代在稳定边缘的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应梯度方法（如Adam）在稳定边缘运行，但现有连续时间模型主要针对梯度下降，缺乏对动量方法的扩展。

Method: 将rod flow扩展到Adam，在联合相空间(w,m)中建模，将二阶矩ν作为平滑辅助变量；同时为heavy ball动量、Nesterov动量以及RMSProp、Adam、NAdam的标量和逐分量版本开发对应的rod flow。

Result: 在代表性机器学习架构上，rod flow比对应的稳定流更准确地追踪了所有八种优化器的离散迭代在稳定边缘区域的行为。

Conclusion: Rod flow是一种有效的框架，能够将边缘稳定性分析从梯度下降推广到更复杂的自适应和动量优化方法。

Abstract: Cohen et al. (arXiv:2207.14484) observed that adaptive gradient methods such as Adam operate at the edge of stability. While there has been significant work on continuous-time modeling of gradient descent at the edge of stability, extending these models to momentum methods remains underdeveloped. In the gradient descent setting, Regis et al. (arXiv:2602.01480) introduced rod flow, which models consecutive iterates as an extended one-dimensional object -- a "rod." Here we extend rod flow to Adam by working in the joint phase space of parameters and first moment $(w, m)$ and treating the second moment $ν$ as a smooth auxiliary variable. We also develop rod flows for heavy ball momentum, Nesterov momentum, and scalar and per-component versions of RMSProp, Adam, and NAdam. For all eight optimizers, we empirically evaluate rod flow on representative machine learning architectures, where it tracks the discrete iterates through the edge-of-stability regime significantly more accurately than the corresponding stable flow.

</details>


### [70] [SHARP: A Self-Evolving Human-Auditable Rubric Policy for Financial Trading Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06822)
*Xiwen Chen,Wenhui Zhu,Songzhu Zheng,Kashif Rasul,Yueyue Deng,Huayu Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SHARP框架，一种神经符号方法，用结构化、可审计的规则策略替代无约束的提示优化，解决LLM在金融交易中的信用分配问题和策略漂移，在多个股票领域和LLM骨干上将小型模型性能提升10-20个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自改进智能体通过无界自由形式提示优化来适应金融市场，但在低信噪比和延迟标量回报环境下，这种非结构化方法加剧了信用分配问题——优化器无法可靠区分系统性逻辑缺陷与随机市场波动，最终导致策略漂移。

Method: SHARP是一种神经符号框架，将智能体推理约束在边界明确、人类可读的条件-动作规则形式中。当次优交易发生时，归因智能体通过跨样本推理定位具体规则失效，执行原子化策略编辑，并通过严格的滚动前向验证进行正则化。

Result: 在三个不同股票板块和四种LLM骨干上评估，SHARP一致地将通用初始启发式策略转化为高度鲁棒的交易策略，将紧凑型模型（如GPT-4o-mini）的实证性能平均提升10-20个百分点。

Conclusion: SHARP证明LLM能够在实现动态高效适应的同时，显著增强机构金融所要求的结构透明性和可审计性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed for autonomous financial trading, a domain requiring continuous adaptation to noisy, non-stationary markets. Existing self-improving agents typically address this through unbounded free-form prompt optimization. However, in low signal-to-noise environments with delayed scalar rewards (P\&L), this unstructured approach exacerbates the fundamental credit assignment problem: optimizers cannot reliably distinguish systematic logic flaws from stochastic market variance, inevitably leading to policy drift. To overcome this bottleneck, we introduce the Self-Evolving Human-Auditable Rubric Policy (SHARP), a neuro-symbolic framework that replaces unconstrained text mutation with structured, symbolic policy optimization. SHARP confines the agent's reasoning to a bounded, human-readable rubric of explicit condition-action rules. When sub-optimal trades occur, an attribution agent employs cross-sample reasoning across multiple samples to isolate specific rule failures. This enables targeted, atomic policy edits that are subsequently regularized through strict walk-forward validation. Evaluated across three diverse equity sectors and four LLM backbones, SHARP consistently transforms generic initial heuristics into highly robust strategies, lifting the empirical performance of compact models by 10 to 20 percentage points on average (e.g., GPT-4o-mini). Ultimately, SHARP demonstrates that LLMs can achieve dynamic and efficient adaptation while significantly enhancing the structural transparency and auditability demanded by institutional finance.

</details>


### [71] [Why DDIM Hallucinates More than DDPM: A Theoretical Analysis of Reverse Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06831)
*Muhammad H. Ashiq,Samanyu Arora,Abhinav N. Harish,Ishaan Kharbanda,Hung Yun Tseng,Grigorios G. Chrysos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上研究了DDPM（随机性）和DDIM（确定性）两种扩散采样器在高斯混合目标下的幻觉现象，证明在关键时刻τ之后，DDIM会卡在两个最近模式之间的连接段上产生幻觉，而DDPM的随机性帮助其摆脱该区域从而避免幻觉。实验验证了DDPM幻觉率更低，并展示了通过增加随机步骤可帮助DDIM避免幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从理论上理解扩散模型中确定性采样（DDIM）和随机性采样（DDPM）产生幻觉的机制差异，为设计更好的采样器提供理论基础。

Method: 以高斯混合分布为目标，分别分析DDPM（反向SDE）和DDIM（反向ODE）的动力学行为，证明DDIM在关键时刻后会被困在两个最近模式之间的连接段上，而DDPM的随机性使其能够逃离该区域。

Result: DDPM的幻觉率显著低于DDIM；增加随机步骤可帮助DDIM避免幻觉。

Conclusion: 扩散采样器的随机性是避免幻觉的关键因素，为设计改进的采样器提供了新思路。

Abstract: We theoretically study the hallucination phenomena in two canonical diffusion samplers: the stochastic Denoising Diffusion Probabilistic Model (DDPM) and the deterministic Denoising Diffusion Implicit Model (DDIM). We analyze the reverse ODE (DDIM) and SDE (DDPM) for a Gaussian mixture target, proving that after a critical time $τ$, (a) DDIM can become stuck on the segment connecting the two nearest modes and (b) DDPM *stochasticity* helps it become unstuck from this region, thus avoiding hallucination. Our empirical validation verifies that DDPM has a significantly lower hallucination rate than DDIM when this region is entered. Building on our observations, we exhibit how using additional stochastic steps can help DDIM avoid hallucinations and offer new insights on how to design improved samplers.

</details>


### [72] [On Privacy Leakage in Tabular Diffusion Models: Influential Factors, Attacker Knowledge, and Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06835)
*Masoumeh Shafieinejad,D. B. Emerson,Behnoosh Zamanlooy,Elaheh Bassak,Fatemeh Tavakoli,Sara Kodeiri,Marcelo Lotif,Xi He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用最先进的成员推断攻击，系统量化了表格扩散模型在不同设置下的隐私泄露风险，并揭示了攻击者无需完美知识即可成功攻击，同时指出距离最近记录等启发式隐私度量的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据在隐私敏感领域应用广泛，对高质量合成数据的需求日益增长，但随之而来的隐私风险需要被理解和量化。

Method: 采用最先进的成员推断攻击，在黑盒和白盒两种设置下，系统评估训练配置、合成选择及攻击者知识对隐私泄露的影响。

Result: 攻击者无需完美的训练知识、相同的分布或大量计算资源即可发动成功攻击；启发式隐私度量（如距离最近记录）存在缺陷。

Conclusion: 表格扩散模型的隐私风险需要更严谨的评估方法，不应依赖简单的启发式度量，且攻击者的知识要求低于预期。

Abstract: Tabular data plays an important role in many fields and industries, including those with elevated privacy considerations and risks. As such, there is a rising interest in generating high-quality synthetic proxies for real tabular data as a means of reducing privacy risk and proprietary data exposure. With tabular diffusion models (TDMs) demonstrating leading performance in synthesizing such data, understanding and measuring the privacy risks associated with these models is imperative. Leveraging state-of-the-art membership inference attacks for TDMs in both black- and white-box settings, this work quantifies the impact of training setup, synthesis choices, and attacker knowledge on privacy leakage. Moreover, the results demonstrate that adversaries need not have perfect knowledge of the training setup, identical data distributions, or massive compute resources to construct successful attacks. Finally, the pitfalls associated with applying heuristic privacy metrics, such as distance-to-closest record, are revealed.

</details>


### [73] [How to Compress KV Cache in RL Post-Training? Shadow Mask Distillation for Memory-Efficient Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06850)
*Rui Zhu,Weiheng Bai,Qiushi Wu,Yang Ren,Haixu Tang,Yuchu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在线强化学习在长上下文推理任务中面临KV缓存导致的内存瓶颈，而压缩KV缓存虽能缓解内存压力却引发严重的离策略偏差，现有统计方法难以有效纠正这一偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在线RL在长上下文推理场景中因KV缓存过大导致的内存墙问题，并纠正KV缓存压缩所引入的离策略偏差。

Method: 分析KV缓存压缩在RL rollout阶段引入的离策略偏差机制（稀疏上下文采样 vs. 稠密上下文更新），并指出现有重要性重加权等统计方法的局限性。

Result: KV缓存压缩在标准推理中近乎无损，但在RL优化中微小近似误差被急剧放大；重要性重加权方法存在高梯度方差和低样本效率问题。

Conclusion: 需要新的方法来解决KV缓存压缩带来的离策略偏差，现有统计校正方法不足以应对RL优化中的偏差放大问题。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has emerged as a crucial paradigm for unlocking the advanced reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs), encompassing frameworks like RLHF and RLAIF. Regardless of the specific optimization algorithm (e.g., PPO, GRPO, or Online DPO), online RL inherently requires an exploratory trajectory generation (rollout) phase. However, for long-context reasoning tasks, this rollout phase imposes a severe ``memory wall'' due to the exorbitant Key-Value (KV) cache footprint. While applying KV cache compression during rollouts mitigates this memory overhead, it induces a critical off-policy bias. Although modern KV compression is often nearly lossless during standard inference, even minuscule approximation errors are drastically amplified by the inherent instability of RL optimization. Specifically, the sampler generates responses under a sparse context, whereas the learner updates parameters using the full, dense context. Existing statistical solutions, such as importance reweighting, struggle to correct this magnified bias, suffering from high gradient variance and severe sample inefficiency.

</details>


### [74] [Christoffel-DPS: Optimal sensor placement in diffusion posterior sampling for arbitrary distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06861)
*James Rowbottom,Nick Huang,Carola-Bibiane Schönlieb,Ben Adcock*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Christoffel函数的无分布传感器放置框架（Christoffel-DPS），用于生成模型（如扩散后验采样）中的最优传感器放置，在低传感器预算下优于传统高斯方法和现有生成模型放置方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典最优传感器放置（OSP）方法依赖高斯假设，无法处理现实系统中复杂分布；现有传感器选择方法与现代生成式重建模型存在不匹配，亟需与强大恢复模型相匹配的新型OSP策略。

Method: 基于Christoffel函数构建无分布传感器放置框架，推导出具有非渐近恢复界的新型OSP策略；开发Christoffel-DPS（包括离线和在线变体），将Christoffel采样实例化到生成模型中，模型无关且适用于无条件和流匹配模型。

Result: Christoffel-DPS在低传感器预算下，在结构化非高斯基准上优于高斯OSP基线和现有生成模型放置方法，验证了无分布传感在理论和实践上的优越性。

Conclusion: 无分布传感框架具有理论原则性和实际优越性，为生成模型中的传感器放置提供了新范式。

Abstract: State estimation is a critical task in scientific, engineering and control applications. Since the reliability of reconstructions depends on the number and position of sensors, optimal sensor placement (OSP) is essential in scenarios where measurements are sparse and expensive. Classical OSP approaches rely on Gaussian assumptions and are consequently unable to account for the complex distributions encountered in many real-world systems. Generative-model-based reconstruction using sensor guided diffusion posterior sampling (DPS) has emerged as a promising technique for reconstructing states from highly complex distributions. However, existing sensor-selection methods either require unrealistically many sensors or emulate classical OSP, creating a mismatch between modern recovery models with classical OSP tools motivating the need for fundamentally new ideas towards OSP that match the recent advances made in powerful recovery models. We introduce a distribution-free sensor placement framework based on the Christoffel function: a mathematical formulation of optimal sampling and recovery guarantees for posterior sampling with arbitrary sensors and signal distributions, from which we derive a new OSP strategy with non-asymptotic bounds on the number of sensors needed for recovery. We develop Christoffel-DPS, with offline and online variants, instantiating Christoffel sampling for generative models. Christoffel-DPS outperforms Gaussian OSP baselines and existing generative-model placement methods, validating that distribution-free sensing is both theoretically principled and practically superior.
