The framework is model-agnostic; we demonstrate its application to a range of unconditional DPS and flow-matching models on structurally non-Gaussian benchmarks, showing the efficacy of Christoffel-DPS in low sensor budget regimes.

</details>


### [75] [Less Random, More Private: What is the Optimal Subsampling Scheme for DP-SGD?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07072)
*Andy Dong,Ayfer Özgür*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明，在差分隐私机器学习中，结构化采样方案（平衡迭代子采样，BIS）比传统的泊松子采样能实现更强的隐私放大效果，在低噪声区域可将噪声乘数降低高达9.6%，推翻了“更多随机性意味着更强隐私”的传统认知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 泊松子采样虽因无结构随机性便于隐私放大分析，但其引入的参与方差导致每个样本在不同训练迭代中出现次数差异巨大，阻碍了最优隐私放大的实现。作者旨在揭示这一方差是隐私放大的根本性次优来源，并寻找更优的替代方案。

Method: 提出平衡迭代子采样（BIS），每个样本在固定次数迭代中恰好参与一次；进行渐近隐私放大分析，证明BIS在噪声谱两端（σ→0和σ→∞）达到最优；引入近精确蒙特卡洛核算方法消除RDP和PLD分析中的解析松弛。

Result: 在60多种DP-SGD配置下，BIS在低噪声区域（高效用私有训练最相关）一致优于泊松子采样，噪声乘数降低高达9.6%。

Conclusion: 在DP-SGD中，结构化参与（BIS）比随机性更强的泊松子采样既更实用又更隐私，推翻了“更多采样随机性必然带来更强隐私放大”的直觉。

Abstract: Poisson subsampling is the default sampling scheme in differentially private machine learning, largely because its unstructured randomness yields tractable privacy amplification analyses. Yet this same randomness introduces substantial participation variance: each sample appears in very different numbers of training iterations. In this work, we show that this variance is not merely a practical artifact to be tolerated, but a fundamental source of suboptimal privacy amplification. We prove that Balanced Iteration Subsampling (BIS), a structured scheme in which each sample participates in exactly a fixed number of iterations, achieves stronger privacy amplification than Poisson subsampling and is optimal at both extremes of the noise spectrum ($σ\to 0$ and $σ\to \infty$). Our analysis reveals that the privacy-noise tradeoff is governed not by maximizing randomness, but by eliminating participation variance while preserving uniform marginal participation across iterations. To translate this asymptotic theory into finite-noise guarantees, we introduce a practical near-exact Monte Carlo accountant for BIS, which removes the analytical slack of existing RDP and composition-based PLD analyses. Evaluations across more than 60 practical DP-SGD configurations show that BIS consistently outperforms Poisson subsampling in the low-noise regimes most relevant for high-utility private training, reducing the required noise multiplier by up to $9.6\%$. These results overturn the common intuition that more sampling randomness necessarily yields stronger privacy amplification: in DP-SGD, structured participation can be both more practical and more private. Our implementation is available at https://github.com/dong-xin-ao-andy/bis-mc-accountant.

</details>


### [76] [Multi-Objective Multi-Agent Bandits: From Learning Efficiency to Fairness Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06864)
*John Wang,Mengfan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多目标多智能体多臂老虎机（MO-MA-MAB）问题，提出了分别针对效率（Pareto后悔）和公平性（Nash社会福利）的两类算法，证明了Pareto UCB1 Gossip达到O(log T)和O(√T)后悔界，Simulated NSW UCB Gossip达到O(T^{3/4})后悔界，揭示了公平性约束带来的收敛代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏同时对多目标、多智能体场景下的效率学习与公平学习进行统一建模的方法，特别是在时变图上异构奖励向量环境下。

Method: 提出Pareto UCB1 Gossip算法，通过设计新的探索半径显式分离Pareto推断中的统计不确定性与共识误差；提出Simulated NSW UCB Gossip算法，整合偏好标量化奖励模拟、gossip效用估计与UCB探索。

Result: Pareto UCB1 Gossip达到O(log T)与O(√T)后悔界；Simulated NSW UCB Gossip达到O(T^{3/4})后悔界；实验显示两算法分别提升约100%和50%性能。

Conclusion: 公平性约束限制了信息聚合从而减缓收敛速度，揭示了效率与公平之间的基本权衡。

Abstract: We study multi-objective multi-agent multi-armed bandits (MO-MA-MAB) under stochastic rewards, where agents observe heterogeneous reward vectors and communicate over time-varying graphs. We formulate this emerging problem setting to address \emph{efficient learning}, measured by Pareto regret, and incorporate \emph{fair learning} as an additional goal, captured via social welfare. To measure efficiency, we formulate Pareto regret and develop \textsc{Pareto UCB1 Gossip}, whose novel exploration radius explicitly separates statistical uncertainty in Pareto-based inference from consensus error. To express the fairness constraint, we formulate a Nash Social Welfare objective over preference-scalarized rewards and propose \textsc{Simulated NSW UCB Gossip}, which integrates preference-based reward simulation, gossip-based utility estimation, and UCB-style exploration. We prove that \textsc{Pareto UCB1 Gossip} achieves \(\mathcal{O}(\log T)\) regret and an instance-independent rate of \(\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{T})\), while \textsc{Simulated NSW UCB Gossip} achieves an instance-independent regret bound of \(\mathcal{O}(T^{3/4})\). This separation reveals the cost of imposing the fairness constraint to our efficiency objective: fairness limits information aggregation and slows convergence. Experiments show that our methods consistently outperform baselines, improving performance by approximately \(100\%\) and \(50\%\) in the efficiency and fairness settings, respectively.

</details>


### [77] [Modulated learning for private and distributed regression with just a single sample per client device](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07233)
*Praneeth Vepakomma,Amirhossein Reisizadeh,Samuel Horváth,Munther Dahleh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对联邦学习中每个客户端仅有一个样本的场景，提出了一种通过单次校准噪声扰动和表示后处理的方法，使客户端能在保护隐私的前提下协作学习全局模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在联邦学习中，当每个客户端只有单个样本时，基于该单点的本地更新（尤其是在早期轮次）对模型系数估计几乎无用，而每轮添加的隐私噪声进一步削弱了这种效用。现有联邦学习范式在此场景下失效，亟需一种能同时解决数据稀缺和隐私保护的方法。

Method: 每个客户端对其单一样本注入单次校准的噪声扰动，然后进行后处理得到表示，再将表示分享给服务器；服务器聚合这些表示后，获得无偏梯度更新，该更新在期望上与无隐私保护的集中式梯度一致。

Result: 该方法使得拥有极有限数据的设备能够协作学习准确且保护隐私的模型，无需依赖大量本地数据集或牺牲个体隐私。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种不同于传统私有联邦学习（传输模型系数）的新范式，即传输经过隐私转换的样本表示，从而有效解决了单样本客户端的联邦学习问题。

Abstract: This work focuses on the question of learning from a large number of devices with each device holding only a single sample of data. Several real-world applications exist to this one sample per client setup up including learning from fitness trackers, data/app usage aggregators, body-worn sensing devices, and daily event monitors to name a few. When a client has only one sample, the standard federated learning paradigm breaks down as a local update based on that single point is far from being useful, especially in the earlier rounds for estimation of the model coefficients. This utility is further weakened by the privacy-inducing noise applied at every round. This work caters to this problem to enable such clients to collaboratively contribute to effectively learn a global model without leaking the privacy of their data. The proposed approach injects a single, carefully calibrated noisy perturbation to transform the sample at each client, followed by a post-processed representation which is shared with the server. These representations aggregated at the server are processed to obtain an unbiased gradient update that in expectation matches the non-private centralized gradient while preserving data privacy. This approach is different than traditional private federated learning, where the communication payloads involve model coefficients as opposed to privately transformed data samples. This method enables devices with extremely limited data to collaborate and learn accurate, privacy-preserving models without requiring large local datasets or sacrificing individual privacy.

</details>


### [78] [Dataset Watermarking for Closed LLMs with Provable Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06865)
*Pengrun Huang,Kamalika Chaudhuri,Yu-Xiang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次提出针对封闭式大语言模型的数据集水印方法，通过增加随机词对共现频率来嵌入水印，并利用统计检验检测水印，在仅占微调数据1%的情况下仍可有效检测（p<0.01），且不影响数据集效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在大量非精心筛选数据上预训练和后训练，可能包含专有数据集或评估基准，因此需要数据集水印技术来检测模型是否在特定数据集上训练过，而现有方法仅适用于开源模型。

Method: 通过改写句子增加随机选取的词对在数据集中的共现频率，嵌入水印信号；检测时对模型生成输出中的共现模式进行统计检验。

Result: 在多个基座模型和基准数据集上，该方法在微调阶段可靠检测水印（p<0.01）；水印数据集仅占总微调token约1%时仍有效；同时保持数据集的效用和语义完整性。

Conclusion: 首次实现面向封闭LLM的可证明数据集水印检测，在数据混合场景下仍保持高灵敏度，为保护专有数据集版权和保障评估基准安全性提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are pre-trained and post-trained on vast amounts of loosely curated data, raising the possibility that these models may have been trained on proprietary datasets or the same benchmarks used for evaluation. This motivates the need for dataset watermarking: designing datasets such that training on them leaves detectable signatures in the resulting model. Prior work has explored this problem for open models. We introduce the first dataset watermarking method for closed LLMs with provable detection. In particular, we embed a dataset-level watermark signal by increasing the co-occurrence frequency of randomly selected word pairs through rephrasing, and detect it using a statistical test on co-occurrence patterns in model-generated outputs. We evaluate our method with multiple base models and benchmark datasets and show that it reliably detects the watermark ($p <0.01$) in the fine-tuning stage. Notably, our method remains effective in a data mixture setting where the watermarked dataset constitutes only approximately $1\%$ of the total fine-tuning tokens. Furthermore, we show that our method preserves the utility and semantic integrity of the benchmark.

</details>


### [79] [When Descent Is Too Stable: Event-Triggered Hamiltonian Learning to Optimize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06868)
*Yi Wang,Chandrajit Bajaj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SHAPE是一种基于自适应端口-哈密顿系统的固定预算非凸优化方法，通过将优化提升到增广相空间$(q, p)$，利用学习到的哈密顿向量场进行结构化局部下降，并在检测到局部平衡时通过事件触发机制切换阶段，从而平衡下降、探索和预算分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定预算非凸优化可能因过早稳定在无信息的局部极小点而失败，需要一种机制来决定何时下降、何时利用有前景的盆地、何时因停滞而触发移动。

Method: 将优化问题建模为控制问题，提出SHAPE方法：从梯度下降动力学出发，将优化提升到增广相空间$(q, p)$，引入控制器$u$处理梯度信息；在每个阶段使用学习到的哈密顿向量场进行结构化局部下降；跨阶段使用固定事件时钟检测局部平衡并更新端口和记忆。

Result: 在固定预算非凸优化任务中，SHAPE相比固定策略优化器提升了最佳性能（best-so-far performance）。

Conclusion: 自适应哈密顿能量整形为平衡非凸优化中的下降、探索和预算分配提供了一种有原理的机制。

Abstract: Fixed-budget nonconvex optimization can fail not because local descent is unstable, but because it is too stable: after reaching a nearby stationary point, an optimizer may spend the remaining evaluations refining an uninformative local minimum. We formulate this failure mode as a control problem over optimizer dynamics, where the learner must decide when to descend, when to exploit a promising basin, and when stagnation should trigger movement elsewhere. We introduce SHAPE, a structured adaptive port-Hamiltonian task-family optimizer for event-triggered minima hunting under local information. Starting from gradient-descent dynamics, SHAPE lifts optimization to an augmented phase space $(q, p)$, where the primal state $q$ represents the candidate solution, the cotangent variable $p$ carries directional sensitivity, and a controller $u$ provides processed information from current gradient oracle. Within each stage, a learned Hamiltonian vector field induces structured local descent; across stages, a fixed event clock in the implementation updates ports and memory when local equilibria are detected, with stage-dependent horizons treated in the analysis as a direct generalization. This design preserves a passivity-compatible structure while allowing the same trained policy to use clean, stochastic, or estimated gradient inputs. Experiments on fixed-budget nonconvex optimization tasks show that SHAPE improves best-so-far performance compared with fixed-policy optimizers. These results suggest that adaptive Hamiltonian energy shaping provides a principled mechanism for balancing descent, exploration, and budget allocation in difficult optimization landscapes.

</details>


### [80] [Continuous First, Discrete Later: VQ-VAEs Without Dimensional Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06870)
*Xinyu Zhao,Nikita Karagodin,Hamed Hassani,Sinan Hersek,Paul Pu Liang,Yury Polyanskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现VQ-VAE存在维度坍缩问题（表征仅使用1-2%的满秩维度），导致损失存在硬下界且现有码本改进技术无法突破。通过率失真理论解释坍缩机制，提出简单有效的"AE预热"方案（先训练自编码器再引入VQ），在图像和音频任务上显著提升表征维度（3-5→17-19）并降低损失（rFID降低17-35%，PESQ提升11-14%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VQ-VAE研究多关注码本大小和利用率，但忽略了训练后表征存在于极低维子空间（维度坍缩）的问题，该问题导致损失存在硬下界，已有码本改进技术均无法突破。

Method: 1) 从率失真理论出发，将Saxe等人[2014]的序列学习效应扩展到VQ-VAE场景，理论分析维度坍缩由VQ抑制低方差方向引起；2) 提出AE预热（AE Warm-Up）解决方案：在引入VQ之前先训练模型作为未量化的自编码器；3) 给出依据预热时长预测下游性能的理论分析，支持自适应切换准则。

Result: 在图像（VQGAN）和音频（WavTokenizer）大规模实验上：AE预热将VQGAN码本有效维度从3-5提升至17-19，rFID降低17-35%；将WavTokenizer码本维度从4提升至17-19，PESQ提升11-14%。预热时长与最终损失的关系与理论预测一致。

Conclusion: VQ-VAE的维度坍缩问题可通过简单的AE预热方案有效解决，该方案无需修改架构或引入复杂技巧，且理论分析可预测预热时长与性能的定量关系，支持自适应切换策略。

Abstract: While many approaches to improve VQ-VAE performance focus on codebook size and utilization, the effect of dimensional collapse, where trained VQ-VAE representations live in an extremely low-dimensional subspace (1-2% of full rank), remains unaddressed. We show theoretically and empirically that dimension collapse causes a hard loss lower bound that various codebook improvement techniques fail to surpass. Our analytic framework extends the sequential learning effect of Saxe et al. [2014] by introducing ideas from rate-distortion theory and explains how the latent collapse is caused by the VQ suppressing lower-variance directions. Our theory justifies a simple solution: a "warm-up phase" that trains the model as an (unquantized) autoencoder before introducing VQ. On both synthetic experiments and large-scale image (VQGAN) and audio (WavTokenizer) VQ-VAEs, we show that AE Warm-Up successfully restores representation dimension, leading to lower reconstruction and perceptual loss at the same training budget. Across codebook sizes $K \in$ {$2^{10}, 2^{14}, 2^{16}$}, AE warm-up raises VQGAN codebook effective dimension from 3-5 to 17-19 and reduces rFID by 17-35%; on WavTokenizer at $K \in$ {$2^{13}, 2^{14}$}, it raises codebook dimension from 4 to 17-19 and improves PESQ by 11-14%. We empirically characterize how warm-up duration governs the achievable final loss. In agreement with experiment, our theoretical analysis predicts downstream performance as a function of warm-up length, enabling an adaptive criterion for switching from AE Warm-up to VQ-VAE training.

</details>


### [81] [On the Divergence of Differential Temporal Difference Learning without Local Clocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06874)
*David Antrobius,Shangtong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现在平均奖励强化学习中，基于局部时钟的学习率（按状态访问次数调整）和全局时钟的学习率（仅依赖时间步）的收敛性对应关系被打破，并构造了差分时序差分学习在全局时钟下发散的反例，解决了该领域的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究发现折扣RL中局部时钟和全局时钟的学习率收敛性等价，但平均奖励RL中两者的关系是否仍然成立尚不清楚，这是一个开放问题。

Method: 通过构造反例，证明差分时序差分学习（Differential TD Learning）在使用全局时钟学习率时会发散，而使用局部时钟学习率时收敛。

Result: 在平均奖励RL中，局部时钟和全局时钟的收敛性对应关系不成立；存在算法（如差分TD）在局部时钟下收敛但在全局时钟下发散。

Conclusion: 平均奖励RL中学习率时钟类型的选择至关重要，全局时钟可能导致发散，而局部时钟能保证收敛，这为设计稳定的平均奖励RL算法提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Learning rate is a critical component of reinforcement learning (RL). This work uses global and local clocks to distinguish two types of learning rates. The former is of the standard form $α_t$ that depends only on the time step $t$ (i.e., a global clock). The latter is of the form $α_{ν(S_t, t)}$, where $ν(s, t)$ counts the number of visits to state $s$ until time $t$ (i.e., a local clock). In discounted RL, an RL algorithm that is convergent with a local clock is always also convergent with a global clock, and vice versa. We are not aware of any counterexample. The key contribution of this work is to show that this nice correspondence breaks down in average-reward RL. Specifically, we construct a counterexample showing that although differential temporal difference learning is convergent with a local clock, it can diverge with a global clock. This counterexample closes the open problem in Wan et al. [2021], Blaser et al. [2026].

</details>


### [82] [Temporal Attention for Adaptive Control of Euler-Lagrange Systems with Unobservable Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06877)
*Giansalvo Cirrincione,Adriano Fagiolini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对具有不可观测有限时域内状态摩擦的Euler-Lagrange系统，本文提出了一种元控制架构，利用自注意力模块生成计算力矩控制器的增益，通过强化学习训练并受屏蔽可容许性约束。在2-DOF机械臂上，单层注意力元控制器在短/匹配记忆模式下优于更深层Transformer基线（跟踪误差降低12-19个百分点），但在长记忆模式下优势消失，揭示了静态注意力头数预选的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当摩擦由不可直接观测的有限时域内状态支配时，Euler-Lagrange系统的自适应控制具有挑战性，因为闭环状态不再具有马尔可夫性，标准确定性等价自适应律会失去收敛保证。

Method: 提出元控制架构：自注意力模块处理短窗口运动历史以生成计算力矩控制器的增益；通过自协方差分析基于增量秩追踪框架预选注意力头数；在屏蔽可容许性约束下进行强化学习训练。

Result: 在短记忆和匹配记忆模式下，单层注意力元控制器跟踪误差分别降低12和19个百分点（效应量d约-1.1和-2.1，Mann-Whitney p < 0.05）；长记忆模式下优势消失，40%的训练运行出现发散或有效载荷不变策略崩溃。

Conclusion: 静态Phase-1注意力头数预选在长记忆模式下存在缺陷，需将秩追踪纳入强化学习循环，允许注意力头在运行时剪枝或增长而非训练前固定。

Abstract: Adaptive control of Euler-Lagrange systems is challenging when friction is governed by a finite-horizon internal state that is not directly observable from joint measurements. In this setting, the measured closed-loop state is no longer Markovian, and standard certainty-equivalence adaptive laws may lose their convergence guarantees.
  The paper proposes a meta-control architecture in which the gains of a computed-torque controller are generated by a self-attention block processing a short window of recent motion history. The number of attention heads is selected before policy training through a surrogate analysis of the autocovariance of the memory-state gradient along the temporal window. This surrogate is based on a temporal adaptation of an incremental rank-tracking framework previously developed by the authors. The selected head count is then fixed and used as an architectural hyperparameter in a reinforcement-learning stage, where the policy is trained under a shielded admissibility constraint.
  The approach is tested on a 2-DOF manipulator with nonlinear friction and variable payload. In the short and matched memory regimes, the single-layer attention-only meta-controller outperforms a deeper Transformer baseline, with tracking-error reductions of 12 and 19 percentage points, respectively. The reported effect sizes are large, with d approximately -1.1 and -2.1, and Mann-Whitney p < 0.05 in both cases.
  In the long memory regime, however, the advantage disappears. Four out of ten training runs show either divergence or payload-invariant policy collapse, revealing a weakness in the static Phase-1 head-count prescription. This motivates moving rank-tracking inside the reinforcement-learning loop, allowing attention heads to be pruned or grown at runtime instead of fixed before training.

</details>


### [83] [Better Protein Function Prediction by Modeling Survivorship Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06879)
*Zhongmou Chao,Poompol Buathong,Ekaterina Selivanovitch,Susan Daniel,Peter I. Frazier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Evo-PU框架，利用核苷酸突变机制建模生存者偏差，在正样本-无标签学习框架下区分"因不功能而缺失"与"因未突变而缺失"的序列。在流感、RSV突变效应预测及SARS-CoV-2变异预测任务上，Evo-PU优于标准PU学习、单类分类和蛋白质语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质序列数据存在生存者偏差：我们只能观测到经过自然选择存活的功能性序列，非功能突变已被淘汰。现有正样本-无标签(PU)学习方法忽略了导致这种观测偏差的进化过程，无法区分序列缺失的不同原因（不功能vs.未出现）。

Method: Evo-PU框架利用对核苷酸突变的科学理解来建模生存者偏差。对于充分监测的单生物体序列数据，将距离常见变体仅一步突变的未观测序列视为非功能（应被观测到但未出现），而将难以通过突变产生的未观测序列视为可能因未出现而缺失。

Result: 在流感病毒和RSV突变效应预测（单生物体均匀覆盖监测数据）以及未来SARS-CoV-2变异预测任务上，Evo-PU优于标准PU学习、单类分类(OCC)和蛋白质语言模型(PLM)。在包含多生物体的ProteinGym数据集（异质监测覆盖）上识别了进一步推广方法的机会。

Conclusion: 融入进化生物学先验知识（核苷酸突变机制）能够有效改进蛋白质功能预测中的生存者偏差建模，在充分监测的单生物体场景下表现优异，未来可推广至更复杂的多生物体异质监测数据。

Abstract: Protein sequence data from nature exhibits survivorship bias: we only observe data from those organisms that survive and reproduce, while non-functional protein mutations are eliminated by natural selection. Thus, predicting whether a protein sequence is functional often requires learning from positive examples alone. While positive-unlabeled (PU) learning frameworks offer a generic solution to this problem, existing PU methods ignore the evolutionary processes that shape sequence observability and cause survivorship bias. Consider a sequence that is one mutation away from a commonly-observed protein variant in a well-surveilled organism. If the sequence were functional, it would likely be observed. If it is not observed, this suggests non-functionality. In contrast, sequences that are unlikely to arise through mutation may be missing simply because they never arose. Thus, these two kinds of missing sequences should be treated differently when training models. In this work, we propose Evo-PU, a PU learning framework that uses a scientific understanding of nucleotide mutation to model survivorship bias for well-surveilled single-organism sequence data. On three prediction tasks using single-organism uniform-coverage surveillance data -- predicting results from held-out influenza and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) mutagenesis studies, and predicting future SARS-CoV-2 variants -- Evo-PU outperforms standard PU learning, one-class classification (OCC), and protein language models (PLMs). On prediction tasks from multi-organism ProteinGym datasets with more heterogeneous surveillance coverage, we identify opportunities to generalize our approach.

</details>


### [84] [Don't Retrain, Align: Adapting Autoregressive LMs to Diffusion LMs via Representation Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06885)
*Fred Zhangzhi Peng,Alexis Fox,Anru R. Zhang,Alexander Tong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出REPR-ALIGN方法，通过表示对齐（余弦相似度）将预训练自回归模型（AR）的隐藏状态迁移到扩散语言模型（DLM）中，在不修改架构的情况下实现4倍训练加速，尤其在低数据场景下效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索自回归预训练模型学到的内部表示几何结构能否在转换为扩散语言模型时被显式保留，验证语言表示是否可以跨生成顺序迁移，从而将DLM训练视为重学解码路径而非重学语言表示。

Method: 提出REPR-ALIGN方法，在标准掩码去噪训练基础上，对每一层DLM的隐藏状态与冻结的AR模型隐藏状态计算余弦相似度作为对齐损失，无需适配器或架构修改（仅改变注意力掩码）。

Result: 在实验设置中实现最高4倍训练加速，尤其在低数据场景下表现优异，表明语言表示能够跨生成顺序迁移。

Conclusion: 表示对齐是一种简单有效的扩散语言模型训练技术，语言表示可以跨自回归和扩散两种生成顺序迁移，AR-to-DLM转换应聚焦于重学解码路径而非语言表示本身。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) have recently demonstrated capabilities that complement standard autoregressive (AR) models, particularly in non-sequential generation and bidirectional editing. Although recent work has shown that pretrained autoregressive checkpoints can be converted into diffusion language models, existing recipes primarily transfer parameters through continued denoising training with objective- and attention-level modifications. We instead ask whether the internal representation geometry learned by next-token prediction can be explicitly preserved during AR-to-DLM conversion. We hypothesize that much of the semantic structure learned by AR pretraining can transfer across generation orders, and thus DLM training should be viewed as relearning the decoding path rather than relearning language representations. To investigate this, we introduce REPR-ALIGN, a representation alignment objective that adapts a bidirectional masked diffusion model to reuse representations from a pretrained AR model of identical architecture. Concretely, we align the hidden states of the DLM to the frozen AR model at every layer using cosine similarity, while optimizing the standard masked denoising objective. This simple alignment, with no adapters and no architectural changes beyond the attention mask, yields up to 4x training acceleration in our setting and is particularly effective in low-data regimes. Our results suggest that linguistic representations can transfer across generation order, and that representation alignment provides a simple and effective technique for training diffusion language models. Code is available at https://github.com/pengzhangzhi/Open-dLLM.

</details>


### [85] [Conservative Flows: A New Paradigm of Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06905)
*Eshed Gal,Md Shahriar Rahim Siddiqui,Moshe Eliasof,Eldad Haber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种替代生成范式，通过离散随机动力学直接从数据支持的状态初始化生成过程，而非从噪声先验出发，利用预训练流模型和两种概率保持采样机制（校正Langevin动力学和预测-校正流），在多个数据集上取得了优于原始生成方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成模型主要依赖从噪声先验到数据的传输，作者希望探索一种替代范式，从数据支持的状态初始化生成过程，以改进生成质量和采样效率。

Method: 提出两种概率保持采样机制：(1) 带Metropolis调整的校正Langevin动力学；(2) 预测-校正流。这些方法可直接在预训练流模型检查点上运行，通过离散随机动力学使数据分布保持不变。

Result: 在Swiss-roll合成数据、ImageNet-256和Oxford Flowers-102数据集上验证，所提采样器一致优于原始生成过程。

Conclusion: 从数据支持状态初始化的离散随机动力学生成范式有效，且可适配任意预训练流模型，为生成建模提供了新思路。

Abstract: Modern generative modeling is dominated by transport from a noise prior to data. We propose an alternative paradigm in which generation is performed by a discrete stochastic dynamics that leaves the data distribution invariant, initialized from data-supported states rather than from noise. The framework can utilize any pretrained flow model. We develop two probability-preserving sampling mechanisms, a corrected Langevin dynamics with a Metropolis adjustment and a predictor-corrector flow, that operate directly on existing checkpoints. We validate the framework on a synthetic Swiss-roll target, ImageNet-256 and Oxford Flowers-102, where our samplers consistently improve over the original generation procedures.

</details>


### [86] [TraXion: Rethinking Pre-training Frameworks for Mobility and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06906)
*Shang-Ling Hsu,Mark Tenzer,Cyrus Shahabi,Khurram Shafique*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出多实体时空事件流（MESES）的统一预训练框架TraXion，通过三项公理设计目标与架构，在六个人类移动数据集、企业认证日志和ICU死亡率预测上均超越任务特定基线，证明不同领域事件流可统一建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将移动性建模为句子（轨迹）和词（访问）的语言模型范本无法处理移动数据的三个结构特性：事件是元组值（位置-时间-活动联合分布）、用户轨迹存在持久签名、用户间通过共位产生非独立依赖。需要满足这三条公理的统一预训练框架。

Method: 提出TraXion框架，其目标函数和架构针对MESES的三条公理联合设计。训练后单个检查点直接用于多种下游任务，无需任务特定改造。框架可直接迁移至企业认证日志和ICU医疗数据等不同领域的事件流。

Result: 在六个公开移动数据集上，单检查点TraXion在异常检测、下一POI推荐、下一次访问预测、社交链接预测等所有任务上超越任务特定基线。在不改动的情况下应用于企业认证日志和ICU死亡率预测，达到或超过现有方法。

Conclusion: 移动性、安全、医疗等看似不同的领域的事件流可以共享同一建模框架，MESES的三条公理为统一预训练提供了理论基础，TraXion是有效实现。

Abstract: Human mobility differs from text and from generic time series in three structural ways: visits are tuple-valued events whose meaning depends on the joint distribution over location, time, and activity; users carry persistent signatures across trajectories; and visits are not independent across users, since co-location at shared places is a primary signal. Existing pre-training recipes for mobility import objectives from language modeling, treating trajectories as sentences and visits as tokens, an analogy that fails against each of the three properties above. These properties define a broader class, multi-entity spatiotemporal event streams (MESES), spanning enterprise authentication logs, electronic health records, and other event-stream domains where entities share infrastructure, schedules, or contexts. We make the properties precise as three axioms that any pre-training framework for MESES should satisfy, and introduce TraXion, whose objectives and architecture are jointly designed to meet them. A single TraXion checkpoint per dataset beats task-specific baselines on every task across six public mobility datasets covering anomaly detection, next-POI recommendation, next-visit prediction, and social-link prediction. The same recipe, applied unchanged to enterprise authentication logs and ICU mortality prediction, matches or exceeds prior work on both, showing that event streams from domains as different as mobility, security, and healthcare can be modeled under a single framework.

</details>


### [87] [Dual-Scale Temporal Fusion Reveals Structured Predictability in Subseasonal-to-Seasonal Temperature Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06911)
*Elnaz Bashir,Jiali Wang,Lin Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个双尺度学习框架，通过分离日历对齐的历史气候背景与领先时间匹配的近期天气演化，并利用空间自适应融合，揭示了次季节到季节（S2S）温度预报的可预测性是由跨时间尺度、空间异质性和大尺度模态相干性共同组织的结构化多尺度现象，而非简单的领先时间衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: S2S温度预报在农业、能源规划和极端天气风险管理中至关重要，但其可靠性随季节和地区变化很大。传统观点将预报技巧主要归因于领先时间，但这无法充分解释可预测性的时空模式，因此需要更深入理解其结构特征。

Method: 开发了一个双尺度学习框架，分别提取日历对齐的历史气候背景和领先时间匹配的近期天气演化特征，通过空间自适应融合实现30-90天窗口的稳定预报，并引入拓扑感知的结构约束以改善预测温度场的空间连贯性。

Result: 融合权重显示两种时间尺度的平衡随季节和地理区域系统变化：冬季在高纬度和复杂地形区域，年际背景占主导；夏季则呈现更平衡的时间贡献。空间上可预测性的显式重组（而非简单的领先时间衰减）成为次季节窗口内预报技巧的主要决定因素。拓扑感知约束显著改善了复杂地形区域的大尺度模态组织。

Conclusion: S2S可预测性应被重新理解为一个结构化的多尺度现象，这为改进预报系统及其实际应用提供了更可解释的基础。

Abstract: Subseasonal-to-seasonal (S2S) temperature forecasts, spanning several weeks to a few months, are critically needed in agriculture practice, energy planning, and extreme-weather induced risk management, yet their reliability varies substantially across seasons and regions. Forecast skill is often attributed primarily to lead time, but this perspective does not fully explain the spatiotemporal patterns of predictability. Here we show that S2S predictability is organized across interacting temporal components, spatial heterogeneity, and large-scale pattern coherence, and that this structure can be explicitly characterized and exploited. We develop a dual-scale learning framework that separates calendar-aligned historical climate context from lead-time matched recent weather evolution, combining them through spatially adaptive fusion to enable stable temperature forecasts across the 30 to 90-day window. The learned fusion weights reveal that the balance between these two temporal scales shifts systematically with season and geography: during winter, interannual context dominates over high latitudes and complex terrain where forecast is the most difficult, while summer predictions reflect a more balanced temporal contribution across the domain. This spatially explicit reorganization of predictability, rather than simple lead-time decay, emerges as the primary determinant of forecast skill within the subseasonal window. Topology-aware structural constraints further improve spatial coherence of predicted temperature fields, stabilizing large-scale pattern organization particularly over complex terrain. These results reframe S2S predictability as a structured, multi-scale phenomenon, providing a more interpretable foundation for improving forecast systems and informing their use in practice.

</details>


### [88] [LLMs are not (consistently) Bayesian: Quantifying internal (in)consistencies of LLMs' probabilistic beliefs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06915)
*Chacha Chen,Matthew Jörke,Adam Goliński,Masha Fedzechkina,Guillermo Sapiro,Sinead Williamson,Nicholas Foti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将LLM视为信息处理规则，利用信息处理差距研究LLM如何从证据更新概率信念，发现某些方法产生（近乎）贝叶斯更新，而其他方法使用学习启发式。令人惊讶的是，非贝叶斯启发式更新在下游任务中通常优于精确贝叶斯计算，表明LLM的世界概率模型存在设定偏差。最后展示了该测量如何诊断LLM推理系统的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI系统部署在医学、科学、法律等复杂领域，不仅需要产生正确答案，还需要在新证据出现时表示和更新关于世界的不确定信念。作者旨在研究LLM的内部（不）一致性，即它们如何从证据更新概率信念。

Method: 引入信息处理差距（information processing gap）概念，将LLM作为信息处理规则进行研究。通过多种方法比较LLM合并证据到信念中的方式，区分贝叶斯更新与非贝叶斯启发式更新。

Result: 部分方法产生（近乎）贝叶斯更新，其他方法使用学习到的启发式。非贝叶斯启发式更新在下游任务表现上往往优于精确贝叶斯计算，说明LLM的世界概率模型存在设定偏差（misspecification）。

Conclusion: 提出的测量方法可用于诊断LLM推理系统的潜在问题，为改进其在需要不确定性推理的领域中的应用提供指导。

Abstract: Modern AI systems are being deployed in complex domains such as medicine, science, and law, where it is important that they not only produce correct answers, but also represent and update uncertain beliefs about the world as new evidence arrives. We introduce the novel technique of studying LLMs as information processing rules and utilize the information processing gap to study the internal (in)consistencies of how LLMs update their probabilistic beliefs from evidence. Our extensive experiments evaluate multiple approaches in which LLMs can incorporate evidence into their beliefs. Some of these approaches produce (nearly) Bayesian updates; others seem to use a learned heuristic. Surprisingly, the non-Bayesian heuristic updates often outperform exact Bayesian computation in terms of downstream task performance -- indicating the LLMs' probabilistic models of the world are misspecified. Lastly, we show how our measure can provide diagnostics to identify issues with LLM-powered inferential systems.

</details>


### [89] [Tyche: One Step Flow for Efficient Probabilistic Weather Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06916)
*Fan Xu,Yuan Gao,Kun Wang,Rui Su,Fenghua Ling,Hao Wu,Wanli Ouyang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Tyche是一种单步条件流模型，通过目标感知平均速度流和JVP正则化矫正目标，实现单次函数评估的高效概率天气预报，在ERA5数据上匹配或超越多步生成模型和ECMWF IFS集合预报性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散模型集合预报方法推理成本随预报时长、集合规模和去噪步数线性增长，导致大规模运筹集合预报成本高昂，亟需一种高效的单步概率预报方法。

Method: 提出Tyche模型：(1) 目标感知平均速度流，将高斯噪声直接映射到未来天气状态（1-NFE）；(2) JVP正则化矫正目标，隐式强制时间自一致性；(3) 各向同性Swin风格Transformer参数化输运场；(4) 基于rollout的微调阶段，使用课程CRPS校准监督提升集合可靠性。

Result: 在ERA5 1.5°和6小时间隔数据上，Tyche仅用单步NFE即可匹配或超越多步生成基线方法和ECMWF IFS运筹集合的预报技巧和校准。

Conclusion: 单步条件流模型能够以极低推理成本实现与多步生成模型和运筹集合相当的概率天气预报性能，为大规模高效集合预报提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Probabilistic weather forecasting requires not only accurate trajectories, but calibrated distributions over plausible atmospheric futures. Recent data-driven systems have achieved remarkable deterministic skill, and diffusion-based ensemble forecasters have substantially improved sample realism and uncertainty quantification. However, their inference cost scales with forecast horizon, ensemble size, and the number of denoising steps required for each transition, making large operational ensembles expensive. To address this, we present Tyche, a one-step conditional flow model for efficient probabilistic weather forecasting. Tyche models the conditional forecast distribution with a destination-aware average-velocity flow that maps Gaussian noise directly to future weather states in a single function evaluation (1-NFE). To make this one-step transport learnable in high-dimensional geophysical fields, we derive a JVP-regularized rectification objective that enforces temporal self-consistency across source and destination flow timesteps without explicitly forming Jacobians. The transport field is parameterized by an isotropic Swin-style transformer that preserves fine-scale spatial structure while remaining scalable on global grids. To improve ensemble reliability under autoregressive forecasting, we further introduce a rollout-based finetuning stage with curriculum CRPS calibration supervision. Experiments on ERA5 at 1.5$^\circ$ and 6-hour resolution show that our Tyche, using merely a single NFE, matches or exceeds the forecast skill and calibration of state-of-the-art multi-step generative baselines and the operational ECMWF IFS ensemble.

</details>


### [90] [A Reproducible Optimisation Protocol for Calibrating Prompt-Based Large Language Model Workflows in Evidence Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06937)
*Teo Susnjak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可复现的校准工作流，用于基于提示的大语言模型（LLM）在结构化证据合成任务中的优化。该方法将科学任务规则与可变的提示框架分离，通过标注样例和任务指标优化提示框架，并将校准后的工作流保存为可审查的制品。以标题和摘要筛选为验证案例，使用小型学生LLM执行任务、大型反思LLM引导提示优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于提示的LLM在结构化证据合成任务中存在校准过程不可复现、提示工程随意性强、任务规则与提示框架耦合过紧的问题，缺乏系统化、可迁移的优化工作流。

Method: 1) 将定义科学任务的规则与可变的提示框架（prompt harness）分离；2) 基于标注/参考样例和明确任务指标对提示框架进行优化；3) 将校准后的工作流保存为包含规范、指标、设置和评估轨迹的可审查制品；4) 采用学生-反思双LLM架构：小型学生LLM执行科学任务，大型反思LLM在校准过程中引导提示优化；5) 使用DSPy和GEPA工具实现，逻辑可迁移至其他支持结构化任务定义、指标引导搜索和制品复用的提示优化框架。

Result: 展示了编译过程、制品往返（round-tripping）能力，以及优化预算对小型学生模型性能的影响。

Conclusion: 该工作流为基于提示的LLM在结构化证据合成任务中提供了可复现、可审查、可迁移的校准方法，通过任务规则与提示框架的解耦以及双LLM架构，实现了更系统化的提示优化流程。

Abstract: This methods article presents a reproducible calibration workflow for prompt-based large language models (LLMs) in structured evidence-synthesis tasks. The method separates the rules that define the scientific task from the mutable prompt harness that frames and applies them. It optimises that harness against labelled or reference examples and an explicit task metric, then preserves the calibrated workflow as an inspectable artefact with its specification, metric, settings, and evaluation traces. The example code instantiates the protocol with DSPy and GEPA tools, but the underlying logic can transfer to other prompt-optimisation frameworks that support structured task definitions, metric-guided search, and artefact reuse. Title and abstract screening is the worked validation case because it provides labelled benchmark data and clear evaluation metrics. The demonstrated workflow uses a smaller student LLM for performing the scientific task execution and a larger reflection LLM to steer the prompt optimisation process during calibration. This work shows compilation, artefact round-tripping, and how optimisation budget affects a smaller student model.

</details>


### [91] [Bias and Uncertainty in LLM-as-a-Judge Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06939)
*James Fiedler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLM-as-a-Judge评估中，使用原始评判输出存在系统性偏差，而共享校准的修正估计在某些情况下会产生严重偏差甚至方向反转。本文通过理论分析、模拟和案例研究，提出J和ΔJ作为诊断指标，用于判断修正估计（尤其是共享校准比较）的可靠性，并提供评估报告指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-Judge评估已成为评估基础模型性能的标准工具，但原始评判输出的朴素估计存在系统性偏差。现有修正估计的可靠性严重依赖于评判质量，且在模型比较中还依赖于校准稳定性。共享校准虽然实用但可能引入严重偏差，包括比较估计指向错误方向且置信度很高的情况，因此需要研究这些失效模式并提出诊断方法。

Method: 通过理论分析推导失效条件；通过模拟实验研究评判质量（J）和跨模型校准不稳定性（ΔJ）的影响；通过MMLU-Pro真实数据案例研究展示符号反转现象；提出J和ΔJ作为可靠性诊断指标。

Result: 共享校准的修正估计在评判质量低或跨模型校准不稳定时会产生严重偏差，包括估计方向完全相反且置信度很高的情况。J和ΔJ可以有效诊断这些失效模式。

Conclusion: 提出J和ΔJ作为判断修正估计（尤其是共享校准比较）可靠性的诊断指标，并为LLM-as-a-Judge评估提供报告指导，以提升评估结果的可靠性和可解释性。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-Judge evaluation has become a standard tool for assessing base model performance. However, characterizing performance via the naive estimator, i.e., raw judge outputs, is systematically biased. Recent work has proposed estimators to correct this bias, but their reliability depends critically on judge quality and, for model comparisons, on calibration stability. Sharing calibration across compared models is practically attractive but can introduce severe bias, including cases where the comparison estimate points in the wrong direction with high apparent confidence. We study these failure modes through analytical results, simulations over judge quality ($J$) and cross-model calibration instability ($ΔJ$), and a real-data MMLU-Pro case study with sign reversal. We propose $J$ and $ΔJ$ as diagnostics for when corrected estimates, especially shared-calibration comparisons, are likely unreliable, and provide reporting guidance for LaaJ evaluation.

</details>


### [92] [Adaptive Memory Decay for Log-Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06946)
*Yaxita Amin,Helen Zichen Li,Mengfan Zhang,Samet Ayhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对对数线性注意力中固定记忆衰减参数λ的问题，提出通过学习输入依赖的、逐token逐层级的自适应衰减参数，在保持对数线性复杂度的前提下，提升长程记忆能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列模型在记忆容量与计算效率之间存在根本性权衡：Transformer二次复杂度高，线性注意力和状态空间模型通过压缩上下文到固定大小隐状态而限制召回能力。对数线性注意力通过Fenwick树层次结构以对数线性成本取得平衡，但其记忆衰减参数λ是固定的、与输入无关的，对所有层级赋予统一权重，缺乏灵活性。

Method: 提出通过一个轻量级两层MLP直接从输入中学习λ，生成逐token、逐层级的自适应衰减。采用softplus激活函数使每个Fenwick树层级独立缩放，避免softmax引入的层级间竞争。该方法保持对数线性复杂度完全不变，仅增加极少参数量。

Result: 在关联召回、选择性复制和语言建模任务上，输入依赖的衰减策略持续优于固定衰减基线，在长程记忆设置中提升最大，基线方法在此情况下λ退化或完全崩溃。

Conclusion: 通过学习输入自适应的、逐层级独立的衰减参数，可以有效克服固定衰减的刚性限制，在不增加计算复杂度的前提下显著提升对数线性注意力模型的长程记忆表现。

Abstract: Sequence models face a fundamental tradeoff between memory capacity and computational efficiency. Transformers achieve expressive context modeling at quadratic cost, while linear attention and state-space models run in linear time by compressing context into a fixed-size hidden state, inherently limiting recall. Log-linear attention navigates this tradeoff by organizing memory across a Fenwick tree hierarchy, growing its hidden state logarithmically with sequence length at log-linear compute cost. However, its memory decay parameter λ is fixed and independent of the input, assigning uniform weights across all hierarchy levels regardless of the content, which introduces unnecessary rigidity. We propose learning λ directly from the input via a lightweight two-layer MLP, producing per-token, per-level decay that adapts to content rather than position. A softplus activation lets each Fenwick tree level scale independently, avoiding the inter-level competition that softmax introduces. This modification preserves log-linear complexity exactly and adds negligible parameter overhead. We evaluate on associative recall, selective copying, and language modeling, finding that input-dependent decay consistently outperforms the baseline, with the largest gains in long-range memory settings where baseline λ degrades or collapses entirely.

</details>


### [93] [Rollback-Free Stable Brick Structures Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06947)
*Chenhui Xu,Ziyue Bai,Fuxun Yu,Heng Huang,Jinjun Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于强化学习的砖块结构生成范式，将物理有效性约束从测试时纠正转移到训练时策略优化，首次实现无回滚的稳定砖块结构生成，推理速度提升数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归3D生成模型在构建物理稳定的砖块结构时面临挑战，依赖外部物理模拟器进行拒绝采样和逐砖回滚，严重制约推理效率。

Method: 采用强化学习范式，通过组装级奖励（碰撞避免、全局连通性、结构互锁、形状一致性）进行训练时策略优化，使模型内化物理先验。

Result: 在生成质量上达到SOTA，同时推理速度提升数个数量级。

Conclusion: 强化学习范式能有效将物理有效性约束内化到模型参数中，实现无需外部物理模拟器回滚的高效稳定砖块结构生成。

Abstract: While autoregressive models have advanced 3D generation, creating physically stable brick structures remains a challenge due to the strict requirements of gravity and interconnectivity. Existing approaches rely on external physical simulators during inference to perform rejection sampling and brick-by-brick rollbacks, which severely bottlenecks efficiency. To address this, we propose a reinforcement learning paradigm that shifts physical validity enforcement from test-time correction to training-time policy optimization. By utilizing assembly-level rewards, the model optimizes for collision avoidance, global connectivity, structural interlocking, and shape conformity. This paradigm allows the model to internalize physical priors, enabling the first rollback-free generation of stable brick structures. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach achieves state-of-the-art generation quality while accelerating inference speed by orders of magnitude. Our code and dataset are available at https://github.com/miniHuiHui/STABLE. Our models are available at https://huggingface.co/miniHui/STABLE.

</details>


### [94] [$f$-Divergence Regularized RLHF: Two Tales of Sampling and Unified Analyses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06977)
*Di Wu,Chengshuai Shi,Jing Yang,Cong Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对在线RLHF中通用的f-散度正则化问题，提出了统一的理论框架，设计了两种算法（基于乐观原则和奖励扰动敏感性），并证明了O(log T)的遗憾界和O(1/T)的最优性差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLHF大多依赖反向KL正则化，尽管已有研究开始探索其他散度（如前向KL、卡方散度）作为正则项，但缺乏对通用f-散度正则化的统一理论理解。

Method: 采用整体视角覆盖整个f-散度函数类，提出两种算法：(1) 基于乐观原则并设计探索奖励的算法；(2) 利用f-散度正则化下最优策略对奖励扰动的敏感性来设计新方法。

Result: 两种算法均实现了O(log T)的遗憾界和O(1/T)的最优性差距，这是首次为通用f-散度正则化下的在线RLHF建立可证明的性能界。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了通用f-散度正则化在在线RLHF中缺乏理论理解的空白，证明了所提算法的可证效率，为后续RLHF中正则化方法的选择提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) has become a cornerstone technique for post-training large language models. While most existing approaches rely on the reverse KL-regularization, recent empirical studies have begun exploring alternative divergences (e.g., forward KL, chi-squared) as regularizers in RLHF. However, a unified theoretical understanding of general $f$-divergence regularization remains under-explored. To fill this gap, this work develops a comprehensive theoretical framework for online RLHF with a general $f$-divergence regularized objective. Rather than treating each possible divergence function individually, we adopt a holistic perspective across the entire function class and propose two algorithms based on distinct sampling principles. The first extends the classical optimism principle with a carefully designed exploration bonus, while the second introduces a new method that exploits the sensitivity of the optimal policy to reward perturbations under $f$-divergence regularization. Theoretical analysis shows that $O(\log T)$ regret and $O(1/T)$ sub-optimality gap are achievable, establishing provable efficiency of both algorithms and, to the best of our knowledge, the first performance bounds for online RLHF under general $f$-divergence regularization.

</details>


### [95] [FastOmniTMAE: Parallel Clause Learning for Scalable and Hardware-Efficient Tsetlin Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06982)
*Ahmed K. Kadhim,Lei Jiao,Rishad Shafik,Ole-Christoffer Granmo,Mayur Kishor Shende*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FastOmniTMAE，通过两阶段并行过程替代顺序训练依赖，加速Omni TM-AE的静态嵌入训练，在FPGA上实现高效逻辑基嵌入学习，训练速度提升最高5倍，相似度得分达0.669-0.696。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Omni TM-AE（Tsetlin Machine自编码器）在静态嵌入学习中训练过程缓慢，限制了其在资源受限场景下的应用效率。

Method: 将Omni TM-AE的顺序训练依赖重构为评估与更新两阶段并行过程（FastOmniTMAE），并在SoC-FPGA平台上实现可复用加速器。

Result: 在单次运行多环境基准测试中，分类任务训练速度提升最高5倍，相似度与聚类质量保持同等水平；在Multi-Hardware Benchmark中，资源受限FPGA上相似度得分0.669，UltraScale+ SoC上达0.696。

Conclusion: FastOmniTMAE以较小的硬件开销实现了高效的逻辑基嵌入训练，兼顾了速度与嵌入质量。

Abstract: Embedding models in natural language processing (NLP) increasingly rely on deep architectures such as BERT, while simpler models such as Word2Vec provide efficient representations but limited interpretability. The Tsetlin Machine (TM) offers an alternative logic-based learning paradigm. Omni TM Autoencoder (Omni TM-AE) applies this paradigm to static embedding by exploiting automaton state distributions within a single clause layer, but its training process remains slow. In this work, we propose FastOmniTMAE, a reformulation of Omni TM-AE that replaces sequential training dependencies with a two-stage parallel process: evaluation and update. Using a Single-Run Multi-Environment Benchmark covering classification, similarity, and clustering, FastOmniTMAE achieves up to 5$\times$ faster training in classification while maintaining comparable embedding quality under both Spearman and Kendall similarity measures. To address the limited efficiency of TM training on conventional GPUs, we further implement FastOmniTMAE as a reusable accelerator on SoC-FPGA platforms. The Multi-Hardware Benchmark shows that FastOmniTMAE achieves similarity scores of 0.669 on a resource-constrained FPGA and 0.696 on an UltraScale+ SoC, demonstrating efficient logic-based embedding training with a small hardware footprint.

</details>


### [96] [Response Time Enhances Alignment with Heterogeneous Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06987)
*Federico Echenique,Alireza Fallah,Baihe Huang,Michael I. Jordan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明在偏好数据中增加用户响应时间这一简单辅助信号，可以解决仅依赖二元选择数据无法识别真实群体平均偏好的根本问题。通过漂移扩散模型（DDM）建模，提出了一致性的异质偏好估计器，理论上证明即使在每个匿名标注者仅贡献一次选择的情况下也能渐近收敛到真实平均偏好，实验结果表明该方法显著优于仅依赖选择标签的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM对齐方法依赖聚合的二元选择反馈训练单一奖励模型，但这一标准做法假设所有标注者共享相同偏好，忽略真实世界中标注者的高度异质性和匿名性，导致仅凭二元选择数据无法识别真实的群体平均偏好，从而扭曲学习到的策略。

Method: 提出在偏好数据中增加用户响应时间作为辅助信号，将每个决策过程建模为漂移扩散模型（DDM），基于该模型设计了一致性的异质偏好估计器，从理论上证明其渐近收敛性。

Result: 理论上证明了即使在每个匿名标注者仅贡献一次选择的极端情况下，估计器也能渐近收敛到真实平均偏好；在合成和真实数据集上，该方法一致性地优于仅依赖选择标签的基线方法，后者存在无法逾越的偏差下限。

Conclusion: 响应时间几乎免费记录且无需用户跟踪或身份识别，该方法为未来数据收集管线在不依赖用户级标识符或重复询问的情况下提升社会效益开辟了新机遇。

Abstract: Aligning large language models (LLMs) to human preferences typically relies on aggregating pooled feedback into a single reward model. However, this standard approach assumes that all labelers share the same underlying preferences, ignoring the fact that real-world labelers are highly heterogeneous and usually anonymous. Consequently, relying solely on binary choice data fundamentally distorts the learned policy, making the true population-average preference unidentifiable. To overcome this critical limitation, we demonstrate that augmenting preference datasets with a simple, secondary signal -- the user's response time -- can restore the identifiability of the population's average preference. By modeling each decision as a Drift-Diffusion Model (DDM), we introduce a novel, consistent estimator of heterogeneous preferences that successfully corrects the distortions of standard choice-only labels. We prove that our estimator asymptotically converges to the true average preference even in extreme cases where each anonymous labeler contributes only a single choice. Empirically, across both synthetic and real-world datasets, our method consistently outperforms standard baselines that otherwise fail and plateau at a bias floor. Because response times are essentially free to record and require zero user tracking or identification, our results bring promises and open up new opportunities for future data-collection pipelines to improve the social benefit without requiring user-level identifiers or repeated elicitations.

</details>


### [97] [Dual-Agent Co-Training for Health Coaching via Implicit Adversarial Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07011)
*Da Long,Lingyi Fu,Diya Michelle Rao,Jasmine Ruales Carrera,Yang Bai,Shandian Zhe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种双智能体框架，通过交互式协同训练健康教练智能体和客户模拟器，利用DPO优化教练、对抗训练优化客户，以提升AI健康教练的对话质量，实验证明该方法在多维度上有效改善了教练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI健康教练方法仅优化交互的单侧（固定客户环境训练对话智能体，或固定助理训练客户模拟器），限制了交互空间的探索，效率低下且难以突破目标智能体的性能边界。同时，人类教练稀缺且成本高昂，亟需可扩展且经济的AI健康教练方案。

Method: 提出双智能体框架，交互式协同训练教练智能体和客户模拟器：(1) 教练智能体使用DPO优化，基于多维LLM评判器识别的帕累托优势响应对进行训练；(2) 客户模拟器通过反转这些偏好进行对抗训练，形成隐式对抗训练动态。该协同训练过程具有自然随机博弈解释。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在多个重要维度上有效提升了教练质量。

Conclusion: 双智能体交互式协同训练框架优于传统单侧优化方法，为开发可扩展、高质量的AI健康教练系统提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Motivational-interviewing-based health coaching is an effective approach for improving mental health and promoting healthy behavior change. However, the scarcity of trained human coaches and the high cost of coaching services make such support inaccessible to many people who could benefit from it. This motivates the development of AI health coaches that can provide scalable and affordable support. Existing methods typically optimize only one side of the interaction: they either train a dialogue agent against a fixed client environment or train a client simulator against a fixed assistant. This one-sided setup can limit exploration of the interaction space and may be inefficient at developing the capabilities required by the target agent and pushing its performance boundaries. In this paper, we propose a dual-agent framework that interactively co-trains both the health coach agent and the client simulator. The coach is optimized with DPO using Pareto-dominant response pairs identified by a multi-dimensional LLM judge. In turn, the client is trained adversarially by reversing these preferences, inducing an implicit adversarial training dynamic. We further show that this co-training process admits a natural stochastic-game interpretation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method effectively improves coaching quality across several important dimensions.

</details>


### [98] [FlashMol: High-Quality Molecule Generation in as Few as Four Steps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07020)
*Xinyuan Wei,Zian Li,Shaoheng Yan,Cai Zhou,Muhan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlashMol是一个基于分布匹配蒸馏的超快分子构象生成模型，仅需4步即可生成高质量3D分子构象，相比1000步的教师模型实现最高250倍加速，且在QM9和GEOM-DRUG数据集上匹配或超越原模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型（如GeoLDM）生成分子构象需要数百步，计算成本过高，不利于大规模虚拟筛选；而近年来的少步生成方法虽将步数降至12-50步，却严重牺牲了样本稳定性。因此需要一种既能保持高质量又能实现极速生成的分子构象模型。

Method: 1) 将分布匹配蒸馏（DMD）——一种反向KL散度最小化目标——适配到分子领域进行有效蒸馏；2) 针对DMD的局部最小化行为，重新设置分子生成的时间步长，为生成器提供更好的初始化；3) 引入Jensen-Shannon散度正则化项，融合前向KL散度的均值搜索行为，以缓解DMD的模式搜索问题并提升多样性。

Result: 在QM9和GEOM-DRUG数据集上的广泛实验表明，FlashMol仅用4步生成即可匹配甚至超越原始1000步教师模型的性能，采样速度实现最高250倍加速，同时保持高分子的质量。

Conclusion: FlashMol成功实现了分子构象生成的超快速高质量生成，为大规模计算药物发现提供了实用的加速方案，验证了分布匹配蒸馏在分子生成领域的有效性。

Abstract: Generating chemically valid 3D molecular conformations is critical for computational drug discovery. Classical diffusion-based models like GeoLDM perform well but require hundreds of steps, making large-scale in silico screening impractical. Recent efforts on few-step molecular generation have accelerated this process to 12-50 steps, but they often largely sacrifice sample stability. In this work, we present FlashMol, an ultra-fast molecule generative model producing high-quality molecular conformations in as few as 4 steps. To achieve this, we adapt distribution matching distillation (DMD) - a reverse KL-divergence minimization objective - to the molecular domain for effective distillation. Considering the local minimization behavior of DMD, we respace the molecule generation timesteps, providing the generator with much better initialization and enables effective distillation. Additionally, to mitigate the mode-seeking behavior of DMD and improve diversity, we further regularize it with a Jensen-Shannon divergence term, which incorporates the mean-seeking behavior of the forward KL divergence. Extensive experiments on QM9 and GEOM-DRUG datasets demonstrate that FlashMol matches and even surpasses the original 1000-step teacher, achieving up to 250$\times$ acceleration in sampling speed while maintaining high molecular quality.

</details>


### [99] [Self Driving Datasets: From 20 Million Papers to Nuanced Biomedical Knowledge at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07022)
*Haydn Jones,Yimeng Zeng,Alden Rose,Li S. Yifei,Yining Huang,Kaiwen Wu,Jiaming Liang,Maggie Ziyu Huan,Yoseph Barash,Cesar de la Fuente-Nunez,Osbert Bastani,Zachary Ives,Mark Yatskar,Jacob R. Gardner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Starling 系统，通过 LLM 驱动的实体标注管道、混合检索和多智能体深度研究系统，将整个 PubMed 语料库（2250万篇论文）自主转化为结构化数据集，在六个生物医学任务上生成约630万条记录，其规模、准确性和语义丰富度均优于传统人工 curated 数据库，且错误率远低于现有基准（0.6-7.7% vs 7.3-16.5%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工维护的生物医学数据库（如 bioactivity、genomics、chemistry 等）成本高昂、更新滞后于原始文献，且丢弃了实验上下文信息，导致难以评估数据的正确性和覆盖范围。现有 curated 数据库存在较高的错误率（如 BBB_Martins 达16.5%），亟需一种自动化、低成本且能保留语义细节的数据提取方法。

Method: (1) 基于 LLM 的实体标注管道，利用九种生物医学本体对 PubMed 语料（2250万篇论文，2.5万亿 token）进行标注，覆盖19个类别共45亿个实体；(2) 混合稀疏-稠密检索系统，支持基于实体过滤的语义查询；(3) Starling 多智能体深度研究系统，根据自然语言任务描述自动设计检索过滤器、推导提取模式，并生成带有语义丰富字段和支持段落的结构化记录。

Result: 在血脑屏障通透性、口服生物利用度、急性毒性（LD50）、基因-疾病关联、蛋白质亚细胞定位和化学反应六大任务上，生成约630万条记录（每任务9.1万至300万条），其中多个为相应属性最大的公开数据集。前沿模型对提取结果的拒绝率为0.6-7.7%，远低于 curated 数据库的错误率（如 BBB_Martins 16.5%、Bioavailability_Ma 7.3%）。

Conclusion: 本文提出的语料库、检索系统和智能体框架为 AI 驱动的治疗设计奠定了基础。数据规模和准确性的优势表明，基于 LLM 的自动化管道可以取代传统人工 curated 数据库，同时保留更丰富的实验语义信息（如口服生物利用度对进食/空腹状态的依赖）。

Abstract: Manually curated biomedical repositories -- spanning bioactivity, genomics, and chemistry -- are expensive to maintain, lag behind primary literature, and discard experimental context, obscuring nuances needed to assess data correctness and coverage. We show that PubMed itself can be autonomously and cost-effectively turned into structured datasets that are larger, more nuanced, and more accurate than the curated databases they replace. We present three coupled contributions: (1) an LLM-based entity-tagging pipeline, grounded in nine biomedical ontologies, that tags 4.5B entities across 19 categories in a 22.5M-paper, 2.5T-token PubMed corpus; (2) hybrid sparse-dense retrieval supporting entity-filtered semantic queries over the tagged corpus; and (3) Starling, a multi-agent deep research system that, given only a natural-language task description, designs precision- and recall-targeted retrieval filters, induces an extraction schema, and emits structured records with nuance-rich fields and supporting passages. Across six tasks -- blood-brain barrier permeability, oral bioavailability, acute toxicity (LD50), gene-disease associations, protein subcellular localization, and chemical reactions -- Starling produces ~6.3M records (91K-3M per task); several are, to our knowledge, the largest public datasets for their property. Frontier-model rejection of our extractions is 0.6-7.7% across tasks, far below error rates we measure on widely used curated counterparts (e.g., 16.5% on BBB_Martins, 7.3% on Bioavailability_Ma). Beyond scale and accuracy, the supporting passages carry nuance tabular databases discard -- e.g., oral bioavailability may depend on fed vs. fasted state. Together, the corpus, retrieval, and agent establish a foundation for AI-driven therapeutic design. Code and datasets: https://github.com/starling-labs/starling.

</details>


### [100] [Delulu: A Verified Multi-Lingual Benchmark for Code Hallucination Detection in Fill-in-the-Middle Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07024)
*Mahdi Erfanian,Nelson Daniel Troncoso,Aashna Garg,Amabel Gale,Xiaoyu Liu,Pareesa Ameneh Golnari,Shengyu Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Delulu是一个经过验证的多语言代码填充（FIM）幻觉基准测试，包含1,951个样本、7种语言和4种幻觉类型。最强模型仅达84.5% pass@1，所有模型家族都会产生幻觉对齐的生成结果，表明该任务存在本质性难度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码生成LLM在FIM任务中频繁产生幻觉（如虚构API、无效参数、未定义变量、不存在的导入），这些错误看似合理但引入运行时错误，现有基准难以系统评估此类问题。

Method: 通过对抗性流程构建基准：前沿LLM生成合理幻觉 → 4个判分模型评估 → 嵌入聚类挖掘困难样本 → Docker容器验证黄金补全能编译而幻觉变体产生错误 → 人类专家最终审查。包含11个开源模型（0.5B-32B），横跨Qwen2.5-Coder、CodeLlama、DeepSeek-Coder-V2、StarCoder2五个家族。

Result: 最强模型仅84.5% pass@1；所有模型家族的Edit Similarity均不超过0.77；每个模型家族都在不可忽视的样本比例上产生与幻觉对齐的补全，证实了Delulu所暴露的难度是任务固有的而非特定于某个模型家族。

Conclusion: Delulu揭示了FIM任务中幻觉问题的固有难度，现有模型远未解决该问题。基准、Docker容器和评估框架已开源。

Abstract: Large Language Models for code generation frequently produce hallucinations in Fill-in-the-Middle (FIM) tasks -- plausible but incorrect completions such as invented API methods, invalid parameters, undefined variables, or non-existent imports. These failures pass superficial review yet introduce runtime errors. We introduce Delulu, a verified multi-lingual benchmark of 1,951 FIM samples across 7 languages and 4 hallucination types. Samples are curated through an adversarial pipeline: a frontier LLM generates plausible hallucinations, four diverse judge models evaluate them, embedding-based clustering mines progressively harder examples, self-contained Docker containers verify that golden completions compile while hallucinated variants produce the expected runtime error, and a final human-expert review removes any remaining biased or trivially decidable samples. We evaluate 11 open-weight FIM models from five families spanning 0.5B-32B parameters: a six-point Qwen2.5-Coder scaling slate, plus a cross-family slate (CodeLlama, DeepSeek-Coder-V2, StarCoder2). The strongest model reaches only 84.5% pass@1, no family exceeds 0.77 Edit Similarity, and every family produces hallucination-aligned completions on a non-trivial share of samples, confirming that the difficulty exposed by Delulu is task-intrinsic rather than family-specific. We release the benchmark, containers, and evaluation framework at https://github.com/microsoft/delulu.

</details>


### [101] [A Systematic Investigation of The RL-Jailbreaker in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07032)
*Montaser Mohammedalamen,Kevin Roice,Reginald McLean,Alyssa Lefaivre Škopac*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统分解了基于强化学习的对抗性越狱攻击框架，揭示了环境形式化（密集奖励和延长回合长度）是越狱成功的主要驱动因素，所有目标模型和安全防护均被成功攻破。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习越狱框架虽然有效，但其成功机制尚不明确，缺乏系统性的理解，无法有效指导生成模型的安全加固。

Method: 将RL越狱框架分解为问题形式化（奖励函数、动作空间、回合长度）和算法度量（RL算法、训练数据、奖励塑形）两个维度，系统识别决定攻击成功的结构性因素。

Result: RL越狱器成功攻破了所有目标模型和安全防护；环境形式化（尤其是密集奖励和延长回合长度）是越狱成功的主要驱动因素。

Conclusion: 该分解分析为提升RL越狱器效率提供了工具，并最终有助于加固生成模型以抵御基于RL的攻击。

Abstract: The evolution of generative models from next-token predictors to autonomous engines of complex systems necessitates rigorous safety hardening. Adversarial jailbreaking, the strategic manipulation of models to elicit harmful output, remains a primary threat to safe deployment. While Reinforcement Learning (RL) frames jailbreaking as a multi-step attack through sequential optimization, a mechanistic understanding of why the framework succeeds remains incomplete. To fill this gap, we present the first systematic decomposition of RL jailbreaking. We deconstruct the framework into problem formalization (reward function, action space, episode length), and algorithmic measures (RL algorithm, training data, reward-shaping) to identify the structural determinants of adversarial success. Our results reveal that the RL-jailbreaker successfully compromised all targeted models and safeguards. Through this first-of-its-kind analysis, we demonstrate that environment formalization, specifically dense rewards and extended episode lengths, is the primary driver of jailbreaking success. This work provides a tool for improving RL-jailbreaker efficiency and, ultimately, harden generative models resistant to RL-based attacks.

</details>


### [102] [Unlocking High-Fidelity Molecular Generation from Mass Spectra via Dual-Stream Line Graph Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07048)
*Xujun Che,Xiuxia Du,Depeng Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DualLGD，一种双流线图扩散方法，通过将分子图去噪分解为原子级和键级两个耦合子问题，并利用线图表示键空间、结合入射约束的交叉注意力机制实现双流同步，在NPLIB1和MassSpecGym基准上取得了约3倍于先前最佳方法的top-1准确率（34.37%和23.89%）。消融实验表明架构本身是性能提升的主要来源，即使无预训练也超越此前最佳全预训练模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从串联质谱进行从头分子生成是一个逆问题，其核心困难在于原子级与键级推理之间存在循环依赖：确定键类型需要知道端点原子的化学环境，而原子环境又由其入射键定义。现有图扩散方法在单一计算流中处理原子和键，原子-键信息同步只能跨层隐式进行。作者认为这种单流范式（而非特定聚合核的选择）是关键的架构瓶颈。

Method: 提出DualLGD（双流线图扩散）：(1) 将分子图去噪重新表述为两个耦合子问题的交替求解——原子级推理和键级推理，各自在专用表示空间中操作；(2) 使用线图作为键空间的自然数学构造，其中键角、二面角、共轭链和环对应于键之间的局部拓扑模式；(3) 设计入射约束的双向交叉注意力机制，在每一层同步两个流，确保每个原子只关注其入射键，反之亦然，遵循原子环境由其成键上下文决定的基本化学原理。

Result: 在NPLIB1和MassSpecGym基准上，DualLGD分别达到34.37%和23.89%的top-1准确率，约为先前最佳方法的3倍。消融实验确认架构是性能提升的主要来源：无任何预训练的DualLGD已超越此前最佳的全预训练模型。

Conclusion: 单流范式是分子图扩散中原子-键信息同步的架构瓶颈；双流分离加入射约束交叉注意力机制能更有效地建模分子结构中原子与键的循环依赖关系，显著提升从头分子生成的性能。

Abstract: De novo molecular generation from tandem mass spectra is a challenging inverse problem whose core difficulty lies in the circular dependency between atom-level and bond-level reasoning: determining a bond's type requires knowing its endpoint atoms' chemical environment, yet an atom's environment is in turn defined by its incident bonds. Existing graph diffusion methods process atoms and bonds within a single computation stream, where atom-bond information synchronization can only occur implicitly across layers. We argue that this single-stream paradigm, rather than the choice of any particular aggregation kernel, is a key architectural bottleneck. We propose DualLGD (Dual-stream Line Graph Diffusion), which reformulates molecular graph denoising as the alternating solution of two coupled subproblems: atom-level reasoning and bond-level reasoning, each operating in its own dedicated representation space. The line graph provides a natural mathematical construction for the bond space, in which bond angles, dihedrals, conjugation chains, and rings correspond to local topological motifs between bonds. Incidence-constrained bidirectional cross-attention synchronizes the two streams at every layer, ensuring that each atom attends only to its incident bonds and vice versa, respecting the fundamental chemical principle that an atom's environment is determined by its bonding context. On the NPLIB1 and MassSpecGym benchmarks, DualLGD achieves top-1 accuracy of 34.37\% and 23.89\%, approximately $3\times$ the previous state of the art. Ablation studies confirm the architecture as the primary source of improvement: DualLGD without any pre-training already surpasses the previous best fully pretrained model.

</details>


### [103] [Towards Differentially Private Reinforcement Learning with General Function Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07049)
*Yi He,Xingyu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次为差分隐私在线强化学习在通用函数逼近下提供了理论保证，超越了先前局限于表格和线性设置的工作。通过结合批量策略更新方案与指数机制及新颖的遗憾分析，在模型无关设置下实现了与线性情况最优结果相匹配的遗憾界 $\widetilde{O}(K^{3/5})$，同时建立了基于覆盖性复杂度度量的批量更新在线RL的遗憾界，并揭示了线性函数近似下私有RL中存在的根本性差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私在线强化学习的研究仅限于表格设置和线性函数逼近，缺乏对更通用函数逼近情形下的理论保证。作者旨在填补这一空白，为通用函数逼近下的隐私在线RL建立首次理论保证。

Method: 将批量策略更新方案与指数机制相结合，并设计了新颖的遗憾分析方法。具体而言，采用批量更新的方式收集数据，利用指数机制实现差分隐私保护，并通过新的理论分析工具导出遗憾界。

Result: 在通用函数逼近下，模型无关设置的遗憾界为 $\widetilde{O}(K^{3/5})$，与线性情况下的最优结果相匹配。同时建立了依赖覆盖性复杂度度量的批量更新在线RL的首次遗憾界。还发现了线性函数近似下私有RL结果中存在的根本性理论差距。

Conclusion: 本文首次将差分隐私在线RL的理论保证从表格和线性设置扩展到通用函数逼近，证明了隐私保护下的遗憾界与线性情况最优结果一致。此外，对线性函数近似私有RL中现有结果的理论缺陷进行了澄清，为该领域的后续研究奠定了更坚实的基础。

Abstract: We present the first theoretical guarantees for differentially private online reinforcement learning (RL) with general function approximation, extending beyond prior work restricted to tabular and linear settings. Our approach combines a batched policy update scheme with the exponential mechanism, together with a novel regret analysis. We show that, even under general function approximation, the regret in the model-free setting under differential privacy matches the state of the art for the linear case, scaling as $\widetilde{O}(K^{3/5})$, where $K$ denotes the number of episodes. As an important by-product, we also establish the first regret bound for online RL with batch update that depends on the standard complexity measure of coverability, complementing existing results based on a newly introduced Eluder-Condition class. In addition, we uncover fundamental gaps in recent results for private RL with linear function approximation, thereby clarifying its landscape.

</details>


### [104] [Integrating Causal DAGs in Deep RL: Activating Minimal Markovian States with Multi-Order Exposure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07057)
*Jiamin Xu,Jacqueline Maasch,Kyra Gan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套从纵向因果图构建最小马尔可夫状态表示的程序，并发现仅用最小表示在深度RL中效果不佳，进而提出MOSE（多阶状态暴露）方法，通过向同一Q函数输入多阶历史状态构建来引入受控冗余，显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果强化学习通常假设已给定马尔可夫状态，聚焦于利用因果加速学习，但缺乏从原始观测变量构建满足马尔可夫性的状态的系统性方法，存在基础性空白。

Method: 提出从纵向因果图构建最小状态表示的程序，保证马尔可夫性；针对深度RL中最小表示失效的问题，提出MOSE方法，将多阶历史状态构建同时输入同一Q函数。

Result: MOSE在常见基准和合成数据集上一致优于最小状态构建和单窗口策略；加入最小表示可进一步提升性能。

Conclusion: 因果深度RL的核心原则是：最小充分性不足，需要受控冗余才能发挥因果状态信息的作用。

Abstract: Online reinforcement learning (RL) relies on the Markov property for guaranteed performance, but real-world applications often lack well-defined states given raw observed variables. While causal RL has attracted growing interest, existing work typically assumes Markovian states are provided and focuses on using causality to accelerate learning, leaving a fundamental gap: \emph{given a longitudinal causal graph over observed variables, how does one construct MDP states that provably satisfy the Markov property?} We address this by providing a procedure that constructs a provably minimal state representation. In deep RL, we observe that the minimal representation alone empirically fails to improve performance, indicating that neural networks cannot directly exploit Markovian minimality. To address this, we propose \textbf{MOSE} (Multi-Order State Exposure), which feeds multi-order historical state constructions into the same $Q$-function. MOSE consistently outperforms both the minimal state construction and single-window policies on common benchmarks and synthetic datasets. Including the minimal representation alongside MOSE can further improve performance. Our results establish a core principle for causal deep RL: minimal sufficiency is not enough, and \emph{controlled redundancy} is necessary to unlock the benefit of causal state information.

</details>


### [105] [Dr. Post-Training: A Data Regularization Perspective on LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07063)
*Pingbang Hu,Xueshen Liu,Z. Morley Mao,Jiaqi W. Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Dr. Post-Training 框架，将通用训练数据重新概念化为数据诱导的正则化器，而非数据选择池，通过构建可行更新方向集并投影目标数据更新方向，在 SFT、RLHF、RLVR 任务上超越现有数据选择方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM后训练中高质量目标数据稀缺，而通用训练数据丰富但存在对齐偏差。现有数据选择方法未能充分利用通用数据，本文旨在重新定义通用数据的角色，将其作为正则化手段防止过拟合。

Method: 提出 Dr. Post-Training 框架：每步训练时，用通用训练数据构建模型更新方向的可行集，将稀缺目标数据指定的更新方向投影到该可行集上。标准训练和数据选择方法均为此框架的特例，对应不同偏置-方差权衡点。进一步提出一系列方法族，并引入系统级优化以适应LLM规模。

Result: 在 SFT、RLHF、RLVR 多个下游任务上，所提方法一致优于当前最优的数据选择基线方法；系统基准测试验证了其高效性，额外开销极小。

Conclusion: 通用训练数据应作为数据诱导的正则化器而非选择池，该视角统一了现有方法并提供了更丰富的设计空间与更灵活的偏置-方差权衡，在多种后训练场景下均有效且高效。

Abstract: Data selection methods address a critical challenge in LLM post-training: effectively leveraging scarce, high-fidelity target data alongside abundant but imperfectly aligned general training data. In this work, we move beyond the data-selection framing and introduce Dr. Post-Training (Data-Regularized Post-Training), a novel framework that reconceptualizes general training data as a data-induced regularizer that prevents overfitting to the scarce target objective, rather than serving as a pool for selection. Specifically, our framework proposes that at each training step, construct a feasible set of model update directions using the general training data, and project the model update direction specified by the scarce target data onto that feasible set. Standard training and existing data selection methods arise as special cases with different choices of the data-induced regularizer, and these methods correspond to different points on a bias--variance spectrum with different regularization strength. Building on this view, we propose a family of methods offering a richer design space and more flexible bias--variance tradeoffs. For practical LLM-scale use, we introduce careful system optimizations that realize these methods with minimal overhead. Extensive experiments across SFT, RLHF, and RLVR show that our methods consistently outperform state-of-the-art data selection baselines, and system benchmarks confirm their efficiency.

</details>


### [106] [PolarAdamW: Disentangling Spectral Control and Schur Gauge-Equivariance in Matrix Optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07067)
*Haozhou Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PolarAdamW优化器，将Muon的极坐标谱范数控制与AdamW的坐标预处理分离。实验发现：在标准Transformer中，谱控制效果显著，PolarAdamW优于Muon和AdamW；但在具有非平凡多重性基自由的SO(3)-等变3D点云回归中，Muon反超PolarAdamW，揭示了Schur规范等变性的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器的矩阵级更新耦合了两种不同效应：通过极坐标映射实现的谱控制，以及在多重性空间基正交变换下的等变性（Schur规范等变性）。作者希望解耦这两种效应，以理解它们各自对优化性能的贡献。

Method: 提出PolarAdamW算法：将Muon的Newton-Schulz极坐标映射应用于AdamW预处理后的方向（而非原始动量），从而保留谱范数控制但打破规范等变性。在计算开销上与Muon相当。

Result: 在DeiT-Tiny（4个100类ImageNet子集）上：PolarAdamW平均测试准确率比Muon高+1.93pp，比AdamW高+9.5pp；300 epoch DeiT配方下仍领先Muon +1.37pp、AdamW +5.80pp。在SO(3)-等变3D点云回归中，Muon在所有容量下均优于PolarAdamW，且差距随容量增大而扩大。两种矩阵极化优化器均优于AdamW。

Conclusion: 谱控制与AdamW预处理在标准Transformer上兼容良好，而Schur规范等变性在多重性基自由具有结构重要性的场景中变得关键。这种双重分离揭示了两种效应的不同适用场景。

Abstract: Muon's matrix-level update couples two distinct effects: spectral control via a polar map, and equivariance under orthogonal changes of multiplicity-space basis (Schur gauge-equivariance). We separate them with PolarAdamW, a controlled hybrid that preserves Muon's polar spectral-norm control but breaks the gauge-equivariance, since AdamW's coordinatewise preconditioner is basis-dependent. Algorithmically, PolarAdamW applies Muon's Newton-Schulz polar map to AdamW's preconditioned direction rather than to raw momentum, at per-iteration wall-time comparable to Muon. We prove that Muon's polar step is Schur gauge-equivariant on multiplicity matrices while AdamW's coordinatewise step is not.
  On DeiT-Tiny trained from scratch on four independently sampled 100-class subsets of ImageNet-1k, where multiplicity-basis freedom is trivial, PolarAdamW outperforms Muon by +1.93 pp in test accuracy on average and AdamW by +9.5 pp; under the 300-epoch DeiT-style recipe, it remains ahead of Muon by +1.37 pp and AdamW by +5.80 pp on average. On SO(3)-equivariant 3D point-cloud regression, where multiplicity-basis freedom is non-trivial, the ordering reverses: Muon outperforms PolarAdamW at every audited capacity, and the gap widens with capacity. Both matrix-polar optimisers continue to outperform AdamW. This double dissociation separates spectral control from Schur gauge-equivariance: the first composes well with AdamW preconditioning on standard transformers, while the second becomes consequential when multiplicity-basis freedom is structurally non-trivial.

</details>


### [107] [ModelLens: Finding the Best for Your Task from Myriads of Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07075)
*Rui Cai,Weijie Jacky Mo,Xiaofei Wen,Qiyao Ma,Wenhui Zhu,Xiwen Chen,Muhao Chen,Zhe Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ModelLens是一个统一的模型推荐框架，通过利用公开排行榜上分散但丰富的交互记录，学习模型-数据集-度量三元组的性能感知潜在空间，从而能够在不实际运行候选模型的情况下，为未见过的数据集推荐未见过的模型。在包含162万条评估记录、4.7万个模型和9.6万个数据集的基准测试中，ModelLens超越了依赖元数据或需要在目标数据集上运行候选模型的基线方法，并将多种路由方法的性能提升高达81%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源模型生态系统中存在数十万个预训练模型，但为新的数据集选择最佳模型变得越来越困难：新模型和未基准测试的数据集不断涌现，实践者缺乏任何先验记录。现有方法只能处理这种野外场景的片段：AutoML和迁移性估计需要小规模预定义候选池或昂贵的逐模型前向传播，而模型路由则预设了给定的候选池。

Method: 核心思路是公开排行榜交互虽然分散且有噪声，但集体描绘了跨异构评估设置的模型能力隐式图谱。通过学习模型-数据集-度量三元组的性能感知潜在空间，ModelLens无需在目标数据集上运行候选模型即可对未见模型在未见数据集上进行排序。

Result: 在包含162万评估记录、4.7万个模型和9.6万个数据集的基准上，ModelLens超越了依赖元数据或需要在目标数据集上运行每个候选模型的基线方法。其推荐的Top-K池将多种代表性路由方法在多样化QA基准上的性能提升了高达81%。

Conclusion: ModelLens成功构建了一个统一的野外模型推荐框架，案例研究进一步证实了其对文本和视觉-语言任务的良好泛化能力。

Abstract: The open-source model ecosystem now contains hundreds of thousands of pretrained models, yet picking the best model for a new dataset is increasingly infeasible: new models and unbenchmarked datasets emerge continuously, leaving practitioners with no prior records on either side. Existing approaches handle only fragments of this in-the-wild setting: AutoML and transferability estimation select models from small predefined pools or require expensive per-model forward passes on the target dataset, while model routing presupposes a given candidate pool. We introduce ModelLens, a unified framework for model recommendation in the wild. Our key insight is that public leaderboard interactions, though scattered and noisy, collectively trace out an implicit atlas of model capabilities across heterogeneous evaluation settings, a signal rich enough to learn from directly. By learning a performance-aware latent space over model--dataset--metric tuples, ModelLens ranks unseen models on unseen datasets without running candidates on the target dataset. On a new benchmark of 1.62M evaluation records spanning 47K models and 9.6K datasets, ModelLens surpasses baselines that either rely on metadata alone or require running each candidate on the target dataset. Its recommended Top-K pools further improve multiple representative routing methods by up to 81% across diverse QA benchmarks. Case studies on recently released benchmarks further confirm generalization to both text and vision-language tasks.

</details>


### [108] [Test-Time Compositional Generalization in Diffusion Models via Concept Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07078)
*Zekun Wang,Anant Gupta,Tianyi Zhu,Christopher J. MacLellan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需预定义概念库的测试时组合生成方法，通过利用预训练扩散模型的时间索引分数几何，从单个查询中恢复局部密度模式并组合成专家乘积模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型的组合生成方法通常假设相关概念或条件信号已经可用，而本文探究预训练扩散模型能否在测试时从时间索引分数中自主发现查询特定概念并进行组合。

Method: 对单个OOD查询，在多个噪声时间步上对分数函数进行梯度上升以恢复局部密度模式；将这些模式映射到干净空间的高斯分布；通过次模似然目标贪心选择相关原型；组合成具有解析分数的专家乘积教师模型；可直接通过无分类器引导采样或用于训练新类嵌入和低秩适配器。

Result: 在ColorMNIST和CelebA的留出组合基准上，解析PoE采样器和低秩适配模型均优于仅查询基线和最近训练类基线。

Conclusion: 扩散模型的时间索引分数几何包含可复用的密度模式概念，支持无需预定义概念库的测试时组合生成。

Abstract: Compositional generalization requires models to produce novel configurations from familiar parts. In diffusion models, prior compositional generation methods typically assume that the relevant concepts or conditioning signals are already available. We instead ask whether a pretrained diffusion model can discover query-specific concepts from the time-indexed scores it learns for the noisy marginals $p_t(x_t)$ and compose them at test time. Given a single out-of-distribution query, our method performs gradient ascent on $s_θ(x_t,t) \approx \nabla_{x_t}\log p_t(x_t)$ at multiple noising timesteps to recover local density modes, maps these modes into clean-space Gaussians, greedily selects relevant prototypes with a submodular likelihood objective, and combines them into a product-of-experts (PoE) teacher model with an analytic score. This teacher model can be sampled directly through classifier-free guidance or used to generate a sample pool for training a new class embedding and low-rank adapter. On held-out composition benchmarks built from ColorMNIST and CelebA, both the analytic PoE sampler and the low-rank adapted model outperform query-only and nearest trained-class baselines. These results suggest that the time-indexed score geometry of the diffusion model contains reusable density-mode concepts that support test-time compositional generation without a predefined concept library.

</details>


### [109] [Actor-Critic with Active Importance Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07094)
*Majid Molaei,Gabor Paczolay,Matteo Papini,Alberto Maria Metelli,Marcello Restelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AISAC算法，通过主动重要性采样优化行为策略以降低Actor-Critic框架中的策略梯度估计方差，在连续动作空间中采用高斯行为策略并通过交叉熵最小化进行优化。理论分析证明了方差缩减与无偏性，在倒立摆和Half Cheetah任务上的实验表明，该方法相比标准Actor-Critic在学习速度、样本效率和训练稳定性上均有提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Actor-Critic方法在策略梯度估计中存在高方差问题，导致学习不稳定、样本效率低下。现有方法缺乏对行为策略的系统性优化来降低梯度估计方差，因此需要一种能够自适应调整数据采集分布以减小梯度方差同时保持估计无偏性的算法。

Method: AISAC算法在Actor-Critic框架中引入主动重要性采样机制，将行为策略作为可优化参数，以最小化目标策略梯度估计的方差为目标进行优化，同时通过重要性采样保证无偏性。在连续动作空间中，采用高斯分布作为行为策略族，通过最小化与最优行为策略的交叉熵来迭代更新行为策略参数。

Result: 在Inverted Pendulum和Half Cheetah任务上的实验表明，AISAC相比标准Actor-Critic方法在收敛速度、样本效率和训练稳定性方面均表现更优。优化行为策略不仅改善了目标策略的更新质量，还提升了Critic的估计精度，且在不同超参数设置下均保持优势。

Conclusion: AISAC通过主动优化行为策略实现方差缩减，有效加速了强化学习的收敛并提升了训练稳定性，具有实际应用潜力。未来工作将探索与SAC、TD3等先进算法的集成，以应对更复杂的环境。

Abstract: This paper introduces the Active-Importance-Sampling Actor-Critic (AISAC) algorithm, an extension of the Actor-Critic framework for reducing variance in policy gradient estimation. AISAC optimizes the behavior policy to minimize gradient variance while preserving unbiased gradient estimates. Using importance sampling principles, the algorithm adapts the behavior policy toward efficient data collection distributions aligned with target policy gradients.
  For continuous action spaces, AISAC employs Gaussian behavior policies optimized through cross-entropy minimization. We provide theoretical analysis demonstrating variance reduction and unbiasedness. Experiments on Inverted Pendulum and Half Cheetah tasks show improved learning speed, sample efficiency, and training stability compared to standard Actor-Critic methods.
  Results indicate that optimizing the behavior policy improves both target policy updates and critic estimation accuracy across different hyperparameter settings. AISAC accelerates convergence and stabilizes reinforcement learning training, making it promising for real-world applications. Future work includes integration with advanced algorithms such as Soft Actor-Critic and TD3 for more complex environments.

</details>


### [110] [Query-efficient model evaluation using cached responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07096)
*Hayden Helm,Ben Johnson,Carey Priebe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于数据核视角空间（DKPS）的方法，利用已评估模型的缓存响应来预测新模型在基准测试中的性能，显著降低查询预算的同时保持相同预测精度，并提出了离线查询选择策略以进一步提升预测准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代评估框架中，对新模型进行完整基准测试成本过高；而实践中已有模型的响应结果通常被缓存，如何利用这些缓存信息来减少新模型评估所需的查询数量是一个有价值但尚未充分研究的问题。

Method: 提出数据核视角空间（DKPS）方法，在黑盒设置下量化模型间关系；基于DKPS从缓存响应中预测新模型的基准性能；提出离线查询选择方法，通过最大化参考模型上的拟合优度来优化查询集。

Result: 理论上证明了DKPS方法在特定条件下的查询高效性；实验上，DKPS方法在用更少查询预算的情况下达到了与基线方法相同的平均绝对误差；离线查询选择相比随机选择提升了预测准确性。

Conclusion: DKPS框架能够有效利用缓存模型响应来减少新模型评估所需的查询数量，具有理论保障和实证验证，为高效模型评估提供了新思路。

Abstract: Evaluating a new model on an existing benchmark is often necessary to understand its behavior before deployment. For modern evaluation frameworks, generating and evaluating a response for all queries can be prohibitively expensive. In practice, responses from previously-evaluated models are often cached -- creating a potential opportunity to use this additional information to decrease the number of queries required to accurately evaluate a new model. In this paper, we introduce an approach for predicting benchmark performance that leverages cached model responses based on the Data Kernel Perspective Space (DKPS), a method for quantifying the relationship between models in the black-box setting. Theoretically, we show that DKPS-based methods are query-efficient under certain conditions. Empirically, we demonstrate that DKPS-based methods achieve the same mean absolute error as baselines with a substantially decreased query budget. We conclude by proposing an offline method for selecting a set of queries that maximizes the goodness-of-fit on reference models, improving prediction accuracy over random query selection.

</details>
