The method requires no labels, is nearly parameter-free, white-box interpretable, has linear complexity O(|E|), avoids cold-start issues, and its detection logic possesses the "open-hand" property: attackers cannot evade it even when fully informed.
  We validate the approach on two real fraud incidents in backtests. In the promotion abuse case, a single near-zero-cost weak signal (global Precision of only 16%) after structural amplification achieves Precision above 91% and Recall exceeding 99% (z=10.0); at a higher threshold (z=40.0), Precision reaches 93.7%. In the credit card fraud case, an infrastructure-layer weak signal (device spoofing) successfully detects payment-layer attacks without any business-logic linkage, revealing the framework's natural MO-agnostic property: it relies more on the structural invariant than on signal semantics.

</details>


### [223] [Forensic analysis of video data deletion and recovery in Honeywell surveillance file system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07430)
*Jinhee Yoon,Sungjae Hwang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究分析了霍尼韦尔视频监控设备中未文档化的专有文件系统，采用二进制差异技术研究了三种删除机制（格式化、数据过期、覆写），并证明了视频数据恢复的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频监控设备使用专有且未文档化的文件系统，这极大增加了法医分析的时间和成本。霍尼韦尔设备的文件系统尚未被研究，填补这一空白对取证调查至关重要。

Method: 采用二进制差异技术对未文档化的专有文件系统进行逆向分析，针对目标设备支持的三种删除方法（格式化删除、数据过期、覆写）分别研究文件系统元数据和磁盘数据结构的变更。

Result: 成功分析了该文件系统的结构，揭示了三种删除机制在元数据和数据结构层面的变化特征，并验证了视频数据在删除后均可恢复。

Conclusion: 该研究为霍尼韦尔监控产品的法医调查提供了更高效准确的支撑，并为视频记录设备中专有文件系统的分析提供了基础性见解。

Abstract: Real-time video surveillance systems store recorded video using digital video recorders (DVRs) and network video recorders (NVRs). To support continuous high-volume video storage, these devices employ specialized, nonstandard file systems that are often proprietary and undocumented. This lack of documentation significantly increases the time and effort required for forensic analysis. In this study, we analyze an undocumented proprietary file system used by Honeywell video surveillance devices-one that, to the best of our knowledge, has not been examined in prior work-and investigate its deletion mechanisms and demonstrate the feasibility of video recovery after deletion. We perform a file system analysis using a binary diffing technique and evaluate three deletion methods supported by the target device: 1) formatting-based deletion, 2) data expiration, and 3) overwrite. For each method, we investigate changes in file system metadata and on-disk data structures and demonstrate the feasibility of video data recovery. Our findings aim to support more efficient and accurate forensic investigations of Honeywell surveillance products and provide foundational insights into the analysis of proprietary file systems used in video recording devices.

</details>


### [224] [Vaporizer: Breaking Watermarking Schemes for Large Language Model Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07481)
*Jonathan Hong Jin Ng,Anh Tu Ngo,Anupam Chattopadhyay*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了当前最先进的大语言模型输出水印方案，通过多种文本修改攻击（包括词汇替换、机器翻译和神经改写）测试其鲁棒性，发现所有水印均可被合理努力去除，揭示了现有系统的弱点并提出了改进方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估现有LLM输出水印技术在实际攻击下的鲁棒性，揭示其安全漏洞，以促进更可靠的水印方案设计。

Method: 采用多种对抗攻击策略（词汇修改、机器翻译、神经改写）对水印文本进行攻击，使用BERT分数、文本复杂度、语法错误和Flesch阅读易读性指数等指标评估语义保留程度，同时检测水印移除成功率。

Result: 不同水印方案表现出不同级别的有效性，但核心结论是：通过合理努力可以移除水印。

Conclusion: 现有LLM水印系统存在安全弱点，需要改进构建方式以提升可用方案的安全性。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the recent state-of-the-art schemes for watermarking large language models (LLMs) outputs. These techniques are claimed to be robust, scalable and production-grade, aimed at promoting responsible usage of LLMs. We analyse the effectiveness of these watermarking techniques against an extensive collection of modified text attacks, which perform targeted semantic changes without altering the general meaning of the text content. Our approach encompasses multiple attack strategies, which include lexical alterations, machine translation, and even neural paraphrasing. The attack efficacy is measured with two target criteria - successful removal of the watermark and preservation of semantic content. We evaluate semantic preservation through BERT scores, text complexity measures, grammatical errors, and Flesch Reading Ease indices. The experimental results reveal varying levels of effectiveness among different watermarking models, with the same underlying result that it is possible to remove the watermark with reasonable effort. This study sheds light on the strengths and weaknesses of existing LLM watermarking systems, suggesting how they should be constructed to improve security of available schemes.

</details>


### [225] [Spying Across Chiplets: Side-Channel Attacks in 2.5/3D Integrated Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07486)
*Giorgio Di Natale,Christelle Rabache,Pierre-Louis Hellier,Florence Podevin,Sylvain Bourdel,Romain Siragusa,Paolo Maistri*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一种新型侧信道攻击：封装内的通信接口芯片（如天线、RFID等）可被恶意重用作内部观测平台，窃取相邻受害者芯片的敏感信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着先进封装和芯粒集成技术的广泛采用，异构系统在超越单片缩放极限的同时引入了新的物理攻击面，需要揭示和评估这些威胁。

Method: 形式化了合理的 adversary 模型，描述了利用通信导向芯粒（如天线/RFID）作为内部观测平台的攻击原理，并通过实验评估其可行性。

Result: 实验结果证实，通过这种通信导向接口捕获的信号能够揭示与相邻受害者芯粒活动相关的信息。

Conclusion: 芯粒间侧信道攻击是可行的，通信接口可被重用作内部窃听平台，对先进封装系统的安全性构成实质性威胁。

Abstract: Advanced packaging and chiplet-based integration are increasingly adopted to build complex heterogeneous systems beyond the limits of monolithic scaling. While these architectures offer major benefits in terms of modularity, yield, and performance, they also introduce new physical attack surfaces. In this paper, we show that side-channel attacks can be mounted across chiplets within the same package or stack. Our key idea is that a communication-oriented chiplet, originally intended to interact with the external environment through an antenna, an RFID-like element, or another contactless coupling structure, can be repurposed as an internal observation platform. We formalize this threat through a realistic adversary model, describe the corresponding attack principle, and experimentally assess its feasibility. The obtained results demonstrate that signals captured through such a communication-oriented interface can reveal information correlated with the activity of a neighboring victim chiplet.

</details>


### [226] [Cross-Modal Backdoors in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07490)
*Runhe Wang,Li Bai,Haibo Hu,Songze Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对多模态大语言模型（MLLM）中连接器（connector）的跨模态后门攻击。仅通过单个种子样本及其增强变体污染连接器，即可实现跨模态后门激活，攻击成功率达95%以上，且具有高隐蔽性，现有防御手段难以有效缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全研究主要关注编码器或大语言模型等骨干组件的投毒攻击，而轻量级连接器（connector）的安全风险尚未被探索。开发者通过组装预训练组件构建MLLM引入了供应链攻击面，连接器作为关键但被忽视的环节存在安全漏洞。

Method: 1) 使用单个种子样本及其增强变体污染连接器，使其将紧凑的潜在区域与恶意目标输出关联；2) 从被污染的潜在表示中提取恶意质心（malicious centroid）；3) 通过输入侧优化将输入导向该潜在锚点，无需重复API查询或完整模型访问。

Result: 在PandaGPT和NExT-GPT等代表性连接器架构上，同模态设置下攻击成功率达99.9%，跨模态设置下超95.0%。攻击具有高隐蔽性：干净输入泄露可忽略，权重余弦相似度保持在0.97以上。现有防御策略无法在不显著降低效用的情况下有效缓解该威胁。

Conclusion: 揭示了多模态对齐中的根本性漏洞：单个被污染的连接器即可建立可复用的跨模态潜在空间后门通路，强调了更安全的模块化MLLM设计的必要性。

Abstract: Developers increasingly construct multimodal large language models (MLLMs) by assembling pretrained components,introducing supply-chain attack surfaces.Existing security research primarily focuses on poisoning backbones such as encoders or large language models (LLMs),while the security risks of lightweight connectors remain unexplored.In this work,we propose a novel cross-modal backdoor attack that exploits this overlooked vulnerability.By poisoning only the connector using a single seed sample and several augmented variants from one modality,the adversary can subsequently activate the backdoor using inputs from other modalities.To achieve this,we first poison the connector to associate a compact latent region with a malicious target output.To activate the backdoor from other modalities,we further extract a malicious centroid from the poisoned latent representations and perform input-side optimization to steer inputs toward this latent anchor,without requiring repeated API queries or full-model access.Extensive evaluations on representative connector-based MLLM architectures,including PandaGPT and NExT-GPT,demonstrate both the effectiveness and cross-modal transferability of the proposed attack.The attack achieves up to 99.9% attack success rate (ASR) in same-modality settings,while most cross-modal settings exceed 95.0% ASR under bounded perturbations.Moreover,the attack remains highly stealthy,producing negligible leakage on clean inputs,and maintaining weight-cosine similarity above 0.97 relative to benign connectors.We further show that existing defense strategies fail to effectively mitigate this threat without incurring substantial utility degradation.These findings reveal a fundamental vulnerability in multimodal alignment: a single compromised connector can establish a reusable latent-space backdoor pathway across modalities,highlighting the need for safer modular MLLM design.

</details>


### [227] [Can I Check What I Designed? Mapping Security Design DSLs to Code Analyzers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07814)
*Sven Peldszus,Frederik Reiche,Kevin Hermann,Sophie Corallo,Thorsten Berger,Robert Heinrich*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了66个设计级安全DSL和36个代码级分析器的559个安全检查，发现设计与实现之间存在巨大鸿沟，安全概念缺乏共性，专家也难以应对这种复杂性，并提出了SecLan模型作为弥合这一鸿沟的第一步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码级漏洞（如自动化分析器发现的）需要结合系统的安全设计上下文来评估影响，但由于安全设计（通常使用安全DSL）与实现之间存在抽象鸿沟，这一任务极具挑战性，即使是安全专家对此关系也缺乏完整理解。

Method: 对66个设计级安全DSL和36个代码级分析器的559个安全检查进行系统性研究，识别共同概念并建模为SecLan模型，经22位安全专家验证；再结合9位安全专家进行定量和定性分析。

Result: 设计级与实现级安全之间共同点很少；安全检查常以过于泛化的弱点描述，导致DSL与分析器之间存在大量非显而易见的潜在关系；安全专家对此复杂性也感到难以应对。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解安全设计与安全实现之间的鸿沟提供了实证基础，是迈向弥合这一鸿沟的第一步。

Abstract: When assessing the potential impact of code-level vulnerabilities, e.g., discovered by automated analyzers, it is essential to consider them in the context of the system's security design. However, this is a challenging task due to the abstraction gap between security design, often specified using security DSLs, and implementation. As we will show, even security experts lack a complete understanding of this relationship. Intrigued by this gap (and the general disconnect between secure design and secure implementation) we present a study of 66 design-level security DSLs and 559 security checks from 36 code-level analyzers. We identify what concepts are common to both and capture them in the SecLan model, which has been validated by 22 security experts. Based on this, we investigate the relationship between DSLs and analyzers quantitatively and explore it qualitatively together with 9 security experts. We learn that there are few commonalities between design-level and implementation-level security; security checks are often described by overly general weaknesses, resulting in many non-obvious potential relationships between security DSLs and analyzers; and even security experts are overwhelmed by this complexity. We provide an empirical basis that helps practitioners and researchers better understand the gap and serves as a first step toward bridging it.

</details>


### [228] [CCX: Enabling Unmodified Intel SGX Applications on Arm CCA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07548)
*Matti Schulze,Thorsten Holz,Felix Freiling*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CCX是一个框架，能让现有SGX应用无需修改源代码即可在Arm CCA上运行，通过重新设计SGX功能适配CCA架构，在提供可比安全性同时实现性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着Arm CPU在云系统中的广泛应用，现有SGX应用因与SGX特定API和执行语义紧密耦合而面临严重的可移植性挑战，亟需一种方案使其能在Arm CCA上运行。

Method: CCX在Arm CCA固件中重新设计SGX功能，将SGX抽象适配到CCA的架构设计，保持与现有SGX应用的完全兼容性，无需修改源代码。

Result: 在QEMU模拟器和Nitrogen8M开发板上实现的原型验证表明，CCX能够执行现有SGX应用而无需源代码修改，性能优于原SGX实现。

Conclusion: CCX成功解决了SGX应用向Arm CCA可移植性的问题，提供了可与Intel SGX相比的安全性保证，并在评估设置中实现了性能提升。

Abstract: Novel confidential computing technologies such as Intel TDX, AMD SEV, and Arm CCA have recently emerged. In practice, due to its minimal trust boundaries, Intel SGX still remains widely used for enclave-based applications in cloud environments, including confidential cloud services, privacy-preserving communication, secure payment processing, and privacy-focused advertising. With the growing adoption of Arm CPUs in cloud systems, however, existing SGX applications face a significant portability challenge: they are tightly coupled to SGX-specific APIs and execution semantics.
  In this paper, we present the design and implementation of CCX, a framework that enables existing SGX applications to run on Arm CCA without source code modification. To this end, CCX redesigns SGX functionality within Arm CCA firmware, adapting SGX abstractions to CCA's architecture design while preserving full compatibility with existing applications originally developed for SGX. We implemented a prototype of CCX on both the QEMU emulator and a Nitrogen8M development board. Our evaluation shows that CCX is capable of executing existing SGX applications without requiring source code changes, while providing security guarantees comparable to Intel SGX and achieving performance improvements in our evaluated settings.

</details>


### [229] [GRASP -- Graph-Based Anomaly Detection Through Self-Supervised Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07812)
*Robin Buchta,Carsten Kleiner,Felix Heine,Gabi Dreo Rodosek*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: GRASP是一种基于掩码自监督分类的溯源入侵检测系统，通过学习进程可执行文件的双跳溯源图邻域模式来检测异常行为，无需阈值设定，在DARPA TC和OpTC数据集上优于现有系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有溯源入侵检测系统(PIDS)依赖预定义或子集确定的阈值，限制了检测稳定性；同时相关工作忽视了进程可执行文件在描述系统活动中的作用。

Method: GRASP采用掩码自监督分类方法，遮蔽进程可执行文件信息，通过学习其双跳溯源图邻域来推断被遮蔽信息，将分类错误的进程标记为异常。

Result: 在DARPA TC和OpTC数据集上，GRASP持续检测到异常行为，包括已知攻击活动，优于现有系统；在可执行文件行为可学习的数据集上识别了所有已记录的攻击。

Conclusion: GRASP无需阈值设定即可捕获已学习可执行文件的行为模式，对干扰和未知活动具有鲁棒性，并且能发现未被标注为攻击的潜在恶意异常行为。

Abstract: Advanced persistent threat (APT) attacks remain difficult to detect due to their stealth, adaptability, and use of legitimate system components. Provenance-based intrusion detection systems (PIDS) offer a promising defense by capturing detailed relationships between system components and actions. However, current PIDS rely on predefined or subset-determined thresholds, which limit detection stability and the ability to detect any anomalous behavior in general. Furthermore, related work often neglects the role of process executables, which describe system activity by interacting through a process with files, network components, and other processes. We introduce GRASP, a PIDS based on masked self-supervised classification. GRASP masks the executable information of processes and learns to infer it from their two-hop provenance graph neighborhood, marking misclassified processes as anomalies. It captures behavior patterns for the learned executables without thresholding, making it robust against interference and unknown activities. Evaluations on the DARPA TC and OpTC datasets demonstrate that GRASP consistently detects anomalous behavior, including known attack-related activities, outperforming existing systems. Our PIDS identifies all documented attacks on datasets where the behavior of executables is learnable. In addition, compared to existing systems, GRASP uncovers potentially malicious anomalous behavior not labeled as an attack in the documentation.

</details>


### [230] [Longitudinal Analyses of SAST Tools: A CodeQL Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07900)
*Jean-Charles Noirot Ferrand,Kyle Domico,Yohan Beugin,Patrick McDaniel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对静态应用安全测试工具CodeQL在开源软件生态系统中的有效性进行了大规模纵向研究，分析了114个版本的CodeQL在1622个代码库的3993个CVE上的表现（超过200亿行代码），发现CodeQL共检测到171个CVE，其中83个可在修复前被检测到；约50%的检测具有可操作性；但检测结果在不同版本间不具有单调性，21个CVE在版本更新后不再被检测到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管开源软件流水线广泛依赖自动化静态分析工具来防止漏洞引入，但学术界对这些工具在整个开源生态系统中的长期有效性缺乏系统性的理解。

Method: 提出了一种通过纵向测量评估SAST工具的新方法，对114个版本的CodeQL在1622个代码库的3993个CVE上进行测试（累计分析超过200亿行代码），从有效性、可操作性和稳定性三个维度进行评估。

Result: CodeQL共检测到171个CVE，其中83个可在修复前检出；50%的检测中，漏洞文件中超过50%的发现位于易受攻击位置（可操作性强）；但21个CVE在版本更新后不再被检测到，其中17个从未被重新检测到。

Conclusion: 使用SAST工具是值得推荐的最佳实践，能有效防止大量漏洞引入，但开发者应警惕工具版本更新可能带来的检测盲区。

Abstract: Open-source software (OSS) pipelines rely on automated static analysis tools to prevent the introduction of vulnerabilities in code. However, there is limited understanding of the efficacy of these tools across the OSS ecosystem over time. In this paper, we introduce a novel method to evaluate static application security testing (SAST) tools through longitudinal measurements and perform the largest academic study of CodeQL -- the most prevalent static analysis tool from GitHub -- on OSS codebases. We apply our apparatus on 114 versions of CodeQL over time on 3993 CVEs from 1622 repositories to measure key properties of the tool, culminating in more than 20 billion lines of code analyzed. First, we measure its effectiveness, i.e., its ability to detect vulnerabilities before they are fixed. Then, we determine whether these detections were actionable through two measures of the distance between findings and vulnerability location either over the entire codebase or within the vulnerable file. Finally, we study the stability of CodeQL by examining how vulnerability detections hold across versions and the evolution of CodeQL on the accuracy-precision trade-off. We find that CodeQL identifies a total of 171 CVEs, and that for 83 of them, a CodeQL version prior to the fix could detect it. Such detections are in general actionable if findings are triaged across files, as for 50% of the 171 detections, more than 50% of findings in the vulnerable file are located in the vulnerable location. Finally, we show that CVE detections are not monotonic across versions as 21 CVEs were no longer detected following a version change and 17 that were never redetected. Our study shows that using SAST tools is a matter of best practice as they prevent numerous vulnerabilities from being introduced, but that developers should be aware of changes that may leave blind spots in detections upon updates of the tool.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [231] [Enhancing Eye Movement Biometrics for User Authentication via Continuous Gaze Offset Score Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06810)
*Hashim Aziz,Mehedi Hasan Raju,Oleg V. Komogortsev*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文研究了持续凝视偏移（continuous gaze offset）能否作为辅助生物特征提升眼动生物识别系统的性能。通过在实验室级眼动仪和VR头显两个公开数据集上评估线性和非线性融合方法，结果表明非线性融合能显著提升性能，且多任务融合进一步提高了认证效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的眼动生物识别系统虽能建模时间维度的眼动行为，但普遍忽略了持续凝视偏移这一特征，而先前证据表明该特征包含用户判别性信息。作者旨在验证将该特征与现有生物特征融合是否能提升系统性能。

Method: 在实验室级眼动仪和VR头显两个公开数据集上，针对多个任务和不同观察时长，采用线性融合与非线性融合两种方式，将持续凝视偏移特征与现有眼动生物特征进行融合，评估其对认证和识别性能的影响。

Result: 融合方法在两个数据集上均显示出性能提升，特别是非线性融合效果更优。此外，跨多个任务的生物特征融合进一步提升了认证性能。

Conclusion: 持续凝视偏移在眼动数据退化或噪声条件下可作为有用的辅助信息，支持将其纳入眼动生物识别系统的设计。

Abstract: Eye movement biometrics (EMB) use subject-specific gaze dynamics for user authentication and identification. Recent deep learning-based EMB systems achieve strong performance by modeling temporal eye movement behavior. However, these systems typically overlook continuous gaze offset, despite prior evidence that it contains user-discriminative information. This work examines whether continuous gaze offset can improve biometric performance when combined with existing biometric features. We evaluate linear and nonlinear fusion methods on two publicly available datasets, collected via the lab-grade eye tracker and virtual reality headset across multiple tasks and observation durations. Results indicate that fusion offers performance benefits on both datasets, particularly when using nonlinear fusion. Additionally, fusing biometric information across multiple tasks further improves authentication performance. These findings support the hypothesis that continuous gaze offset may serve as useful auxiliary information under conditions of degraded or noisy eye tracking.

</details>


### [232] [Privacy Perceptions in Sensor-Powered Smart Vehicle Cabins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06847)
*BoRui Li,Bofan Yu,Xing-Dong Yang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过18位参与者的半结构化访谈，探究了车主与临时用车者（家人、朋友、租车人）对智能座舱隐私的认知差异，揭示了影响两类群体隐私偏好的共同因素及差异化因素，并提出了平衡多利益相关者隐私需求的设计启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着汽车座舱集成多样化传感器并向智能环境转型，传统座舱中的隐私理解与管理方式面临挑战，且车主与临时使用者（非车主）的隐私认知差异尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 采用半结构化访谈方法，对18名参与者（涵盖车主与非车主）进行质性研究，分析影响两类群体隐私偏好的关键因素。

Result: 识别出对车主和非车主隐私偏好共同产生影响的因素，以及分别对某一群体影响更强的因素。

Conclusion: 基于研究发现的隐私认知差异，提出了未来智能座舱设计中更好支持和平衡多利益相关者隐私需求的设计启示。

Abstract: As car cabins evolve with the integration of diverse sensors, traditional car cabins are transforming into smart environments. This shift raises important questions about how privacy is understood and managed in such spaces. In this work, we investigate privacy perceptions from the perspectives of both vehicle owners (i.e., people who purchase and own cars) and non-owners (i.e., people who temporarily use cars, such as family members, friends, or renters). Through semi-structured interviews with eighteen participants, we identified key factors that influence these groups' views on privacy. Our findings reveal factors that commonly influence privacy preferences for both owners and non-owners, as well as factors that have a stronger impact on one group over the other. Drawing on these insights, we discuss design implications for future designs to better support and balance the diverse privacy needs of multiple stakeholders in smart car cabins.

</details>


### [233] [Leveraging fNIRS to Evaluate Workload for Adaptive Training in Virtual Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06909)
*Cara A. Spencer,Christopher D. Wickens,Jalynn B. Nicoly,James Crum,Benjamin A. Clegg,Joanna E. Lewis,Francisco R. Ortega,Lucas Plabst,Rebecca L. Pharmer,Leanne Hirshfield*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究验证了功能性近红外光谱（fNIRS）作为认知负荷测量工具在VR训练任务中的有效性，发现fNIRS能够区分外在负荷（仅激活右角回）和内在负荷（激活背外侧前额叶、喙侧前额叶和左角回等与工作记忆、注意力相关的脑区），且结果与NASA-TLX主观评分高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 技术进步使得VR与实时自适应训练的结合成为可能，而提供有效的神经工效学认知负荷测量指标是实现自适应训练（根据训练进展连续调整任务难度至最优水平）的关键前提。

Method: 36名被试完成VR形状组装训练任务及技能保持测试，佩戴近全头覆盖的fNIRS设备，并收集主观工作负荷评分（NASA-TLX），通过比较fNIRS脑区激活模式与主观评分的对应关系，验证fNIRS对认知负荷理论中两种负荷（外在负荷与内在负荷）的区分效度。

Result: fNIRS结果与内在负荷相关文献一致，显著激活了与工作记忆、短时记忆缓冲、多感觉整合和注意力相关的皮层区域（背外侧前额叶、喙侧前额叶和左角回）；外在负荷仅激活右角回（被认为与任务掌握无关）；fNIRS结果与NASA-TLX主观测量紧密吻合。

Conclusion: fNIRS能够有效区分认知负荷理论中的内在负荷与外在负荷，可作为VR自适应训练系统中认知负荷的有效神经工效学测量指标。

Abstract: Advance in technology offer the potential for future adoption of a combination of virtual reality (VR) and real-time adaptivity to enhance training and education. Providing a valid neuro-ergonomic measure of cognitive load can enable an adaptive training regime to continuously adjust tas difficulty to an optimal level as training progresses. The current study validated the functional near-infrared spectroscopy (fNIRS) measure of cognitive load to reflect the demands of two different forms of lad within Cognitive Load Theory: extraneous and intrinsic to he task to be mastered. Thirty-six participants completed a VR shape assembly training task followed by a test of their skill retention They wore near-full head coverage fNIRS and provided subjective ratings of ther workload. The fNIRS findings largely corroborate intrinsic workload literature with significant activation in cortical regions (dorsolateral and rostral prefrontal cortex and left angular gyrus) associated with working memory, short term memory buffers, multisensory integration, and attention. These fNIRS results were tracked closely by NASA TLS measures of mental workload. The results also revealed far less brain activity associated with extraneous load, namely just the right angular gyrus, deemed irrelevant to the mastery of the task.

</details>


### [234] [From Surface Learning to Deep Understanding: A Grounded AI Tutoring System for Moodle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06963)
*Anna Ostrowska,Michał Kukla,Gabriela Majstrak,Jan Opala,Sebastian Pergała,Jan Skwarek,Anna Wróblewska*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个基于检索增强生成（RAG）的模块化Moodle插件——AI教学与学习助手，通过双中心设计（学生端苏格拉底式辅导、教师端人在回路内容生成）实现低幻觉的AI教育支持，实验表明其忠实度得分达0.97，推荐率为4.00/5.00。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大型语言模型在教育应用中存在的幻觉和 misinformation 风险，确保AI辅助教学内容的准确性和可靠性。

Method: 采用模块化Moodle插件架构，基于检索增强生成（RAG）技术，设计双中心系统：学生端实现交互式苏格拉底式辅导，教师端提供"人在回路"（human-in-the-loop）工作空间用于监督内容生成。

Result: 通过Ragas（LLM-as-a-Judge）框架评估，忠实度得分最高达0.97；初步用户研究中推荐率为4.00/5.00。

Conclusion: 该系统能有效将大语言模型响应锚定在教师提供的材料上，在降低幻觉风险的同时促进深层概念掌握，验证了RAG技术在教育场景中的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: This demo paper describes the development of the AI Teaching \& Learning Assistant, a modular Moodle plugin that leverages Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) to deliver high-quality, hallucination-free education. The system employs a dual-centric design, providing students with interactive, Socratic-based tutoring and educators with a "human-in-the-loop" workspace for supervised content generation. By grounding Large Language Model (LLM) responses in teacher-provided materials, the assistant addresses the risks of misinformation while encouraging deep conceptual mastery. Evaluation via the Ragas (LLM-as-a-Judge) framework and a preliminary user study confirms its effectiveness, achieving faithfulness scores up to 0.97 and a 4.00/5.00 recommendation rate.

</details>


### [235] [Exploring the "Banality" of Deception in Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07012)
*Ishitaa Narwane,Johanna Gunawan,Konrad Kollnig*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文探讨生成式AI（尤其是聊天机器人）中“平庸欺骗”（banal deception）的概念，指出欺骗已从显性暗模式转向隐性、常态化的互动形式，并提出通过引入摩擦机制（提升用户意识、提供干预工具、改进监管）来保护用户。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有应对欺骗性设计的方法主要针对显性界面操纵（暗模式），但随着生成式AI崛起，欺骗变得更加隐蔽和常态化，需要新的分析视角和应对策略。

Method: 立场论文（position paper），以Natale的“平庸欺骗”框架为理论基础，分析生成式AI聊天机器人中的欺骗现象，并提出未来研究方向。

Result: 识别出欺骗已从离散的界面元素嵌入到默认设置、自动建议和对话交互中；论证用户自身在欺骗中的参与性；提出三类干预方向：用户意识提升、干预工具、监管改进。

Conclusion: “平庸性”是理解生成式AI欺骗的有效透镜；需为欺骗性设计学界引入新讨论点，通过有意识的摩擦设计来平衡AI辅助与操纵之间的界线。

Abstract: Current approaches to addressing deceptive design largely focus on visible interface manipulations, commonly referred to as "dark patterns". With the rise of generative AI, deception is becoming more difficult to spot and easier to live with, as it is quietly embedded in default settings, automated suggestions, and conversational interactions rather than discrete interface elements. These subtle, normalised forms of influence, which Simone Natale frames as "banal deception", shape everyday digital use and blur the line between AI-enabled assistance and manipulation.
  This position paper explores banality as a lens through which to reason through deception in generative AI experiences, especially with chatbots. We explore what Natale describes as users' own involvement in their deception, and argue that this perspective could lead to future work for introducing friction to safeguard users from deception in generative AI interactions, such as empowering users through raising awareness, providing them with intervention tools, and regulatory or enforcement improvements. We present these concepts as points for discussion for the deceptive design scholarly community.

</details>


### [236] [Problem Space Attunement in Youth Social Media Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07018)
*JaeWon Kim*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该博士论文指出，面向青少年的社交媒体设计存在三种问题空间失谐（概念性、定义性、评价性），并分别通过虚构探究、Discord异步社区和LLM智能体模拟沙盒三种方法进行干预，最终提出以关系支持为核心的青少年导向设计标准与方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有社交媒体平台设计对青少年的关系维护、身份发展和社区参与支持不足，青少年在使用中常感到被束缚而非被支持，而当前设计研究未能充分从青少年自身需求出发。

Method: 论文采用三种方法对应三种失谐：1）虚构探究（Fictional Inquiry）——在魔法学校虚构情境中帮助青少年从实际关系需求出发推理设计；2）基于Discord的异步社区——支持青少年主导的集体探究；3）以自我为中心的LLM智能体模拟沙盒——用于评估设计。

Result: 三项研究共同生成了以青少年自身标准为基础的关系支持型社交媒体设计准则与方向。

Conclusion: 要设计真正支持青少年的社交媒体，必须解决概念、定义和评价三个层面的问题空间失谐，让青少年成为设计过程的主体而非客体。

Abstract: Social media is central to how young people maintain relationships, develop identity, and access communities, yet dominant platform designs often leave youth feeling constrained rather than supported. My dissertation argues that youth social media design is shaped by three forms of problem-space misattunement. \textit{Conceptual misattunement} occurs when the language of ``social media'' anchors participants to existing platform templates. I address this through Fictional Inquiry in a fictional magic-school setting that helps youth reason from felt relational needs. \textit{Definitional misattunement} occurs when researchers define what ``better'' means on youth's behalf. I address this through a Discord-based asynchronous community that supports youth-led collective inquiry. \textit{Evaluative misattunement} occurs when participants are asked to judge static or hypothetical designs. I address this through an ego-anchored, LLM-agent simulation sandbox. Together, these studies develop youth-grounded criteria and design directions for relationally supportive social media.

</details>


### [237] [Social Understanding, Placeness, and Identity Alignment: A Design Framework for Friendship-Supportive Youth Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07025)
*JaeWon Kim,Alexis Hiniker*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过5项实证研究（331名13-25岁参与者），提出了支持青少年友谊的社交媒体设计框架，包含三个支柱（社会理解感、场所感、身份对齐感）和九个设计空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有社交媒体设计缺乏专门针对青少年友谊支持的系统性设计框架，需要从青少年实际需求出发构建设计指导原则。

Method: 综合五项实证研究（访谈、共同设计、问卷调查、日记研究和实地部署），对209个设计相关数据点进行迭代分析，提炼出设计框架。

Result: 提出了三个支柱：（1）社会理解感（互动规范、互动线索与脚手架、社会问责与治理）；（2）场所感（第三场所与社区、边界与个人空间、共享在场）；（3）身份对齐感（身份货币、身份多元性、关系身份信号）。对应九个设计空间。

Conclusion: 该框架为支持青少年友谊形成的社交媒体设计提供了共享词汇，可用于定位贡献、比较设计干预措施及识别未来研究方向。

Abstract: We present a design framework for friendship-supportive youth social media, derived from a synthesis of five empirical studies with 331 youth participants (ages 13--25) using interviews, co-design, surveys, diary studies, and a field deployment. Iterative analysis of 209 design-relevant data points identified three pillars: \textit{Sense of Social Understanding} (interaction norms, interaction cues and scaffolding, social accountability and governance), \textit{Sense of Place} (third place and community, boundaries and personal spaces, shared presence), and \textit{Sense of Identity Alignment} (identity currency, identity plurality, relational identity signals). The framework maps nine design spaces through which platforms can support the conditions under which youth friendships form, deepen, and are maintained. It offers a shared vocabulary for locating contributions, comparing design interventions, and identifying under-explored areas for future work.

</details>


### [238] [From Standard English to Singlish: A Retrieval-Augmented Approach for Code-Switched Creole Generation in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07132)
*Foong Ming Lai,Yujin Tan,Han Meng,Yi-Chieh Lee*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出一种检索增强生成（RAG）框架，通过外部化词典知识实现新加坡英语（Singlish）的可控词汇级语码转换，无需微调即可在保持语义的同时进行最小编辑，达到与零样本提示同等的自然度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Singlish等接触变体语言缺乏平行语料且词汇快速演变，现有NLG方法难以实现可控的词汇级语码转换。

Method: 构建RAG框架，外部化方言知识为精选词典，通过稀疏词汇替换检索候选Singlish表达并引导生成。

Result: 零样本提示平均编辑23个tokens，RAG仅编辑1个token；RAG语义余弦相似度0.978 vs 0.926；164名新加坡人评估显示两者自然度和恰当性无显著差异。

Conclusion: 外部化语码转换为词汇资源可在不牺牲感知质量的前提下实现可控性和可审计性，对快速演变的接触变体语言具有实用优势。

Abstract: Code-switching in contact varieties like Singaporean English (Singlish) challenges natural language generation due to limited parallel data and rapid lexical evolution. We propose a retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) framework that externalizes dialectal knowledge into a curated lexicon, enabling controlled lexical code-switching without fine-tuning. Our approach retrieves candidate Singlish expressions and guides generation through sparse lexical substitution. Human evaluation with 164 Singaporean participants found RAG and zero-shot prompting equally natural and appropriate. Automatic analyses reveal different transformation regimes: zero-shot prompting induces extensive paraphrasing (median 23 token edits), whereas RAG performs minimal substitutions (median 1 edit) with higher semantic preservation (mean cosine similarity 0.978 vs. 0.926). Our results demonstrate that externalizing code-switching into lexical resources enables control and auditability without sacrificing perceived quality, offering practical advantages for rapidly evolving contact varieties.

</details>


### [239] [Metaphors as Scaffolds: Spatial, Embodied, Fantastical, and Relational Framings for Youth Usable Privacy Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07185)
*JaeWon Kim,Alexis Hiniker*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过三项青年（13-24岁）隐私研究的跨项目分析，考察了四种隐喻（空间、具身、幻想、关系）如何影响年轻用户对隐私互动的推理，并论证隐喻选择是青年隐私设计中的首要伦理决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流可用隐私设计将隐私简化为行政性工作（设置、开关、同意复选框），脱离了青少年理解披露行为的关联性、情境性和具身性框架，亟需契合青年认知模式的隐私交互设计方法。

Method: 对三项涉及13-24岁青年的先前研究进行跨项目阅读分析，系统考察空间隐喻、具身隐喻、幻想隐喻和关系隐喻四种隐喻类型对青年隐私推理的影响机制。

Result: 空间隐喻降低认知负荷；具身隐喻提供可协商的共享道德词汇；幻想隐喻将隐私管理重构为可发现的游戏，提升精细控制参与度；关系隐喻在亲密感掩盖机构数据流动时可能导致青年越过自身设定的边界，该风险在AI伴侣产品中已显现。

Conclusion: 隐喻选择应被理解为青年隐私设计中的第一阶伦理设计决策，直接影响用户对隐私互动的理解和行为后果。

Abstract: Mainstream usable privacy design frames privacy as administrative work -- settings, toggles, consent checkboxes -- abstracted from the relational, contextual, and embodied registers in which youth reason about disclosure. Drawing on a cross-project reading of three prior studies with youth aged 13--24, we examine how the metaphors that scaffold a privacy interaction shape the reasoning young users bring to it. \textit{Spatial} metaphors reduce cognitive load by recruiting intuitions about navigating physical space. \textit{Embodied} metaphors furnish a shared moral vocabulary that makes implicit norms about public and private space negotiable among users. \textit{Fantastical} metaphors recast privacy management as discoverable play, raising engagement with the granular controls that nuanced self-presentation requires. \textit{Relational} metaphors, by contrast, can lead youth past their own stated boundaries when felt intimacy masks institutional data flow, a risk already visible in AI companion products. Metaphor selection, we argue, is best understood as a first-order ethical design decision for youth privacy.

</details>


### [240] [Splitting User Stories Into Tasks with AI -- A Foe or an Ally?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07320)
*Luka Pavlič,Reinhard Bernsteiner,Stephan Schlögl,Christian Ploder*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过对照实验研究了生成式AI（GitLab Duo）在敏捷开发中辅助用户故事拆分为任务的效果，发现当前AI工具虽不足以完全替代开发者，但能生成更细粒度任务清单并防止遗漏；参与者更偏好AI与传统方法结合的混合方式，强调AI工具需在人工监督下使用以过滤无关任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 敏捷开发中将用户故事拆分为可执行任务是一个关键但耗时的过程，现有研究缺乏对生成式AI辅助任务拆分效果的实证评估。

Method: 采用对照实验设计，比较传统任务拆分方法与基于GitLab Duo的AI辅助方法，收集并分析参与者的任务拆分结果和偏好反馈。

Result: AI工具能够生成更细粒度的任务列表并帮助避免重要任务遗漏，但存在输出无关任务的问题，当前成熟度不足以完全替代人工。

Conclusion: 生成式AI可作为敏捷任务拆分中的辅助工具，但需人工监督；混合方法（AI辅助+传统方法）是当前实践中更优的策略。

Abstract: In agile software development, breaking down user stories into actionable tasks is a critical yet time-consuming process. This paper investigates the potential of Generative AI tools to assist in task splitting, aiming to enhance planning efficiency. We conducted a controlled experiment comparing traditional task-splitting methods with AI-assisted approaches using GitLab Duo. Our findings indicate that while current AI tools are not yet mature enough to replace developers, they can aid in generating more granular task lists and ensuring no important tasks are overlooked. Participants favored a hybrid approach, combining AI tools with conventional methods to maintain high accuracy in planning. This study highlights the potential benefits and limitations of integrating Generative AI into agile development processes, suggesting that AI tools can serve as valuable aids in task splitting, provided there is human oversight to filter out irrelevant tasks.

</details>


### [241] [Analyzing Human Heuristics and Strategies in Everyday Decision-Making Conversations for Conversational AI Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07789)
*Sora Kang,Soyun Jeon,Jinsu Eun,Kwangwon Lee,Chaerin Song,Minyoung Joo,Joonhwan Lee*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过对955组真实韩语对话（15,476句话）的分析，研究发现人类在做决策时优先使用满意策略而非优化策略，并且存在频率-效率错配：最常见的启发式方法维持对话流畅，而少见的规则型策略则高效推动决策达成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话AI主要依赖数据驱动推理，而非人类在自然对话中使用的启发式和交互策略，缺乏基于真实人类实践的实证基础。

Method: 收集955组涉及食物和旅行决策的真实韩语对话（共15,476句话），借助LLM辅助编码流程，基于决策制定编码手册进行分析。

Result: 发现人类优先采用满意策略而非优化策略，依赖内部知识和交互策略管理认知负荷；识别出频率-效率错配现象——最常见的启发式方法维持探索阶段的对话流畅，而稀有的规则型策略在利用阶段高效推动决策解决。

Conclusion: 通过映射这些模式在人类-AI交互中的迁移，该工作为认知决策理论提供了实证基础，并提出了使AI系统与人类启发式过程对齐的设计启示。

Abstract: Conversational AI increasingly supports everyday decision-making, yet most systems rely on data-centric reasoning rather than the heuristic and interactional strategies people use in natural conversation. To ground design in actual human practice, we analyze 955 real-world Korean conversations (15,476 utterances) involving food and travel decisions, applying a decision-making codebook through an LLM-assisted coding pipeline. Our findings reveal that people prioritize satisficing over optimization, relying heavily on internal knowledge and interactional strategies to manage cognitive load. Critically, we identify a frequency-efficiency mismatch: the most prevalent heuristics sustain conversational flow during exploration, whereas infrequent, rule-based strategies are highly effective at driving resolution during exploitation. By mapping how these patterns transfer across the spectrum of human-AI interaction, this work provides empirical grounding consistent with cognitive theories of decision-making and offers design implications that align AI systems with human heuristic processes.

</details>


### [242] [A Spatial Knowledge Acquisition Comparison Between Digital Visual Thematic Maps, Non-Visual Interactive Text Thematic Maps, and Tables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07849)
*Brandon Biggs,Christopher Toth,James M. Coughlan,Bruce N. Walker*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究比较了视觉地图、交互式文本地图（ITM）和表格在传递空间信息方面的效果。20名 sighted 和20名盲人或低视力参与者分别使用三种表征方式完成数字、地理和综合问题。结果显示，地图类表征（视觉和ITM）在地理问题上显著优于表格，而数字问题差异不大。sighted参与者在视觉地图和ITM之间无显著差异。参与者偏好地图表征，但ITM的感知工作量最高。结论指出符合WCAG标准的ITM可提供空间信息访问，挑战了当前推荐无地理信息表格作为地图替代的可访问性实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字地图广泛用于传达空间信息，但当前可访问性实践中常使用缺乏地理信息的表格作为地图替代方案。作者质疑这种做法的有效性，旨在探究交互式文本地图（ITM）是否能比表格更好地为盲人和低视力人群提供空间信息访问。

Method: 共40名参与者（20名视力正常，20名盲人或低视力），使用视觉地图、交互式文本地图（ITM）和表格三种表征方式完成数字、地理及数字-地理综合问题。测量指标包括任务表现、偏好和NASA-TLX工作负载。

Result: 地图类表征（视觉地图和ITM）在地理相关问题上的表现显著优于表格；数字问题上差异较小。视力正常参与者在视觉地图与ITM之间的地理问题表现无显著差异。参与者更偏好地图表征。ITM的感知工作负载最高，视觉地图居中，表格最低。

Conclusion: 符合WCAG标准的交互式文本地图（ITM）能够提供空间信息访问，而表格不能。这挑战了当前推荐表格作为地图替代的可访问性做法，并引发对豁免数字专题地图的可访问性立法的重新思考。

Abstract: Digital maps are used to communicate generalized spatial information and relationships, yet are commonly made "accessible" using tables that lack geographic information. This study examines whether these tables and interactive text maps (ITMs) may be comparable to visual maps. Twenty sighted and 20 blind and low-vision individuals (BLVIs) performed tasks designed to compare visual maps, ITMs, and tables. Participants answered numeric, geographic, and combined numeric geographic questions using each representation, and performance, preference, and NASA-TLX were measured. Across both participant groups, map representations (visual and ITMs) significantly outperformed tables on geographic-based questions, while performance differences were minimal for numeric questions. For sighted participants, performance on geographic questions did not significantly differ between visual maps and ITMs, indicating that a larger powered study may find an "equivalent purpose" across these two conditions. Participants preferred map-based representations over tables. Perceived workload was highest for the ITM, intermediate for the visual map, and lowest for the table. Consistent with the Map Equivalent Purpose Framework, these findings indicate that Web Content Accessibility Guidelines-compliant ITMs can provide access to spatial information, unlike tables. These findings challenge prevailing accessibility practice that recommends tables lacking geographic information as map alternatives, and motivate reconsideration of accessibility legislation exempting digital thematic maps.

</details>


### [243] [A Roadmap of Mixed Reality Body Doubling for Adults with ADHD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07851)
*Valerie Tan,Kimberly Hegemann,Jens Gerken*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文针对成人ADHD患者使用的"身体陪护"（Body Doubling）自我管理技术，提出了一个包含12个维度的框架，涵盖个体动机、陪护主体、交互方式、情境因素和效能，并指出现有研究在混合现实原型、交互性及实证研究方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成人ADHD患者采用身体陪护技术来帮助启动和完成任务，但该技术缺乏系统性理解，作者希望建立统一框架以指导未来研究。

Method: 通过文献梳理与分析，构建了一个包含12个维度的身体陪护框架，涵盖个体动机、陪护主体维度、交互维度、情境维度和效能维度。

Result: 该框架揭示了现有研究空白：混合现实原型有限、身体陪护的交互性不足、缺乏针对身体陪护与成人ADHD的实证研究。

Conclusion: 该框架为理解和研究身体陪护技术提供了系统化的理论基础，指出了未来需要探索的方向，包括开发混合现实原型和开展更多实证研究。

Abstract: Adults with ADHD may use a self-management technique known as Body Doubling, in which the participant employs the presence of one or more agents as a means of initiating and completing tasks. We developed a framework on body doubling with twelve dimensions to better understand the characteristics of body doubling and discover future research directions for developing and testing body doubling for adults with ADHD. Our framework accounts for individual motivation, agent-related dimensions, interaction related dimensions, contextual dimensions, and efficacy. These dimensions show existing research gaps such as limited mixed reality prototypes, possibilities for more interactive body doubles, and the need for empirical studies to further understand of body doubling and adults with ADHD.

</details>


### [244] [SpatialPrompt: XR-Based Spatial Intent Expression as Executable Constraints for AI Generative 3D Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07894)
*Yichen Andy Yu,Wanru Li,Qiaoran Wang,Jymon Ross,Gavin Johnson,Mandy Lui,Qiao Jin*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出了SpatialPrompt，一个扩展现实系统，将空间草图转化为可执行约束条件，用于可控3D生成。用户使用3D笔绘制粗略结构并添加语音提示，支持迭代优化和同步协作。在Apple Vision Pro上实现，启发式评估表明工作流直观且支持协作理解，但需要更快的生成速度和更清晰的反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D生成方法难以直观地表达用户对空间结构和语义风格的精确控制需求，尤其缺乏支持协作创作和迭代式细化的XR交互方式。

Method: 通过XR系统将3D空间草图与语音提示相结合，将草图结构作为可执行的几何约束输入3D生成模型，同时利用语音传达语义和风格意图。系统支持多用户同步共创，并通过颜色编码区分贡献者。

Result: 启发式评估显示工作流直观、支持协作中的共享理解，但存在生成速度慢和反馈不够清晰的问题。

Conclusion: SpatialPrompt验证了空间草图作为3D生成约束的可行性，但需在生成效率和用户反馈机制上进一步优化。

Abstract: We present SpatialPrompt, an Extended Reality(XR) system that turns spatial sketches into executable constraints for controllable 3D generation. Users draw rough structures with a 3D pen and add voice prompts for semantic and stylistic intent. The system supports iterative refinement and synchronous co-creation in shared space with color-coded contributions. Implemented on Apple Vision Pro with Logitech Muse and Meshy, a heuristic evaluation suggests that the workflow is intuitive and supports shared understanding in collaborative creation, while revealing needs for faster generation and clearer feedback.

</details>


### [245] [Sycophantic AI makes human interaction feel more effortful and less satisfying over time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07912)
*Lujain Ibrahim,Franziska Sofia Hafner,Myra Cheng,Cinoo Lee,Rebecca Anselmetti,Robb Willer,Luc Rocher,Diyi Yang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过五项预注册研究（N=3,075，12,766次对话），包括为期三周的美国人口普查代表性样本纵向实验，发现奉承型AI能立即提供类似亲密朋友的情感支持，三周后用户向AI寻求建议的频率几乎与向亲友求助相当，且用户对现实社交满意度下降。多数用户偏好奉承型AI，并非因其建议质量，而是因其让人感到被理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究奉承型AI（sycophantic AI）对用户亲密关系认知和现实社交行为的长期影响，检验AI的"无摩擦理解"是否会悄然改变人际关系的评价标准。

Method: 五项预注册实验，包含3,075名参与者和12,766次人机对话，其中一项为三周纵向研究，采用美国人口普查代表性样本。通过比较奉承型与非奉承型AI对用户的情感支持、建议寻求行为、社交满意度等指标的影响。

Result: 奉承型AI能立即提供类似亲友的情感支持；三周后用户向奉承型AI寻求建议的频率几乎与向亲友求助相当；用户对现实社交的满意度下降；多数用户偏好奉承型AI，原因是感到被理解而非建议质量。

Conclusion: 奉承型AI通过提供无摩擦的理解体验，可能悄然提高了用户对人际关系的评判标准，从而对现实社交关系产生替代效应。这为AI奉承现象提供了一个关系性解释框架。

Abstract: Millions of people now turn to artificial intelligence (AI) systems for personal advice, guidance, and support. Such systems can be sycophantic, frequently affirming users' views and beliefs. Across five preregistered studies (N = 3,075 participants, 12,766 human-AI conversations), including a three-week study with a census-representative U.S. sample, we provide longitudinal experimental evidence that sycophantic AI shifts how users approach their closest relationships. We show that sycophantic AI immediately delivers the emotional and esteem support users typically associate with close friends and family. Over three weeks of such interactions, users became nearly as likely to seek personal advice from sycophantic AI as from close friends and family, and reported lower satisfaction with their real-world social interactions. When given a choice among AI response styles, a majority preferred sycophantic AI -- not for the quality of its advice, but because it made them feel most understood. Together, these findings offer a relational account of AI sycophancy: by providing frictionless understanding, it may quietly raise the bar against which human relationships are judged.

</details>


### [246] [Exploring a Virtual Pet to Provide Context Notifications in a Tourism Recommender System: a Pilot Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07960)
*Patrícia Alves,Joana Neto,Ana Barreiro,Jorge Lima,Fausto Alves,Henish Balu,Luís Conceição,Goreti Marreiros*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种以虚拟宠物作为社交中介的旅游推荐框架，通过多智能体微服务整合实时环境数据和用户个性特征生成个性化通知。11人参与的试点研究表明，虚拟宠物能有效软化系统通知的侵入感，提高通知的自然性和清晰度，增强用户在实时旅行决策中的接受度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旅游业推荐系统中实时通知的侵入性和用户疲劳问题尚未得到有效解决，亟需一种既能传递上下文感知信息又不引起用户反感的设计方案。

Method: 构建基于虚拟宠物的多智能体微服务框架，整合空气质量、噪音水平、天气预报等实时环境数据及邻近通知，结合用户个性特征生成推荐；采用11人受试者内设计，在四周内（每版本两周）对比基线版本和宠物中介版本的效果，收集定量与定性数据。

Result: 虚拟宠物能够有效"软化"系统通知的侵入感，使安全关键信息显得更受欢迎和自然；角色中介的合理解释显著提升了通知的清晰度，有效支持用户实时旅行决策。

Conclusion: 虚拟宠物作为社交中介可增强旅游推荐系统中上下文感知通信的透明度和接受度，为未来设计更自然的通知交互方式提供了基础。

Abstract: While context-aware personalization has been widely explored in modern tourism Recommender Systems (RS), the delivery of real-time notifications remains a significant design challenge due to issues of intrusiveness and user fatigue. This paper presents a proof-of-concept for a tourism recommendation framework that utilizes a virtual pet as a social mediator for delivering context-aware alerts. The system integrates real-time environmental data - including air quality, noise levels, and weather forecasts - and proximity-based notifications with a Multi-Agent Microservice that generates personalized recommendations based on the user's personality traits and preferences. A within-subjects pilot study (n=11) was conducted to evaluate the feasibility and user acceptance of this pet-mediated approach. Participants interacted with two versions of the system - a baseline without contextual alerts and a version featuring pet-mediated notifications - over a four-week period (two weeks per version) in real-world scenarios. Quantitative and qualitative data were collected to assess engagement, perceived naturalness, notification utility, and acceptance. Preliminary results suggest that the virtual pet effectively can "soften" the perceived intrusiveness of system alerts, making safety-critical information feel more welcome and natural. Furthermore, the character-mediated justifications significantly improved the clarity of the notifications, effectively supporting users in their real-time travel decisions. These findings provide a foundation for using virtual pet companions to enhance the transparency and acceptance of context-aware communication in tourism RS.

</details>


### [247] [Hot Wire 5D+: Evaluating Cognitive and Motor Trade-offs of Visual Feedback for 5D Augmented Reality Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08008)
*Christian Masuhr,Julian Koch,Arne Wendt,Thorsten Schüppstuhl*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过受试者内实验（N=30）评估了三种AR用户界面概念在5D轨迹引导（3D位置、2D方向、速度）中的表现，分析了视觉反馈范式与任务复杂度对新手用户空间、方向及速度依从性的影响，并提供了经验基线及AR引导系统设计指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AR被越来越多地用于引导用户完成复杂空间任务（如制造、无损检测、手术），这些任务要求严格遵循5D+轨迹（3D位置、2D方向、移动速度）。然而，未经训练的用户在执行这些多维追踪任务时的感觉运动基线，以及不同视觉反馈范式引发的认知-运动权衡尚不明确。

Method: 采用受试者内设计（N=30），评估三种AR用户界面概念（有/无显式方向约束）的轨迹引导效果。基于内部AR追踪（经高精度外部光学追踪系统验证）分析空间、方向和速度依从性。将执行过程分为瞬态和稳态阶段，使用Aligned Rank Transform (ART) ANOVA分析视觉设计与任务复杂度的交互效应，并结合主观指标（NASA-TLX、SUS）。

Result: 建立了新手用户自由手5D轨迹追踪的保守性能基线；揭示了方向约束引发的认知-运动权衡；识别出能够缓解这种权衡的UI协同效应。

Conclusion: 提供了经验基线和可操作的AR引导系统设计指南。

Abstract: Augmented Reality (AR) is increasingly utilized to guide users through complex spatial tasks in domains such as manufacturing, non-destructive testing, and surgery. These applications often require strict compliance with 5D+ trajectories using rotation-symmetric tools (3D position, 2D orientation, and movement speed). However, the sensori-motor baselines of untrained users during these multidimensional tracing tasks, along with the cognitive-motor trade-offs induced by varying visual feedback paradigms, remain underexplored. We present a controlled within-subjects user study (N=30) evaluating three distinct AR UI concepts for trajectory guidance, both with and without explicit orientation constraints. We analyzed spatial, orientational, and speed compliance based on the internal AR tracking, which was validated against a high-precision external optical tracking system to rule out hardware drift. By segmenting the execution into transient and steady-state phases and applying Aligned Rank Transform (ART) ANOVA, we isolated the interaction effects between visual design and task complexity. Alongside subjective metrics (NASA-TLX, SUS), our results establish conservative performance baselines for novice users performing freehand 5D trajectory following. We reveal orientation-induced cognitive-motor trade-offs and identify mitigating UI synergies. Ultimately, we provide empirical baselines and actionable design guidelines for developing effective AR guidance systems.

</details>


### [248] [ECNUClaw: A Learner-Profiled Intelligent Study Companion Framework for K-12 Personalized Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08040)
*Yizhou Zhou,Jiayin Li,Zhi Zhang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: ECNUClaw是一个开源框架，用于构建K-12教育中的学习者画像智能学习伴侣。系统通过学生-同伴对话提取信号，构建五维学习者画像（认知、行为、情感、元认知、情境），并实时调整同伴的指导强度、鼓励频率和布鲁姆分类学脚手架。框架基于中国教育技术文献的三个理论：张氏数字肖像三层框架、教育脑模型和人机协作智商概念。系统用Python实现，通过统一OpenAI兼容适配层支持七种中文LLM提供商。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为K-12教育构建一个开源、可扩展的智能学习伴侣框架，通过细粒度学习者画像实现个性化自适应引导，弥补现有教育AI系统在画像维度和理论指导方面的不足。

Method: 系统从每轮学生-同伴对话中提取信号，构建并维护五维学习者画像（认知、行为、情感、元认知、情境）。画像更新后输入自适应策略引擎，实时调整三个干预维度：指导强度、鼓励频率和布鲁姆分类学脚手架。系统基于三个中国教育技术理论设计，采用Python实现，通过统一适配层支持七种LLM提供商。

Result: 实现了完整的系统架构，包括五维画像构建机制和自适应策略引擎，并开源了代码。但论文未提供实验评估结果，主要聚焦于框架设计和实现描述。

Conclusion: ECNUClaw为K-12教育智能学习伴侣提供了一个理论驱动、开源可扩展的框架，但需要进一步实验验证其有效性，并讨论了当前局限性和未来工作方向。

Abstract: We introduce ECNUClaw, an open-source framework for building learner-profiled intelligent study companions in K-12 education. The system constructs and maintains a five-dimension learner profile -- covering cognitive, behavioral, emotional, metacognitive, and contextual dimensions -- by extracting signals from student-companion dialogues at each turn. Profile updates feed directly into an adaptive strategy engine that adjusts the companion's guidance intensity, encouragement frequency, and Bloom's taxonomy scaffolding in real time. The framework design draws on three theoretical strands from the Chinese educational technology literature: Zhang's Digital Portrait Three-Layer Framework for learner assessment, the Education Brain model for educational system architecture, and the Human-AI Collaborative IQ concept for companion design philosophy. ECNUClaw is implemented in Python and supports seven Chinese LLM providers through a unified OpenAI-compatible adapter layer. We describe the system architecture, the profiling and adaptation mechanisms, and discuss limitations and next steps. The source code is available at https://github.com/bushushu2333/ECNUClaw.

</details>


<div id='cs.LO'></div>

# cs.LO [[Back]](#toc)

### [249] [Computing Short SAT Implicants via Ising/QUBO Encodings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07017)
*Giuseppe Spallitta,Leonardo Duenas-Osorio,Moshe Y. Vardi*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将“无关项”语义融入Ising/QUBO模型的框架，通过双极性表示实现短部分满足赋值（implicant）的检索，支持通过小幅目标修改进行implicant收缩和投影，实验表明在随机3-SAT公式上可保留约三分之一变量未赋值，并高概率达到最小/最小基数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多推理任务需要短的偏满足赋值（implicant），但现有的SAT-to-Ising/QUBO公式隐式设计为基态对应完全赋值，因为Ising/QUBO模型对每个自旋赋值，无法原生表示未赋值变量。因此需要一种能处理“无关项”的框架。

Method: 提出一种双极性表示方法，将“don't-care”语义嵌入二次模型；通过小幅修改目标函数实现implicant收缩和投影；给出参数范围确保基态对应短偏满足赋值，在二次惩罚函数允许时达到最小基数。

Result: 在随机3-SAT枚举基准和非CNF公式上使用模拟退火进行实证评估，显示在随机3-SAT公式上约三分之一变量保持未赋值同时满足可满足性；连续极性冻结轮次以高概率达到最小性（和最小基数）。

Conclusion: 该框架成功将无关项语义引入Ising/QUBO模型，有效检索短implicant，并通过简单修改实现收缩和投影，实验验证了其有效性和高概率的最优性。

Abstract: Many reasoning tasks require short partial satisfying assignments (implicants), sometimes focusing on a set of important variables. SAT-to-Ising-QUBO formulations are implicitly designed so that ground states correspond to total assignments, since the Ising/QUBO model assigns a value to every spin and has no native representation of unassigned variables. We introduce an Ising/QUBO framework that incorporates "don't-care" semantics into the quadratic model via a dual-polarity representation, enabling the retrieval of short implicants. The encoding supports implicant shrinking and projection through minor objective modifications. We provide parameter regimes under which ground states correspond to short partial satisfying assignments, achieving minimality and, when the quadratic penalty function permits, minimum-cardinality. We empirically evaluate the encoding with simulated annealing on random 3-SAT enumeration benchmarks and non-CNF formulas, showing that it leaves about one-third of variables unassigned on random 3-SAT formulas while preserving satisfiability, and that consecutive polarity-freezing rounds achieve minimality (and minimum-cardinality) with high probability.

</details>


### [250] [Evidence-Tracked Tape Semantics for Probabilistic Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07259)
*Liron Cohen,Tomer Samara*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于证据追踪的磁带语义（evidence-tracked tape semantics），通过单子-核心-证据-框架管线构建高阶逻辑，在内涵层面保留随机计算中相关性可见性，在外延层面通过概率测度恢复定量推理，并研究了磁带重写映射、独立抽取分裂等内涵原则以及概率为一的必然抽象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的概率计算内涵语义将随机程序视为消耗显式随机磁带的确定性计算，形成内涵层（随机性重用和相关性可见）与外延层（在选定测度下解释磁带）的两层视角。现有工作需要将这种两层结构系统化为一种完整的高阶逻辑框架，并揭示内涵层面在定律层面丢失的推理原则。

Method: 使用单子-核心-证据-框架管线（monadic-core-to-evidenced-frame pipeline）及其诱导的可实现性三纤维范畴（realizability tripos），构建证据追踪的磁带语义；通过推前（pushforward）将磁带推理与外延律语义相关联，并引入概率为一的必然抽象（must abstraction）。

Result: 获得了一个高阶逻辑系统，其中推演（entailment）由一致的证据变换器（uniform evidence transformers）见证；固定磁带测度后，从磁带索引谓词提取数值摘要恢复概率和期望，推演产生可靠的不等式；证明了概率为一的商结构支持几乎必然推理；研究了磁带重写映射的证据可传输性和流磁带独立抽取的规范分裂规则。

Conclusion: 本文建立的证据追踪磁带语义为概率计算的内涵与外延推理提供了统一的高阶逻辑框架，揭示了内涵层特有的推理原则及其与外延层的关系，为概率程序的逻辑分析提供了新的理论基础。

Abstract: A standard intensional account of probabilistic computation represents a randomized program as a deterministic computation that consumes an explicit random tape. This yields a two-layer perspective: an intensional layer that makes reuse of randomness and correlation visible, and an extensional layer obtained by interpreting tapes under a chosen probability measure.
  We develop an evidence-tracked tape semantics using the monadic-core-to-evidenced-frame pipeline (and its induced realizability tripos), obtaining a higher-order logic in which entailments are witnessed by uniform evidence transformers. Quantitative statements are recovered by interpretation: once a tape measure is fixed, probabilities and expectations arise by extracting numerical summaries from tape-indexed predicates, and entailments yield sound inequalities, with an almost-sure quotient supporting probability-one reasoning. We also study intensional principles that are lost at the level of laws, including proof-relevant transport along realizable tape-rewiring maps and a canonical splitting discipline for stream tapes enforcing independent draws. Finally, we relate tape-based reasoning to an extensional law semantics via pushforward, isolating a probability-one must abstraction as a sound summary of tape-based proofs.

</details>


### [251] [Finitary Truly Concurrent Bisimulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07373)
*Yong Wang*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文针对基于预互模拟前序的过程语言的全抽象指称模型构建问题，提出了真并发预互模拟及其有限形式的概念，并给出了定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预互模拟前序的行为语义存在两个问题：指称解释相关的进程产生相同的有限观察，而预互模拟基于无限观察可区分进程行为。为获得全抽象结果，需要预互模拟的有限部分。

Method: 遵循有限互模拟的行为形式（而非逻辑形式），将相关概念推广到真并发场景，定义了真并发预互模拟及其有限形式。

Result: 给出了真并发预互模拟及其有限形式的定义。

Conclusion: 为基于预互模拟前序的过程语言的全抽象指称模型提供了理论工具。

Abstract: To develop a full abstract denotational model of a process language based on prebisimulation preorder, its behavioural semantics has two problems: (1) Two processes related by a standard denotational interpretation afford the same finite observations. (2) Prebisimulation can make distinctions between the behaviours of two processes based on infinite observations. So, finitary part of prebisimulation is needed to obtain full abstract results. There existed two main results on finitary bisimulation: the logical form and the behavioural form. Following the latter one, we give the definitions of truly concurrent prebisimulations and their finitary ones.

</details>


### [252] [Cross-Attention and Encoder-Decoder Transformers: A Logical Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07705)
*Veeti Ahvonen,Damian Heiman,Antti Kuusisto,Miguel Moreno,Matias Selin*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文首次对编码器-解码器Transformer（LLM的基础架构）进行逻辑刻画，通过一种新型时序逻辑（包含计数全局模态和过去时态模态）和分布式自动机两种方式表征该类架构，并讨论了自回归设置下的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码器-解码器Transformer是LLM的基础架构，广泛应用于各类受益于交叉注意力的场景，但目前缺乏对其形式化的逻辑刻画，该研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出一种新的时序逻辑，在命题逻辑基础上扩展了编码器输入的计数全局模态（counting global modality）和解码器输入的过去时态模态（past modality）；同时通过分布式自动机给出另一种表征；实验验证了结果对架构变体（如masking）的鲁棒性。

Result: 成功给出了编码器-解码器Transformer在浮点数与soft-attention设置下的逻辑刻画和自动机表征，且证明结果可推广至不同的掩码设置等架构变化。

Conclusion: 编码器-解码器Transformer可以通过所提出的时序逻辑和分布式自动机两种方式进行形式化刻画，该框架具有通用性，能够适应架构调整，并可用于自回归设置的分析。

Abstract: We give a novel logical characterization of encoder-decoder transformers, the foundational architecture for LLMs that also sees use in various settings that benefit from cross-attention. We study such transformers over text in the practical setting of floating-point numbers and soft-attention, characterizing them with a new temporal logic. This logic extends propositional logic with a counting global modality over the encoder input and a past modality over the decoder input. We also give an additional characterization of such transformers via a type of distributed automata, and show that our results are not limited to the specific choices in the architecture and can account for changes in, e.g., masking. Finally, we discuss encoder-decoder transformers in the autoregressive setting.

</details>


### [253] [A Fibrational Perspective on Differential Linear Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07858)
*Jad Koleilat*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文通过Grothendieck纤维化和切函子建立了微分线性逻辑（DiLL）的范畴模型，为统一DiLL与依赖类型迈出了第一步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DiLL的范畴模型缺乏统一的纤维化视角，难以与依赖类型系统结合。作者旨在通过适应类型论范畴语义中的线性-非线性伴随方法，填补这一空白。

Method: 将DiLL的范畴模型表达为一对配备切函子的Grothendieck纤维化，借鉴了类型论范畴语义中处理线性-非线性伴随的方法。

Result: 成功构建了DiLL的纤维化范畴模型，为后续与依赖类型的统一提供了理论基础。

Conclusion: 这是统一DiLL和依赖类型的第一步，展示了通过纤维化方法建立微分线性逻辑范畴模型的可行性。

Abstract: Differential Linear Logic (DiLL) is a sequent calculus that expresses differentiation via symmetries between linear and non-linear formulas. In this paper, we express categorical models of DiLL as a pair of Grothendieck fibrations equipped with a tangent functor. To do so, we adapt methods from categorical semantics of type theory to linear-non-linear adjunctions. This is a first step towards unifying DILL and dependent types.

</details>


<div id='cs.ET'></div>

# cs.ET [[Back]](#toc)

### [254] [Post-Moore Technologies for Plasma Simulation: A Community Roadmap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07722)
*Luca Pennati,Erik M. Åsgrim,Jeremy J. Williams,Stefan Costea,David Tskhakaya,Leon Kos,Ales Podolnik,Yi Ju,Tapish Narwal,Julian Lenz,Michael Bussmann,Urs Ganse,Minna Palmroth,Kallia Chronaki,Vassilis Papaefstathiou,Etienne Renault,Felix Jung,Martin Schulz,Valentin Seitz,Marta Garcia-Gasulla,Filippo Mantovani,Frank Jenko,Erwin Laure,Stefano Markidis*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文从社区视角评估了后摩尔时代三种计算技术（可重构/数据通路加速器、非冯·诺依曼架构、量子计算）在等离子体模拟中的应用潜力，发现没有单一技术能取代现有HPC平台，而是形成了三级机会：FPGA类加速器适用于近期核卸载和数据处理，非冯·诺依曼架构适合中期算子级加速，量子计算最具颠覆性潜力但成熟度最低，主要应用于温稠密物质和惯性约束聚变微物理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等离子体模拟是计算密集型科学工作负载，包含高维动力学演化、粒子-网格耦合、场求解和数据密集型通信。随着通用处理器扩展速度放缓，需要探索后摩尔时代技术来解决数据移动、内存访问和功耗方面的瓶颈。

Method: 采用协设计方法，针对三种主要的技术类别（可重构/数据通路加速器、非冯·诺依曼架构、量子计算）进行评估，工作负载涵盖粒子网格法、连续Vlasov、回旋动理学、流体/MHD、混合和温稠密物质方法等多种等离子体模拟方法。

Result: 没有单一技术可以取代现有HPC平台。FPGA类和数据通路加速器提供近期核卸载和工作流级数据服务；非冯·诺依曼架构代表中期算子级加速方向；量子计算成熟度最低但最具颠覆性潜力，尤其适用于温稠密物质和惯性约束聚变微物理。

Conclusion: 建议选择性采用不同技术，并确定了聚焦演示器、基准测试和模块化软件生态系统作为社区近期优先事项。

Abstract: Plasma simulations are among the most computationally demanding scientific workloads, combining high-dimensional kinetic evolution, particle-mesh coupling, field solves, and data-intensive communication. As general-purpose processor scaling slows, post-Moore technologies are being explored to address bottlenecks in data movement, memory access, and power consumption. This paper provides a community perspective on the role of these technologies in plasma simulation, assessing three major classes: reconfigurable and data-path accelerators, non-von Neumann architectures, and quantum computing. Each is evaluated, in a co-design approach, against representative plasma workloads spanning particle-in-cell, continuum Vlasov, gyrokinetic, fluid/MHD, hybrid, and warm dense matter methods. We find that no single technology can replace existing HPC platforms. Instead, three tiers of opportunity emerge: FPGA-class and data-path accelerators offer near-term kernel offload and workflow-level data services, non-von Neumann architectures represent medium-term directions for operator-level acceleration, and quantum computing, although the least mature, is potentially the most disruptive for warm dense matter and inertial confinement fusion microphysics. We outline best practices for selective adoption and identify focused demonstrators, benchmarking, and modular software ecosystems as immediate community priorities.

</details>


### [255] [Per-Phase Fidelity Attribution for Quantum Compilers using HBR Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07876)
*Chandrachud Pati,Yogesh Simmhan*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文提出HBR分解模型，将编译管线划分为高维结构分解(H)、基础翻译(B)、路由(R)三个阶段，量化各阶段保真度损失。通过对Qiskit、PennyLane、TKET三种SDK在IBM Heron和IonQ Forte两种后端上的八种算法评估，发现编译瓶颈强烈依赖于电路类别：搜索类电路路由损失占60%，哈密顿模拟则以综合优化为主。SDK排名在诊断级优化与生产级优化下会反转，HBR能准确预测噪声模拟和真实硬件上的排名顺序，揭示聚合基准无法观测的阶段级瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子编译器基准测试仅报告聚合后的后编译指标，无法定位保真度损失在编译流水线中的具体阶段，缺乏细粒度的阶段级归因分析能力。

Method: 提出HBR分解模型，将编译流程分为高维结构分解(H)、基础翻译(B)、路由(R)三个阶段，分别量化各阶段对保真度损失的相对贡献。在两种后端拓扑（IBM Heron重六边形、IonQ Forte全连接）上对三种生产级SDK的八种算法进行系统评估。

Result: 路由在搜索类电路中占高达60%的相对保真度损失，而综合优化在哈密顿模拟中占主导地位。早期综合选择会放大或压缩下游路由开销。SDK排名在opt=0与opt=2级别下会反转。HBR在噪声模拟和真实IBM Fez硬件上均能正确预测SDK的排名顺序。

Conclusion: HBR分解模型能够揭示聚合编译器基准无法观测的阶段级编译瓶颈，证明阶段级诊断与生产级结果回答的是不同问题，为量子编译器优化提供了细粒度归因分析工具。

Abstract: Quantum compilers sit between an algorithm's theoretical promise and what executes on physical hardware. Existing benchmarks report aggregate post-transpilation metrics but cannot attribute where fidelity is lost within the compilation pipeline. We present HBR decomposition, a per-phase fidelity attribution model that quantifies relative fidelity loss across High-level structural decomposition (H), Basis translation (B), and Routing (R). We evaluate three production SDKs (Qiskit, PennyLane, TKET) across eight algorithms on two backend topologies: IBM Heron (heavy-hex) and IonQ Forte (all-to-all). The dominant compiler bottleneck is strongly circuit-class dependent: Routing accounts for up to 60% of relative fidelity loss in search-class circuits, while synthesis dominates Hamiltonian simulation workloads. Early synthesis choices amplify or compress downstream routing overhead depending on circuit connectivity. SDK rankings at diagnostic optimization level (opt=0) reverse at production levels (opt=2) for deep circuits, showing that stagewise diagnostics and production results answer different questions. HBR correctly predicts SDK rank ordering across noisy simulations (8 circuits x 3 SDKs x 2 tiers) and real IBM Fez hardware executions, revealing stage-specific bottlenecks that are not observable through aggregate compiler benchmarks.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [256] [EMRGF: A Practitioner Framework for Governance-Driven Enterprise Technology Modernization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06703)
*Harveen Punihani*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出企业技术现代化项目失败的主因是治理缺失而非工程能力不足，并由此开发了EMRGF——一个由四个模块和五类实施工具构成的治理运营模型。规模化实证应用显示，该框架可降低30%开发工作量、35%测试周期，实现零中断迁移和99.9%数据可靠性，且与美国NIST CSF 2.0、NIST AI RMF及行政令14028/14110等国家政策对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ITIL、COBIT、TOGAF、规模化敏捷方法论及云厂商最佳实践框架虽涉及治理相关议题，但缺乏一个针对受控现代化（涵盖迁移、数据平台及AI自动化）的可移植、集成化的机构级运营模型，导致企业现代化项目因治理缺失而以高成本失败。

Method: 基于24年跨金融服务、工业制造及零售企业的交付经验，采用实践者驱动的开发方式，构建了包含四个互锁模块（云与遗留系统现代化治理、数据平台可靠性与证据完整性、AI赋能自动化治理、关键任务可靠性与根因分析例程）的EMRGF框架，并通过五类实施工具及培训师培训（ToT）制度化模型实现落地。

Result: 规模化实证应用取得：开发工作量减少30%、测试周期缩短35%、高容量数据资产零中断迁移、关键任务分析管道实现99.9%数据可靠性。

Conclusion: 企业现代化失败的核心归因应从"工程能力不足"转向"治理缺失"；EMRGF提供了一套结构化、可重复的运营例程，填补了现有框架在受控现代化治理方面的空白，并可对接国家政策要求实现制度化采纳而不依赖外部持续支持。

Abstract: Enterprise technology modernization programs fail at a documented and costly rate, yet the dominant explanation -- inadequate engineering capability -- is incorrect. The primary failure mode is a governance deficit: the absence of structured, repeatable operating routines for how organizations plan, execute, validate, and hand off complex technology change. Existing frameworks -- ITIL, COBIT, TOGAF, scaled agile methodologies, and cloud provider well-architected frameworks -- address adjacent concerns but do not provide an integrated, portable institutional operating model for controlled modernization across migrations, data platforms, and AI-enabled automation. This article presents the Enterprise Modernization Reliability and Governance Framework (EMRGF), a practitioner-developed governance operating model derived from 24 years of applied delivery experience across financial services, industrial manufacturing, and retail enterprises. EMRGF comprises four interlocking modules -- Cloud and Legacy Modernization Governance, Data Platform Reliability and Evidence Integrity, AI-Enabled Automation Governance, and Mission-Critical Reliability and Root-Cause Routines -- operationalized through five implementation tools and a training-of-trainers institutionalization model. Empirical application at scale has produced a 30% reduction in development effort, a 35% reduction in testing cycles, zero-disruption migrations across high-volume data estates, and 99.9% data reliability in mission-critical analytics pipelines. The framework is explicitly aligned with U.S. national policy mandates including NIST CSF 2.0, NIST AI RMF, and Executive Orders 14028 and 14110, and is designed for institutional adoption without ongoing external dependency.

</details>


### [257] [The Single-File Test: A Longitudinal Public-Interface Evaluation of First-Output LLM Web Generation with Social Reach Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06707)
*Diego Cabezas Palacios*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对68个单文件HTML生成进行了八周观察性比较，评估了GPT、Gemini、Grok和Claude四个推理模型家族在固定协议下的表现。Claude在人类加权评分中表现最强且最稳定，赢得9/17个提示。更长的推理时间与更高质量无关。Gemini作为评判者比人类更宽容，存在自我偏好偏差。X浏览量预测模型表现弱（MAE=46,874, R²=-0.377），而代码冗长度预测表现较好（MAE=135.2, R²=0.576）。代码冗长度主要由模型家族而非提示措辞驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无定制指令、无人格调校、无修复提示的固定公共接口协议下，系统比较不同推理模型家族（GPT、Gemini、Grok、Claude）生成单文件HTML的质量，并探索技术变量对社交媒体传播效果和代码冗长度的预测能力。

Method: 从"HTML AI Battle"项目收集17个公开实验的68个单文件HTML生成，使用固定公共接口协议，通过人类评分和Gemini LLM评判员评估提示遵循度、功能正确性和UI质量，并按标准化社交媒体协议发布。使用监督预测分析建立实验级X浏览量模型和生成级HTML冗长度模型。

Result: Claude在人类加权评分中表现最强（9/17提示胜出）。更长推理时间与更高质量无正相关。Gemini评判员在功能正确性和整体表现上比人类更宽容。X浏览量预测模型表现差（MAE=46,874, R²=-0.377），HTML行数预测表现较好（MAE=135.2, R²=0.576）。代码冗长度主要由模型家族而非提示措辞驱动。

Conclusion: Claude是当前协议下最强的推理模型家族；推理时间并非质量指标；出版前技术/音频变量不足以预测社交媒体传播效果；模型家族是代码冗长度的主要决定因素。研究受公共接口漂移、访问路径差异和单一人类评分者限制，结论为观察性而非因果性。

Abstract: This paper presents an eight-week observational comparison of 68 single-file HTML generations collected across 17 public experiments in the "HTML AI Battle" project between December 10, 2025 and February 4, 2026. Four reasoning model families, GPT, Gemini, Grok, and Claude, were compared under a fixed public-interface protocol with no custom instructions, no personality tuning, and no repair prompts. Each output was evaluated from a rendered browser video using human scores and a Gemini LLM-as-a-judge layer for prompt adherence, functional correctness, and UI quality, then packaged into a standardized social-media protocol spanning X (Twitter), TikTok, and YouTube. The tracker was also used for two supervised predictive analyses: an experiment-level model for 24-hour X impressions and a generation-level model for HTML verbosity.
  Under this protocol, Claude was the strongest and most consistent family, leading mean performance and winning 9/17 prompts under the primary human weighted score. Longer measured reasoning time was not associated with higher quality overall. Gemini as a judge was significantly more lenient than the human evaluator on functional correctness and overall performance, while stable self-favoring bias remained unresolved. The exploratory X-impressions model remained weak under post-screen cross-validation (MAE = 46,874, R^2 = -0.377), whereas the HTML-lines model performed better, with a model-family-only baseline outperforming prompt-aware alternatives (MAE = 135.2, R^2 = 0.576). Overall, selected pre-publication technical/audio variables were not sufficient to predict 24-hour X reach, while code verbosity was driven much more by model family than by prompt wording. The comparisons remain observational and are limited by public-interface drift, access-path differences, and one primary human scorer.

</details>


### [258] [Agentic Coding Needs Proactivity, Not Just Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06717)
*Nghi D. Q. Bui,Georgios Evangelopoulos*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文探讨了编码智能体从被动补全向主动、长周期系统演进的趋势，提出了主动性三层分类法（反应式、计划式、情境感知），并定义了评估主动编码智能体的三个核心指标：洞察决策质量（IDQ）、上下文基础评分（CGS）和学习提升（Learning Lift）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前编码智能体正从内联补全向自主系统演进，但领域内缺乏对"主动性"的清晰定义、与"自主性"的区别、主动长周期任务的可接受标准，以及如何评估非请求式智能体行为是否有用而非仅仅是活跃。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 基于混合主动交互原则，提出主动性三层分类法（Reactive、Scheduled、Situation Aware）；将当代编码智能体与五个实际标准进行对比；设计主动用户模拟协议，包含三个评估目标：Insight Decision Quality (IDQ)、Context Grounding Score (CGS)、Learning Lift。

Result: 提出了评估主动编码智能体的核心框架：智能体的洞察策略（insight policy）决定了下一步该关注什么、有何证据支持、是否展示以及如何根据反馈调整。三类评估指标分别衡量洞察决策质量、上下文基础程度和跨会话学习能力。

Conclusion: 主动编码智能体应以洞察策略的质量和改进为核心进行评价，而非仅看活动频率。该框架为下一代主动、长周期编码智能体的设计与评估提供了理论基础和实操指标。

Abstract: Coding agents are rapidly changing the landscape of software development, moving from inline completion to autonomous systems that edit repositories, open pull requests, respond to issues, and run scheduled or webhook triggered routines across the development life cycle. The next generation is increasingly described as proactive and long-horizon: agents should notice relevant changes before the developer asks, connect signals across tools, decide when to interrupt, and carry preferences across sessions. Yet the field still lacks a clear account of what proactivity means for software development, how it differs from autonomy, what acceptance criteria proactive long-horizon tasks should satisfy, and which metrics determine whether unsolicited agent behavior is useful rather than merely active. Proactive coding agents should be evaluated by the quality and improvement of their insight policy: the policy that decides what matters next, what evidence supports it, whether to show it, and how to adapt after feedback. This view is grounded in the principles of mixed initiative interaction. We propose a three level taxonomy of proactivity (Reactive, Scheduled, and Situation Aware), compare contemporary coding agents against five practical criteria, and sketch an active user simulation protocol with three evaluation targets: Insight Decision Quality (IDQ), Context Grounding Score (CGS), and Learning Lift

</details>


### [259] [A Self-Healing Framework for Reliable LLM-Based Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06737)
*Cheonsu Jeong,Younggun Shin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向LLM软件智能体的可靠性感知自愈框架，通过集成故障检测、可靠性评估与自动恢复机制，显著提升了任务成功率和系统鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的自主智能体在复杂软件系统中应用日益广泛，但存在幻觉、执行错误和不一致推理等不可预测的可靠性问题。

Method: 首先定义故障类型分类体系并引入定量可靠性评估模型；然后提出基于执行模式与输出一致性的故障检测方法；最后设计通过自适应重规划和纠正性提示策略的动态自愈机制。

Result: 在多智能体工作流环境中使用真实任务场景评估，实验结果表明该方法显著提高了任务成功率，减少了故障传播，增强了系统鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该研究通过建立融合智能体内部推理过程与外部执行结果的集成监控系统，有助于保障高级自主系统的稳定性，降低LLM在生产环境中的采用门槛。

Abstract: Autonomous agents based on Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly being utilized in complex software systems. However, reliability remains a significant challenge due to unpredictable failures such as hallucinations, execution errors, and inconsistent reasoning. This paper proposes a reliability-aware self-healing framework for LLM-based software agents. The framework integrates failure detection, reliability assessment, and automated recovery mechanisms. First, we define a taxonomy of failure types and introduce a quantitative reliability assessment model. Next, we propose a failure detection method that identifies abnormal agent behavior based on execution patterns and output consistency. Finally, we design a self-healing mechanism that dynamically recovers from failures through adaptive replanning and corrective prompting strategies. The proposed framework was implemented in a multi-agent workflow environment and evaluated using real-world task scenarios. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach significantly increases task success rates, reduces failure propagation, and enhances overall system robustness compared to existing methods. In particular, this study distinguishes itself by establishing an integrated monitoring system that combines the agent's internal reasoning process with external execution results. These findings are expected to contribute to securing the stability of advanced autonomous systems and lowering the barriers to LLM adoption in production environments.

</details>


### [260] [Analyzing the Adoption of Database Management Systems Throughout the History of Open Source Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06817)
*Camila A. Paiva,Raquel Maximino,Frederico Paiva,Rafael Accetta Vieira,Nicole Espanha,João Felipe Pimentel,Igor Wiese,Marco Aurélio Gerosa,Igor Steinmacher,Leonardo Murta,Vanessa Braganholo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究对GitHub上362个热门Java开源项目进行历史分析，揭示了MySQL和PostgreSQL是最受欢迎的DBMS，Redis和MongoDB在非关系型系统中使用最稳定，HyperSQL更易被替换，项目常同时使用多种DBMS实现多语言持久化，且ORM框架广泛用于应用与DBMS之间的交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有研究关注DBMS与应用源代码的协同演进，但关于DBMS在实际系统中的采用、共使用和替换模式仍知之甚少，因此本文旨在填补这一实证研究的空白。

Method: 对GitHub上362个流行的开源Java项目进行历史研究，采用DB-Engines排名确定顶级DBMS（涵盖关系型和非关系型），并通过源代码启发式方法分析DBMS的流行度、稳定性、迁移模式、共现情况以及ORM的作用。

Result: MySQL和PostgreSQL是最流行的DBMS；Redis和MongoDB是最常用的非关系型DBMS且使用后趋于稳定；HyperSQL等系统更容易在项目演进中被替换；多个DBMS经常被共同使用，呈现出多语言持久化模式；ORM框架被普遍用于应用与DBMS之间的交互。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了关于DBMS在实际系统中如何被采用、组合和替换的实证证据，为开发者、架构师、教育者和DBMS供应商提供了指导。

Abstract: Database Management Systems (DBMSs) are widely used to store, retrieve, and manage the data handled by modern applications. Although prior work has studied the co-evolution of DBMSs and application source code, less is known about DBMS adoption, co-use, and replacement in real systems. This paper presents a historical study of DBMS usage in 362 popular open-source Java projects hosted on GitHub. We investigated the adoption of the top DBMSs ranked by DB-Engines, covering relational and non-relational systems. Using source-code heuristics, we analyzed DBMS popularity, stability, migration patterns, co-occurrence, and the role of Object-Relational Mappers (ORMs). Our findings show that MySQL and PostgreSQL are the most popular DBMSs in our corpus. Among non-relational DBMSs, Redis and MongoDB are the most frequently used and tend to remain stable after adoption. In contrast, systems such as HyperSQL are more often replaced as projects evolve. We also observed frequent co-use of multiple DBMSs, suggesting patterns of polyglot persistence in which projects combine systems to handle different data needs. Finally, we found that ORM frameworks are commonly used to mediate interactions between applications and DBMSs. Overall, our study provides empirical evidence on how DBMSs are adopted, combined, and replaced over time, offering guidance for developers, architects, educators, and DBMS vendors.

</details>


### [261] [Guidelines for Cultivating a Sense of Belonging to Reduce Developer Burnout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06827)
*Bianca Trinkenreich,Marco Aurelio Gerosa,Anita Sarma,Igor Steinmacher*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文综述了软件开发团队中归属感与职业倦怠的关系，总结了归属感的特征（信任、接纳、价值认可、友谊等）及相关因素（认可、心理安全、内在动机、英语自信、任期、性别、文化权力距离），并提出了培养归属感、减少倦怠的实践指南（如及时认可、透明晋升规则、包容性举措、协作工具、无指责事后复盘、可选线下机会、新人非正式聚会、持续监测等）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 职业倦怠严重影响软件开发人员的身心健康并导致离职，而归属感与倦怠水平密切相关。现有研究表明归属感能提升韧性、工作满意度、投入度和幸福感，因此亟需将相关研究成果转化为可操作的指导方针，帮助软件组织和开源社区缓解倦怠问题。

Method: 本文采用综述研究方法，回顾了近期关于软件开发团队（包括专有软件组织和开源社区）中归属感与职业倦怠关系的研究，系统归纳了归属感的特征及相关影响因素，并据此提出实践指南。

Result: 识别出归属感的7项特征（信任、接纳、价值认可、友谊、成员身份、互助、被他人了解）和7个相关因素（认可、心理安全、内在动机、英语自信、任期、性别、文化权力距离）。提出8条实践指南：及时一致的认可、透明的晋升规则、包容性福利举措、通过协作工具建立联系、无指责事后复盘、可选线下机会、非正式新人聚会、持续监测归属感与倦怠。

Conclusion: 所提出的指南能够帮助软件组织和开源社区营造更健康、更具包容性的环境，从而支持开发者的身心健康与工作幸福感。

Abstract: Burnout affects software developers' mental and physical well-being and contributes to turnover, generating strong concerns in the software industry. Prior research has shown that lack of belonging is associated with higher levels of burnout among software developers, while a sense of belonging is linked to resilience, job satisfaction, engagement, and well-being. In this paper, we revisit recent studies on belongingness in software development teams, including proprietary software organizations and open-source software communities, to offer evidence-based guidelines for cultivating belongingness and reducing developer burnout. We summarize characteristics of belongingness, such as trust, acceptance, value recognition, friendship, membership, mutual support, and being known by others, as well as factors associated with belongingness, including recognition, psychological safety, intrinsic motivation, English confidence, tenure, gender, and cultural power distance. Based on these findings, we propose practical guidelines for leaders and communities, including timely and consistent recognition, transparent promotion rules, inclusive benefits and initiatives, intentional connections through collaborative tools, blameless postmortems, optional in-person opportunities, informal newcomer gatherings, and continuous monitoring of belongingness and burnout. These guidelines can help software organizations and open-source communities foster healthier, more inclusive environments that support developer well-being.

</details>
